### **Introduction**

**The Darkened Sea** is EverQuest's 21st expansion. It was announced on August 14, 2014, and launched on Tuesday, October 28, 2014 (for All Access members) and Tuesday, November 11, 2014 (for Free-To-Play members).

- For the original announcement, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34894) (August 14, 2014 - SOE Live).
- For an early preview of the expansion, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=34918) (August 20, 2014).
- For a full preview of the expansion, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35284) (October 24, 2014).
- For the patch notes as the expansion launched, see [this news story](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35295) (October 28, 2014).

As discussed in [this letter from the producer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35075), there will be no traditional pre-orders for this expansion. Instead, All Access members will have the option to buy the expansion on October 28 (its "early release" date). Two weeks after this, everyone (Free-To-Play members included) will be able to buy it.

* * *

### **Content & Features**

- Level cap increase to Level 105
- New spells
- New AAs
- Eight new zones

  - SOE Zone Preview: [Tempest Temple](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35183)
  - SOE Zone Preview: [Caverns of Endless Song](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35215)
  - SOE Zone Preview: [Degmar, the Lost Castle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35258)
  - SOE Zone Preview: [Combine Dredge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35285)
  - SOE Zone Preview: [Thuliasaur Island](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35313)
  - SOE Zone Preview: [Arx Mentis](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=35344)

- Mount keyring

  - You can now use separate mounts (one for stats; one for visual appearance) at the same time. The keyring can hold up to 10 mounts (with option to buy more slots), and you can select any mount on your keyring to lock in its stats to be used regardless of what mount you're visually using.

* * *

### **What's Going On**

Lanys T\`Vyl and Firiona Vie are at odds, and Katta Castrum's emperor is in trouble... The Buried Sea is the backdrop as its storylines and characters will be revisited.

Firiona Vie has been travelling the world on a mission from the goddess Tunare to bring the realm back into balance. Her latest efforts have brought her to the Buried Sea, seeking to heal the ruler of the Combine Empire, Tsaph Katta.

Now itâ€™s time for you to travel to the domed city of Katta Castrum and find Firiona! After Firiona came here with her allies to heal Tsaph, the city was attacked by Lanys T\`vyl and a corrupt splinter faction of the Combine. In the chaos, Tsaph was kidnapped. Now, he must be rescued and Lanys must be stopped.

Main Lore article: ["The Darkened Sea" Lore](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:The_Darkened_Sea_Lore)

* * *

### **Zones**

Eight zones are included with this expansion, all of which will have full mob populations in their static versions:

- Tier 1 (open zones):

  - [Tempest Temple](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1015)
  - [Katta Castrum: Deluge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1016)

- Tier 2:

  - [Brother Island](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1017) (open zone)
  - [Caverns of Endless Song](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1018) (locked behind progression)

- Tier 3 (all locked behind progression; opened one at a time):

  - [Degmar, the Lost Castle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1019)
  - [Thuliasaur Island](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1020)
  - [Combine Dredge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1021)
  - [Arx Mentis](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1022)

- Check out [this link](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zcont=The+Darkened+Sea) for a list of zones in The Darkened Sea expansion.
- A screenshot of the in-game map of zone connections can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/gallery/ingamemaps/tds.jpg).

* * *

### **Getting There**

- The entry zone here is Tempest Temple:

  - Entered through Monkey Rock in the Buried Sea.
  - Druid and Wizard portal spells have drop-off points here.

- The Guild Hall portal can be set to Katta Castrum: Deluge.

- If you bought/buy the Collector's Edition of the expansion, you can claim a [Brother Island Parrot Whistle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/item.html?item=122702) with a right-click port to Brother Island.

* * *

### **Progression**

Progression through The Darkened Sea is strictly linear with a storyline that spans 24 quests through all eight zones. Tiers 2 & 3 zones are unlocked as you make your way through the expansion storyline.

- Main Progression Article: [Progression Through The Darkened Sea](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_The_Darkened_Sea)
- Progression Overview: [TDS Progression Questing](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7701)

* * *

### **Heroic Adventures**

There are six heroic adventures in this expansion:

- [Storm of Sorts](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7730) in Tempest Temple (no pre-requisites)
- [Seductive Subterfuge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7745) in the Caverns of Endless Song (no pre-requisites)
- [Castle Relic](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7757) in Degmar, the Lost Castle (some pre-requisites)
- [A Fateful Arrival](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7758) in Thuliasaur Island (no pre-requisites)
- [Kedge Counterblow](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7799) in Combine Dredge (no pre-requisites)
- [Shake the Citadel](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7809) in Arx Mentis (some prequisites)

* * *

### **Raids**

There are five primary raids and two "one-off" raids, all of which occur in Arx Mentis. ("One-Off" just means you only get one attempt at it: The lockout timer occurs upon engage rather than upon winning the event.)

Main raiding article: [Raiding in The Darkened Sea](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Raiding_in_The_Darkened_Sea)

### **Armor**

In the group game, there are three Tiers of armor. In the raid game, there are two Tiers.

Main armor article: [The Darkened Sea Armor Guide](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:The_Darkened_Sea_Armor_Guide)

Each tier has an adventure merchant from whom you can buy gear using currency (Pieces of Eight) earned from heroic adventures:

- Group:

  - Tier 1: [Yllethil Starsun](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47687) ( [**click for map image**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-yllethil_starsun.jpg)) in Tempest Temple
  - Tier 2: [Cha\`Nya Athendi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47688) ( [**click for map image**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/endlesscaverns-chanya_athendi.jpg)) in the Caverns of Endless Song
  - Tier 3: [Agrimensor Crescentia](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47689) ( [**click for map image**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/degmar-agrimensor_crescentia.jpg)) in Degmar, the Lost Castle

- Raid:

  - See: [Researcher Orbiana](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47690) ( [**click for map image**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-researcher_orbiana.jpg)) in Katta Castrum: Deluge (only a single tier of gear)

For the new set of Type 3 augmentations, see [Naissa Gemmarii](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47691) ( [**click for map image**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-naissa_gemmarii.jpg)) in Katta Castrum: Deluge.

There is also new Mercenary gear available, bought from [a merchant](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47692) on the zone-in level of Arx Mentis.

* * *

### **AAs**

All AAs can be freely trained. There are no locked AAs.

## Overview

There are two open zones and six locked zones in The Darkened Sea.

Progression is strictly linear, with a storyline ( [quest overview](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7701)) that begins in Tier 1 and ends in the final zone. You must complete all partisan tasks in the order listed here. Missions, if you have someone else to request them, can be completed any time, but to request them, you'll need to have completed all prior progression tasks.

Basically, to access a given zone, you need to have completed all bolded tasks listed before it.

Requirements for requesting mercenary tasks and heroic adventures vary by zone. Some have pre-requisites; some don't.

- Tier 1:

  - [Tempest Temple](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1015)
  - [Katta Castrum: Deluge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1016)

- Tier 2:

  - [Brother Island](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1017)
  - [Caverns of Endless Song](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1018)

- Tier 3:

  - [Degmar, the Lost Castle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1019)
  - [Thuliasaur Island](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1020)
  - [Combine Dredge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1021)
  - [Arx Mentis](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=1022)

* * *

## List of Tasks

Listed here are all tasks and group missions.

- **Bolded** tasks represent the expansion storyline and are meant to be completed in the order they appear here.
- Unbolded tasks are stand-alone, generally (but not always) with no pre-requisites other than a minimum level of 90-95.

### Tier 1

#### Tempest Temple

- Partisan Tasks

  - **[A Rising Storm](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7702) \- solo task from [Mate Rominald](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47702) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-mate_rominald.jpg); off the eastern coast of the island)**
  - **[Gathering Clouds](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7703) \- solo task from [Krinoch Jal](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47703) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-krinoch_jal.jpg); on the floating island in the southeast part of the zone)**

- Mission

  - **[Clearer Skies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7704) \- group mission from [Ognit Eznertob](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47704) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-ognit_eznertob); eastern coast camp)**

- Mercenary Tasks

  - [Of Pirates and Men](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7726) \- solo task from [Gregor Olmstead](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47705) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-gregor_olmstead.jpg); eastern coast area)
  - [Coral Crushers](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7727) \- solo task from [Jessup Stormwind](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47706) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-jessup_stormwind); on the floating island in the southeast part of the zone)
  - [So Many Serpents](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7728) \- solo task from [Jessup Stormwind](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47706)
  - [Bigger Fish](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7729) \- shared task from [Jessup Stormwind](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47706)
  - [Storm of Sorts](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7730) \- heroic adventure from [Petran Searake](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47707) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/tempesttemple-petran_searake.jpg); next to the dock on the eastern coast)

#### Katta Castrum: Deluge

- Partisan Tasks

  - **[The Absent Scholar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7707) \- solo task from [Firiona Vie](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47708) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-firiona_vie.jpg); zone-in area)**

- Mission

  - **[The Edge of Balance](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7706) \- group mission from [Firiona Vie](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47708)**

- Mercenary Tasks

  - **[The Pellarus Satum](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7705) \- solo task from [Head Mage Antius](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47710) ( [map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-head_mage_antius.jpg); zone-in area)**
  - [Elemental Rage](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7732) \- solo task from [Tactician Sodut](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47709) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-tactician_sodut.jpg); down the ramp from the temple)
  - [Bury the Head](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7733) \- solo task from [Tactician Sodut](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47709)
  - [Library Aquatic](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7731) \- solo task from [Alchemist Hesia](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47711) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/kattacastrumb-alchemist_hesia.jpg); zone-in area)

### Tier 2

#### Brother Island

- Partisan Tasks

  - [Where It Hurts](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7805) \- solo task from [Serphus Arlis](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47749)
  - [An Immaterial World](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7806) \- solo task from [Oreda Safli](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47750) ( [**map**](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/spawns/arxmentis-oreda_safli.jpg); the lower of the two very narrow halls in the western part of the second floor)
  - [Civilized Society](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=7807) \- solo task from [Oreda Safli](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=47750)

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_Progression through The Darkened Sea is strictly linear and involves 23 (+2) quests. Zones open up as you progress through the expansion's storyline._
**Tier 1**
Tempest Temple
_This is an open zone._
Katta Castrum: Deluge
_This is an open zone._

---

**Tier 2**
Brother Island
_As of December 11, 2014, this is an open zone._
Caverns of Endless Song
_Access to the Caverns of Endless Song is granted upon:_
\- Completion of " A Rising Storm [quest=7702]" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of " Gathering Clouds [quest=7703]" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of " Clearer Skies [quest=7704]" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of " The Pellarus Satum [quest=7705]" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of " The Edge of Balance [quest=7706]" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of " The Abscent Scholar [quest=7707]" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)

---

**Tier 3**
Degmar, the Lost Castle
_Access to Degmar is granted upon:_
\- Completion of " Just Recompense [quest=7708]" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of " The Cover of Night [quest=7709]" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of " Loyal is the Heart [quest=7710]" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of " Leave No Brother Behind [quest=7711]" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of " Two Sides of the Shield [quest=7712]" (Caverns of Endless Song)
Thuliasaur Island
_Access to Thuliasaur Island is granted upon:_
\- Completion of " The Sacred Profaned [quest=7713]" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of " Crying for the Dead [quest=7714]" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of " Burden of the Past [quest=7715]" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Note: In order to request "Crying for the Dead" (solo task), you must have completed " Castle Relic [quest=7757]" (the heroic adventure for Degmar). In order to get credit for the heroic adventure, you must be in the zone at the time of its completion. In order to zone into the instance (or request it), you must be of apprehensive+ faction with Degmar's Commoners (increased by completing " Castle Raid [quest=7747]" and " Castle Crasher [quest=7748]"). None of these tasks are technically counted as part of progression._
Combine Dredge
_Access to Combine Dredge is granted upon:_
\- Completion of " An Endless Cycle [quest=7716]" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of " To Remake Eden [quest=7717]" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of " Fit for the New World [quest=7718]" (Thuliasaur Island)
Arx Mentis
_Access to Arx Mentis is granted upon:_
\- Completion of " A Fickle Wind Blows [quest=7719]" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of " What Hate Begets [quest=7720]" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of " Deep Lies the Snow [quest=7721]" (Combine Dredge)

---

Raiding
_To request the Arx Mentis raid, you must have:_
\- Completion of " Free Us From Ourselves [quest=7722]" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of " A Beginning From an End [quest=7723]" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of " All Rivers Lead to the Sea [quest=7724]" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of " The Cost of Balance [quest=7725]" (Arx Mentis)
_As of a patch in December 2014:_
\- Confronting Calix [quest=8000] (Arx Mentis) - group version of the final raid event.

---

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Krinoch Jal ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple. This is a Kedge found on the southwestern part of the floating island._
_Pre-requisites for requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm"
_Say "speak" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Krinoch Jal says 'The sea coughed and spat like the angriest surf you've ever seen, and there was a terrible glow that dove deep into the dark water. Then they began arriving through the portal: Combine from Katta Castrum, escaping some calamity. A few made it through only to be struck down by the storms. If only I could stop this [madness]...'
You say, 'What madness?'
Krinoch Jal says 'Cecile Aldegund's [vision] seemed so lovely: A home away from the prisons created by the Combine, and the embrace of a god who would protect us. But with this storm we wreck our ocean and end the lives of the creatures around us. And must we make the innocent among the Combine suffer for the sins of their ancestors?'
You say, 'What vision?'
Krinoch Jal says 'It is clouded now, like the skies above the island. I want to do the right thing, like that gnome I've spied on the beach, helping those who have suffered from our actions. Please [speak] to Kelpin Larch and beg him to cease the storms around the floating island. But don't tell him I spoke to you!'
You say, 'I'll speak to him...'
Krinoch Jal says 'Remember not to tell him about me!'
You have been assigned the task 'Gathering Clouds'.
Krinoch Jal told you that a Kedge named Kelpin Larch has been sending storms against the floating island where the portal lies. Krinoch wants you to speak to Kelpin about changing his ways. If he does not, Ognit will need to come up with a different plan.

---

!

---

Find Kelpin Larch and speak with him 0/7 (Tempest Temple)
_Kelpin Larch ( **click for map image**) is found at the top of the mountainside on the southern end of the mountain in the middle of the zone. He /cons KOS, but is non-aggro. Hail him until the task updates._
Kelpin Larch's fins bristle. 'Krinoch sent you, didn't he? The traitorous lover of the legged ones would have us cease the storms and forsake our pursuit of Karana's favor. And for what? For the safety of the Combine?'
Kelpin Larch says 'If they spill from the portal and fall under the fists of my lightning, it is as Cecile wishes. She knows what the Combine do to Kedge. I know what their knives and potions have done to my body.'
Kelpin Larch says 'The experiments resume. They will not stop. And so the Combine shall not set foot on the island while I watch!'
Bindess Lon looks to her master. 'Don't let them take you again! Ask Cecile to send another blast into the waves! Destroy Katta Castrum!
Kelpin Larch touches his apprentice's shoulder. 'You have been with me long, and remained true to my teachings even during my captivity. I will not leave you now. But I doubt even Cecile can channel magic as powerful as we saw breaching the sea.'
Kelpin Larch says 'You, however, have learned much about storms and magic, Bindess! Show this friend of the enslavers what you know!'
Bindess Lon says 'Happily!'
You hear Kelpin's stern voice mingled with the surf. 'Into the sea, not into the storm totems! No, it is fine. They know not how they work. Next time you need to carry an object on an air current...' Kelpin begins lecturing Bindess on the finer points of controlling storm winds through magic.

---

Inspect the storm totem 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_After speaking with Kelpin Larch, you are ported to a small floating island on which you find "a storm totem". /inspect it, and you'll be knocked off into the water._
The storm winds swirl around you when you touch the totem.
Krinoch calls from the floating island above. 'You see how the totems bolster the winds? Perhaps the gnome you've spoken of can use his magic to rob the totems of their power!'

---

Speak with Ognit about the storm totems 0/4 (Tempest Temple)
_Ognit Eznertob ( **click for map image**) is on the eastern coast of the island. Hail him until the task updates._
Ognit Eznertob taps his stomach and scowls in thought. 'Too much time gone by... I'm sorry my spell wasn't more, um, helpful. It's been so many years since I studied under Al`Kabor. He said I'd never make a decent wizard, and maybe he was right.'
Ognit Eznertob touches his head. 'Soon these streaks of gray will pile up in snow drifts. Despite what Al`Kabor wishes, we don't live forever. Sometimes you must stop struggling up the path to the summit if you know you don't have the strength or skill to make it.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'After we thought Firiona died, I admitted to myself that I would never be a great wizard. I went to Ak`Anon and devoted myself to tinkering.' Ognit rubs his chin. 'And with those skills and what little magic I recall, we might come up with a solution for the storm.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'With a lot of tinkering and a little magic, I might be able to reverse the effects of the totems. Could you find a kedge totem that has not yet been imbued with storm magic? You might find some lying around the island. You might need to defend yourself if the kedge don't like your taking their totems.'

---

Inspect and retrieve an unpowered storm totem for Ognit 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
Deal with the kedge guarding the totem 0/2 (Tempest Temple)
_"an unpowered storm totem" ( **click for map image**) are found around the northern and northwestern parts of the island (and one is on the southern part). Each is guarded by 2x "a totem empowerer"._
_/inspect "an unpowered storm totem" to receive "Unpowered Storm Totem" on your cursor. The Kedge guards are immune to stuns and mezzes (duo guards are NOT aggro-linked)._
Give the storm totem to Ognit 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
Ognit Eznertob says 'Good. Now, if I only had my tools... I left them near the boat we took from the Brothers' island. Brother Minkins was standing guard, but I haven't heard from him in a while.'

---

Find the chest on the shore and Brother Minkins 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Find "a tool chest" ( **click for map image**) along the northeastern coast. (Location update here.)_
You don't see Brother Minkins, but a voice moans from within the coral forest. 'The sirens... help me...'

---

Find Brother Minkins inside the coral formation 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Brother Minkins ( **click for map image**) is found along the western edge of the reef. This is a location update (/loc 545, 140, 3)._
Minkins lies in the shallows, and crabs are picking over his body.

---

Kill the scavenging crabs that are picking over Brother Minkins 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
_Kill any three of the "a scavenging crab" in the area. These are not social with any other mobs and are mezzable/stunnable._

---

Speak with Brother Minkins and see how he fares 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Be ready for more mob aggro upon hail._
You say, 'Hail, Brother Minkins'
Brother Minkins says 'The sirens... they took me... used me... don't go in the caverns... don't go...' A rattling sigh issues from Brother Minkins' mouth as his stomach twitches and separates. 'Guuuhhhhhh...'
_5x "a gut crab" spawn at his location._

---

Kill the gut crabs issuing from Brother Minkins 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
_These hit for a max ~1,000 and have relatively few hitpoints. They are not social with the other crabs in the area, but you'll want to be careful about proximity aggro. If you set up in the tiny cubby next to Brother Minkins, you should be able to fight them without any aggro from the full-size crabs (except maybe the closest one)._
_If these are left idle for any longer than about 20 seconds, they despawn. If you fail this step, you can respawn them by hailing Brother Minkins again._

---

Speak with Brother Minkins 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Brother Minkins'
Brother Minkins says 'I thought I'd take the tools and get out of here; maybe sell them... I won't need 'em anymore... I didn't wear my helmet... always wear your helmet... tell Jameswell I did my best... to avoid temptation...'
_You receive "Ognit's Tinkering Tools"._

---

Speak with Ognit about his tinkering tools 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Ognit Eznertob'
Ognit Eznertob says 'Brother Minkins is...? On the voyage he kept telling us sirens could be lurking anywhere. I suppose he finally found them.' Ognit takes the tools and looks thoughtfully at one of the storm totems. 'Now, if I only knew more about the [spells] the kedge are using...''
_Rewards:_
Offers 2 Options:
OPTION 1
75 platinum
Experience (Approximately 2% of experience required to go from level 105 to 106)
4 AA Points
OPTION 2
Glyph of Glowing Experience
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Glyph of Glowing Experience [item=118739]

---

---

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

This group mission begins with Ognit Eznertob ( **click for map image**), who is located on the eastern coast of the island.
_Pre-requisites for requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm"
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds"
_Say "scrolls" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Ognit Eznertob says 'Brother Minkins is...? On the voyage he kept telling us sirens could be lurking anywhere. I suppose he finally found them.' Ognit takes the tools and looks thoughtfully at one of the storm totems. 'Now, if I only knew more about the [spells] the kedge are using...''
You say, 'What spells?'
Ognit Eznertob says 'Al`Kabor never taught me enough of all he knows. I would think Norrath's greatest wizard would have possessed even a puff of knowledge about storms. So again I am at a loss. If the kedge carry [scrolls] filled with the knowledge of storm magic, I could puzzle out their workings.'
You say, 'What scrolls?'
Ognit Eznertob says 'The druids surely carry such scrolls.'
You have been assigned the task 'Clearer Skies'.
Ognit almost has everything he needs to combat the intense storm. Once you supply him with the rest of what he needs, you can push back the storm around the portal to Katta Castrum.

---

Loot storm scrolls from the kedge 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
_Kill Kedge mobs ("a tempest keeper"; "a tempest evoker") in the center area of the island (patrolling the path through the mountain) until you've looted 6x "Storm Scroll". Mobs in this area hit for a max ~12,500 and tend to be immune to both stun and mez (exceptions being "a storm dervish" and "an earthen dervish")._
Give the storm scrolls to Ognit 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
Ognit Eznertob unrolls several scrolls and scans them quickly. 'I see that they say... well... I have no idea what these say!' Ognit tosses the scrolls on the ground in frustration. 'I really should have kept up with my studies! I'll need more time to figure out what the scrolls mean, but what I can make out so far is that I will need aged metal. There should be plenty of that in the shipwreck.

---

Loot aged metal pieces from the undead sailors 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
_Head to the shipwreck off the northeast coast and kill undead until you've looted 6x "Aged Metal"._
Give the aged metal pieces to Ognit 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
Ognit Eznertob hammers, curses, casts a spell, and curses again. He holds up the altered totems. 'These should disrupt the storms. Should. I've imbued them with a calming spell that should interact with the charges in the air, and... well, I hope it's enough. We should [go] place these before the kedge know what we are up to!'
_The next part of the task involves an instanced version of Tempest Temple. Say "go" to zone into the instance._
You say, 'Let's go...'
Ognit Eznertob says 'We'll meet at the floating island!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Tempest Temple Mission.

---

Speak with Ognit near the floating island 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Ognit Eznertob'
Ognit Eznertob lifts his head to the clouded sky and shields his eyes from the rain, looking at the floating rocks around the island. 'Take the disruptors. We'll head up the ramp and place them around the island. Tell me when you're ready, and I'll deliver you safely to the rocks.'
_When ready for a fight, hail Ognit Eznertob to be ported up to the small island next to the path._
You say, 'Hail, Ognit Eznertob'
Ognit Eznertob says 'Up you go!'

---

Place the first disruptor and defeat the totem defenders 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
_After a small delay, you'll see this..._
Ognit Eznertob shouts 'Place the disruptor next to the storm totem to knock out the power of the kedge's storm!'
Kelpin Larch yells from the island ridge, 'You would make this island safe for the Combine? You would strip us of our refuge? We endured decades of suffering at their hands, and now you would return us to their clutches? One of us will die before that happens!'
_...And 3x "a temple guard" spawn around the storm totem. These mobs are permanently rooted, each hit for a max ~9,500; have ~1,750,000 hitpoints; and are stunnable but are NOT mezzable. They summon when damaged, do NOT see invisibility, and are NOT auto-aggro._

---

Place the second disruptor and defeat the totem defenders 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
_Repeat as above for the next island up._
Bindess Lon joins her voice with her master's. 'You will never take our island from us! You will never subjugate us again -- not even with your most powerful beams of magic!'

---

Place the third disruptor and defeat the totem defenders 0/4 (Tempest Temple)
_Repeat as above for the next island up, except this time with one additional mob._
Kelpin Larch shouts 'Katta Castrum will drown! This island will be no refuge for the Combine! There will be no survivors!'

---

Place the fourth disruptor and defeat the totem defenders 0/4 (Tempest Temple)
_Repeat as above, again with four mobs._
Ognit Eznertob shouts 'This is the last one!'
Bindess Lon glances worriedly at her master. 'They're still coming! May I use all you've taught me?'
Kelpin Larch shouts 'Yes, show them the resilience of the kedge!'

---

Speak with Kelpin Larch 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Hail him until the task updates. (Be ready for a fight.)_
Kelpin Larch shouts 'Krinoch! Slink back into the sea, slave! You are not worthy to stand among free kedge! Why don't you teleport to Katta Castrum and offer yourself as a sacrifice for your beloved Tsaph Katta!'
Krinoch Jal shouts 'No, no, I am loyal to Karana! I love the rain and the storms! So delightful on the skin!' Krinoch shivers and averts his eyes from Kelpin. 'I only want peace for us all!''
Kelpin Larch shouts 'There should be no peace for the Combine! Did you know that at one time Tsaph Katta preached of inclusiveness and the value of all races? And what of our race? To the Combine we were lower than the fish in the ocean deep. But now we have risen to the surface, and the Combine languish below the waves!'
Ognit Eznertob holds out his palm and puffs his chest, looking as big as a gnome can. 'The storms shall cease! Return to your island and make peace, or you will have to deal with me!'
Bindess Lon laughs. 'Such big words for so small a creature! I've seen your spells, and they don't frighten me. You're no sort of wizard!' Bindess looks to Kelpin. 'Master, may I teach the gnome how to properly cast a spell?'
Kelpin Larch shouts 'A magic lesson would be beyond him. Teach the gnome how to die!'
Bindess Lon says 'Get off our isle, landling!'
Kelpin Larch shouts 'You can defeat him! He is no match for you!'

---

Defeat Bindess Lon 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Bindess Lon isn't much harder than regular mobs here. She has ~3.9 million hitpoints and is immune to mez and stun. She casts "Lesser Storm" on random single targets:_
Lesser Storm: Single Target, Cold (-975)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 50000
_Once she reaches very low health, she goes non-aggro, and Kelpin Larch attacks:_
Kelpin Larch shouts 'Bindess, no!' Kelpin rushes to the aid of his apprentice, wreathing a barrier of magic around her. 'They will not harm you!'

---

Defeat Kelpin Larch 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Like Bindess Lon, Kelpin Larch is only a bit more difficult than the average mob here (has ~5 million hitpoints). He casts "Greater Storm":_
Greater Storm: PB AE 100', Cold (-975)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 60000
_DPS Kelpin Larch to the death._
Bindess Lon shouts 'Master, you can't die! Not to them! You're stronger than they are! Master!'
Kelpin Larch has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Kelpin Larch's corpse moans malevolently, "We will rise... from the deep..."
Bindess Lon says, 'Master... I followed you all those years... I followed you to freedom, to the surface... and now am I to follow you in death?' Bindess beseeches you with her eyes. 'Please don't kill me... I don't want to die yet...'

---

Speak with Krinoch Jal 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Krinoch Jal'
Krinoch Jal says 'I thought I would be the one mixing my blood with the rain...' Krinoch looks at the sky. 'The storm has diminished near the portal. Now it should be safe enough to cross through it. I don't know what's happening in Katta Castrum, but once the Combine start arriving here, my brethren might be even less amenable to words than Kelpin was.'

---

Speak with Ognit 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
_Hail him until the task completes ("go" will zone you out). A chest also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Ognit Eznertob says 'Thanks to you the portal should be safer, but still the storms rage.' Ognit looks at his hands. He breathes hard. 'It's been a long time since I've been in a fight. I don't know whether to cheer or wretch... I had forgotten how it felt to see your enemy lying dead at your feet. It used to be easier. But there might be many more dead to follow.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'Katta Castrum must be in trouble if their people are trying to come here.' Ognit looks at the volcanic island glowing brightly in the darkness. 'The beam arced above the island, but it must not have come from the kedge. And still Cecile controls the winds. I cannot yet accompany you.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'I will wait here. I know I can't face Cecile. You saw how little I remember about magic! But I will see what can be done and help the shipwreck survivors in the meantime. [Go] in my stead. See Firiona and make sure she is safe; tell her what has happened here. If she doubts you, tell her 'the companions sail the darkened sea.' Storms and black waters lie ahead, \_\_\_\_\_.'
_Rewards:_
187 platinum, 5 gold
Experience

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This group mission begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Katta Castrum: Deluge. She's found in the southwestern part of the temple's main floor._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "The Pellarus Satum" (Head Mage Antius's non-progression task)
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
_Say "Tsaph" to request the mission and "ready" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie says 'You come from the surface. I can see that. You have come beneath the waves to a broken place, a place I thought I could heal.' Firiona crosses her arms. 'But I cannot be too quick to trust after what has happened here. How do I [know] who sends you?''
You say, 'You know it's Ognit...'
Firiona Vie frowns and nods as you speak Ognit's phrase to her and tell what happened on the island of storms. 'Ognit is safe then? Safer certainly than we shall be. That [beam] of light he saw: It did not fall harmlessly into the sea.
You say, 'What beam of light?'
Firiona Vie says 'I was walking the courtyard when a brilliant light cracked through the air and struck the Pellarus Sanctum. The sea began gushing into the city and flooding the lower wards. Citizens drowned, and those remaining fled to higher ground or tried to escape through [portals].'
You say, 'What portals?'
Firiona Vie says 'Before we arrived, the Combine had been busy establishing portals throughout the Buried Sea, reconnecting to lost parts of their empire. But most of those portals are either underwater or inoperable now. There are few options for escape, so we must [repair] the dome before it crumbles and reactivate the portal to [Tsaph]'s chamber. We must move quickly, so I must apologize if I have not had the time to give you a more detailed [account].'
You say, 'How do we repair it?'
Firiona Vie says 'Head Mage Antius has been leading the efforts to repair the dome. You should speak with him and see what can be done.'
You say, 'Tell me about your account'
Firiona Vie looks at you without speaking for several seconds. You can see the sadness in her eyes. 'I must apologize to you, \_\_\_\_\_, and to everyone in Norrath. I have been [absent] for many years.'
You say, 'Why have you been absent?'
Firiona Vie says 'I remembered little after the Kithicor explosion: my hands pressed against glass, the needles, the leering faces, the dark chants. And I remember seeing myself mirrored through two panes: the dark elf, my nemesis, held in a chamber just like mine, both of us [prisoners] of Discord.'
You say, 'Prisoners?'
Firiona Vie says 'I do not know how long we were there. For us time ceased to exist, and I cannot say whether I bore the captivity asleep or awake. When the adventurers came and released us, I sadly could not recall their faces even if I were to see them. Perhaps you were there, \_\_\_\_\_. If so, I have [squandered] the gift you gave me.'
You say, 'Squandered, how?'
Firiona Vie says 'I do not say this to evoke your pity. It was a long journey home, through the portal and the Void. I returned to a kingdom that thought I was dead, that had already mourned and forgotten me. What [use] was I to them?'
You say, 'No use...'
Firiona Vie sighs. 'I languished in the chambers of my father's house, as I admit I am wont to do in times of grief. How I have failed Norrath in my absence! I could not face my father or [Tunare]. When at last I had the courage to enter the Plane of Growth and see my father, Galeth, who had thought I was dead and who had long since abandoned his mortal form...' Firiona trails off and wipes a tear from her eye.
You say, 'Tunare?'
Firiona Vie says 'She spoke so gently, like a caressing wind... There was no condemnation, only love and purpose. She asked for no penance, only the commitment of her champion to balance. Tunare spoke of the Buried Sea, of the storm clouds gathering over the islands and the darkness that would soon descend beneath the waves. She bade me to take the Lifeguide, find my [companions], and set sail for Katta Castrum.'
You say, 'What companions?'
Firiona Vie says 'You too would doubt your eyes if a ghost walked into the room. My friends had already mourned me and gone their separate ways. And the years have piled upon them in deep drifts, as Ognit might say. It is no simple thing to set out on the road to [adventure] when the cold has already begun seeping into your bones. But they followed me as though it were not only I who had come back from the dead.'
You say, 'What adventure?'
Firiona Vie says 'We chartered the Swift Fin from the Barren Coast, and Captain Sedgethwait sailed us into the Buried Sea. I will not digress about how we met the [Brothers]. Already they have played a far greater role in this journey than I would have liked.'
You say, 'Who are the Brothers?'
Firiona Vie says 'I will say this about them: their leaders, Jameswell Dagmire and Quigley Barnables, have better knowledge of these seas and their coming storms than I do. They told us of the strange things they are seeing on the islands, and I left most of my companions there to investigate while I headed to [Katta Castrum]. After what has transpired, perhaps that was a mistake...'
You say, 'Katta Castrum?'
Firiona Vie says 'Strange... it occurs to me now that even Zebuxoruk said it would all begin in Katta Castrum. So much of my thoughts then were in a fog, and I could not take in all he was saying. But Tunare was clear: I was to seek Katta Castrum and through the healing of Tsaph Katta restore [balance].'
You say, 'Balance?'
Firiona Vie says 'It is never [simple] to know what that means. If the legends are true, Tsaph sought balance through inclusion, desiring to unite all races under one rule, as one people. If he were healed, could he be another champion for balance, or merely another tyrant marching with balance and unity as his vanguard?'
You say, 'Not simple...'
Firiona Vie says 'Indeed, how can I ever feel as though I have achieved balance while Lanys still lives, while her face still stares at me from behind that glass...' Firiona looks away. 'She is never far off... but I had my [duty] in Tsaph's chamber.'
You say, 'What duty?'
Firiona Vie says 'I consulted with Head Attendant Haestus, Head Mage Antius, and Al`Kabor. I drew upon all the healing arts I could remember, and we decided to place the gem of the Lifeguide within Tsaph's stasis chamber. With Tunare's blessing it just might draw the poison from Tsaph's body. Despite my dark visions and my apprehension about what we may find in [Tsaph]'s chamber, I still cling to hope. We must reach him as soon as possible!'
You say, 'So, about Tsaph...'
Firiona Vie says 'With the dome safe for now and the portal active, we must hurry to Tsaph's chamber! Tell me when you are [ready]!'
You have been assigned the task 'The Edge of Balance'.
Now that the dome has been stabilized, Firiona wants to check on Tsaph and make sure nothing has happened to him. She is worried about what this attack will mean for her mission in the Buried Sea.

---

Speak with Firiona 0/5 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Hail her until the task updates._
Firiona Vie gazes at the portal. 'I hope Tsaph remains safe. The gem of the Lifeguide that I placed in his stasis chamber still protects and heals him, but who knows what dark fates will visit this place now that the first blow has been struck?'
Firiona Vie says 'I have not yet told you of the vision I saw. One night as I held vigil over Tsaph, with the staff of the Lifeguide in my hands, my eyes lost sight. Then stars appeared all around me. I could see her: Lanys gripping the Lifeguide. I stared at her, and I could see her looking back. I knew she was in the Void, and she knew I was in Katta Castrum. It was only a matter of time.'
Firiona Vie says 'Has Lanys arrived already? As soon as my vision returned, I hastened to Al`Kabor. I entrusted the staff of the Lifeguide to him and told him to warn the other companions that Lanys is coming. But he did not travel through the portal that brought us here, and I have not heard of him since. I worry even more now that Ognit mentioned nothing of him... and now the attack has come...'
Firiona Vie says 'The beam you spoke of -- could it have been Lanys' first salvo?' Firiona pauses. 'I did not think she possessed such power... It was the beam that might have ruined my work in healing Tsaph.'
Firiona Vie says 'The teleporter to Tsaph's chamber was flooded when the dome was pierced. I have been trying to reconfigure this teleporter to lead to the chamber. With my notes and the work you have done for Researcher Chlearpa, we might be able to gain access. Speak with her about the notes I have made thus far. We must get inside the chamber!'

---

Speak with Researcher Chlearpa 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
You say, 'Hail, Researcher Chlearpa'
Researcher Chlearpa says 'Brilliant... if we just extrapolate the electrogenic charge on the mana plate transducifier, we can create a free-linked coordinate paradigm reorganizer...' Researcher Chlearpa tinkers with the portal. 'There! We should be able to reach Tsaph's chamber now... at least parts of us will probably make it!'
Raised voices from the praetorians in the courtyard echo inside the temple. Tactician Sodut shouts, 'Ready your weapons! Something is coming!'

---

Investigate the disturbance outside the temple 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Just outside the temple, several members of the Combine attack Tactician Sodut and company._

---

Kill the invaders 0/5 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_The "a combine \_\_\_\_\_" mobs hit for a max ~13,000 and are immune to stuns, but are mezzable and rootable/snareable. They are NOT aggro-linked._
_Upon the last death:_
Tactician Sodut grunts, trying to ignore the blood trickling down his side. 'They fight like Combine, but I've never seen such armor before. Fall back to the temple! There may be more of them!'

---

Fall back to the temple 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_You may want to invis up before moving back in (or move quickly). Once you step inside the temple, five more "a combine \_\_\_\_\_" mobs spawn at the doorway._
Head Mage Antius shouts 'Behind you, near the temple entrance! More of the them!'

---

Kill the invaders 0/5 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Same as above._
Head Mage Antius shouts 'I will defend this temple as long as my magic holds!'

---

Speak with Head Mage Antius 0/4 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Hail him until the task updates, at which point (shortly after) the two brutes become auto-aggro._
Una Lucem stares placidly at Antius through the magical barrier. Her eyes do not blink, and her brow betrays no hint of anger. 'My father says if you don't give me Tsaph, I can see what's inside you.'
Una Lucem shouts 'My father let me see Quellin's insides after I took off his head. There were so many colors, like the autumn leaves.' Una Lucem finally blinks. 'Give me Tsaph.''
Una Lucem shouts 'Is this where Tsaph sleeps?'
Researcher Chlearpa's words leap from her mouth, trying to escape. 'I fixed it, and now you can use it, and Tsaph is up there; go ahead, and don't kill me, please!'
Una Lucem points to her guards and then to the ground in front of the portal. 'Stay. If they come to bother me, sit them at the table, with their heads on their plates. We'll have tea.'

---

Kill Una Lucem's brutes 0/2 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_The brutes, by this time, have moved to the portal in the northwestern corner of the zone-in room. These two are stunnable, mezzable, and rootable/snareable. They might be aggro-linked. Each hits for a max ~16,000 and summons when damaged._

---

Enter Tsaph's chamber and speak with Una Lucem 0/2 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Enter the portal, and you'll find yourself in Tsaph Katta's resting chamber alongside Firiona Vie. Hail Una Lucem, and be ready for a fight._
Una Lucem shouts 'He looks peaceful. Don't make noise and wake him up.'
Firiona Vie rushes to aid the head attendant. 'Haestus, look out! Don't let her...' Firiona's voice catches as Una Lucem envelops her in magic. Her eyes entreat you to stop Una Lucem.

---

Defeat Una Lucem 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Una Lucem hits for a max ~22,000 (no flurry; no rampage). Every 20 seconds, she casts "Shock of Magic" on a random single target:_
Shock of Magic: Single Target, Magic (-975)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 80000
2: Stun (3.00 sec)
_DPS her to 10%, at which point she goes non-aggro._
Una Lucem says, 'You are strange.' She waves her hand, and you feel magic gripping you.

---

Speak with Una Lucem 0/8 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Hail her until the task updates._
You say, 'Hail, Una Lucem'
Una Lucem raises an eyebrow. 'You are stronger than the criminals father lets me fight. But the ones in chains can't protect their necks.'
_Lanys T`Vyl appears..._
Lanys T`Vyl laughs. 'Una, stop playing with your enemies. Where is your sense of bloodlust, your anger that your opponents still draw breath? Obviously your father did not mix enough dark elf into you!'
Lanys T`Vyl says 'If you're going to kill them, then kill them -- unless you have a good reason not to.' Lanys smiles at Firiona. 'Like Miss Vie here. How could I kill my old friend who shared with me the long captivity of Discord?'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'You've found yourself captured again! Does this seem familiar: trapped and helpless while your allies are hurt and dying? Una, kill the head attendant.'
Una Lucem's sword flicks out like a snake's tongue, swift and silent. Haestus' belly sags and begins to spill its contents. 'It's more colorful than his robe.' Una watches the man dying on the floor.
Firiona Vie's eyes seethe with anger, though her mouth cannot voice it while Una's magic holds her.
Una Lucem shouts 'Father will be happy, but I don't know why he cares about some sleeping atavism.'
Lanys T`Vyl laughs. 'Do you see this, Firiona? Once again you lose your purpose in this world: your cause and your Lifeguide. But don't fret; I have plans for you and your companions. Perhaps you will find there are more important things to fight for than balance.
Firiona Vie punches the blood-soaked rug next to Haestus' body. 'Lanys...'

---

Speak with Firiona 0/6 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Hail her to complete the task. A chest also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Firiona Vie says 'You're never as ready as you think you are to lose someone under your care. I thought I was prepared for this journey. Too easily I forgot the cost of balance...'
Firiona Vie says 'Now my enemy has both Tsaph and the gem of the Lifeguide. And how long will it be before she realizes the staff is missing? She will tear apart the Buried Sea looking for it, and my companions will be shredded by her claws...'
Firiona Vie says 'If she can still feel the connection to the Lifeguide, no doubt she will search for Al`Kabor.' Firiona sighs. 'It's no easy thing to lead.''
Firiona Vie says 'Already my companions are spread over the seas, Al`Kabor is missing, and Lanys has part of the Lifeguide. And who is this Una that fights with Lanys? None of this has gone as I foresaw.'
Firiona Vie says 'Before all else, we must find Al`Kabor. If Lanys recombines the Lifeguide, there is no telling how much destruction she will wreak on Norrath. Speak with me after we [leave] and return to the chamber below.'

90 platinum

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Katta Castrum: Deluge. She's found in the southwestern part of the temple's main floor._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of A Rising Storm [quest=7702] (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of Gathering Clouds [quest=7703] (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of Clearer Skies [quest=7704] (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of The Edge of Balance [quest=7706] (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Say "speak" to request the task.
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks up as you approach, taken out of deep thought. '\_\_\_\_\_, I still do not know where Al`Kabor is. Before the [beam] struck the dome, he had been reading the [books] in the city and wandering the streets, visiting portals. Since our journey began, he has seemed [troubled], and his agitation grew when we descended into the sea.
You say, 'Troubled, why?'
Firiona Vie says 'He has been talking about his theory of mind -- how the mind sustains the body and how the body sustains the world. He kept saying that he would not be able to save anything unless he saved his own mind. And when we arrived in Katta Castrum, he started reading the [books]'
You say, 'What books?'
Firiona Vie says 'Books of dreams and their effects, treatments of mental instability, and accounts of historical Combine gone mad. The time I saw him before I gave him the Lifeguide's staff, he was reading near Arcanist Thalian. It might prove fruitless, but will you [speak] with Thalian and see if he knows the subject Al`Kabor was researching?'
You say, 'I'll speak to him'
Firiona Vie says 'Thank you. The arcanist is nearby in the temple.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Absent Scholar'.
Firiona has asked Al`Kabor to keep the staff of the Lifeguide safe, but she does not know where he went. Now you must search Katta Castrum for clues.

---

!

---

1\. Speak with Arcanist Thalian 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Arcanist Thalian [npc=47903] ( **click for map image**) is in the southeastern part of the same room as Firiona Vie.
You say, 'Hail, Arcanist Thalian'
Arcanist Thalian says 'Al`Kabor? I tried not to pay much attention to the man. I was doing my best to help hold up the dome with my magic, and he had the nerve to glance over the book he was reading and criticize the wording of my spell. Which book? He dropped it on the floor by the shelf when he was done with it.'

---

2\. Find the book Al`Kabor was reading near Arcanist Thalian 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
The book History of Portal Travel [item=119500] is a ground spawn right next to Arcanist Thalian.
You peruse the contents of the book titled 'A History of Portal Travel.' '... in the year following the dimming and restoration of the Pellarus Sanctum, reconstruction began on the portals connecting domes once thought lost...'

---

3\. Speak with Arcanist Thalian again 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
You say, 'Hail, Arcanist Thalian'
Arcanist Thalian says 'You want to know more about him? Well, he did ask me where we keep books about the mind and consciousness. They are on the floor below, but that area was flooded when the dome cracked.'

---

4\. Find the book Al`Kabor was reading in the submerged area under the temple 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Head to the basement of the temple (accessed down the ramps off either side of the room in which Arcanist Thalian is located). The book Of Dreams and the Mind [item=118731] is a ground spawn on top of the southeastern-most table in the submerged basement.
Despite the runny ink and soggy pages, you peruse the contents of the book titled 'Of Dreams and the Mind' and see a marked section. '... when the slumbering mind is visited by nightmares, the waking mind cannot be free of dark thoughts. Unless the garden of the mind be purged of the nightmare memories, it will bear rotten fruit... '

---

5\. Return to the upper temple and keep looking for Al`Kabor 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Head Mage Antius calls out to you as you enter the main hall. 'I hear you are searching for Al`Kabor. He has not been seen in here since he left the temple days before the dome cracked. Tactician Sodut might know more.

---

6\. Speak with Tactician Sodut 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Tactician Sodut [npc=48385] ( **click for map image**) is found at /loc 265, -15, 1 (just down the ramp extending out of the temple).
You say, 'Hail, Tactician Sodut'
Tactician Sodut favors his injured side but still stands upright. 'I see you survived the attack. I could use you among my guards! Al`Kabor? While I was on patrol I saw the wizard heading south around the temple. Construction crews had been camped in tents there while working on restoring the temple walkways, but now the tents are occupied by shissar. And while you're at it, why don't you reclaim our tents? That canvas is too good for shissar.'

---

7\. Search the tents for clues about Al`Kabor's whereabouts 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
8\. Kill some shissar for Tactician Sodut 0/8 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Kill any eight shissar mobs. For the "clues", go to the shissar tents due south of the main temple (same area as Overlord Salazin) - Find "Al`Kabor's Journal" as a ground spawn on top of a crate in one of the south tents.

---

9\. Speak with Firiona about the books and journal 0/4 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Hail her until the task updates.
Firiona Vie opens the journal. 'It's in a cipher we have used before.' She scans the pages intently. 'His moods must have grown darker when we arrived beneath the sea. He says that he was seeking unused portals and domes, searching for a place to think...'
Firiona Vie reads from the journal. '...when time is given to me, I shall seek out the portal just beyond the inner wall. If the records speak truly, the dome shall be my refuge...'
Firiona Vie tosses the journal on the table. 'Did he take the staff there?! I told him to return to Ognit and set sail to Brother Island! Our party had to be alerted to the threat Lanys poses! Now they're all blind to the darkness that is about to descend!'
Firiona Vie crosses her arms. 'You should find the portal to which Al`Kabor referred. It is northwest of the temple. I must know where he's gone!'

---

10\. Investigate the portal northwest of the temple 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
You'll see a portal in one of the stone gazebos northwest of the temple. Approach it.
You see a cracked portal with writing scrawled next to it. The message is written in another cipher, and you copy the letters.

---

11\. Speak with Firiona Vie about the cipher 0/4 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Hail her until the task updates.
Firiona Vie translates the cipher and lays the paper on the table. 'Al`Kabor left before the attack, and... he teleported to an abandoned dome. He left coordinates and the words 'Station 10.' He took the staff there to think.'
Firiona Vie clenches her fist. 'I told him to go straight to Brother Island! How can I manage this expedition if I can't trust its members? Lanys and Una will be searching for him and the staff, if indeed Lanys still feels a connection with the Lifeguide. I must hasten to Al`Kabor immediately!'
Firiona Vie looks you in the eyes and places her hands on your shoulders. '\_\_\_\_\_, you have proven yourself capable and trustworthy thus far. I need you to go to Brother Island. Tell my companions they are all in danger and that we must meet on the island near the glinting shores.'
Firiona Vie says 'Ognit remains on the tempestuous island. Dagda, Dabner, and Dreezil stayed with the Brothers. Lyirae and Galeth sailed to the lost jungle shores. Thubr investigated the sunken halls of the dwarves. Return to Tempest Temple, the island of storms. Sail to Brother Island on the boat that stops at the dock. There you can speak with Jameswell Dagmire, one of the island's leaders. He can tell you more about my instructions to my companions. Go swiftly; we do not know how much time we have!'
_Rewards:_
Experience
60 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

_This shared task (1-6 players) begins with Quigley Barnables ( **click for map image**) in the tower on Brother Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
_Pre-Requisites for Participating:_
\- None
_Say "sleep" to request the task and "bed" to zone into the instanced zone._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Quigley Barnables crosses his arms and furrows his brow. 'It's a good start, but a day's worth of deliveries can't repay a sack of gold! You're going to be here a long time if you think menial labor is going to return Firiona and her companions to my good graces!' Quigley laughs. 'You better get some [sleep]; you're going to have a long day ahead of you!'
You say, 'Hail, Quigley Barnables'
Quigley Barnables says 'Why don't you go to [sleep] already?!'
You say, 'Sleep? Me?'
Quigley Barnables says 'Yes, you should lie down. Starting tomorrow, I'll turn your sweat into gold, no matter what it takes! Get to [bed]!'
You have been assigned the task 'The Cover of Night'.
Dagda wants to use the cover of night to escape the island. If there is little trouble with sirens, this will be an easy escape.
You say, 'Bed, yes...'
Quigley Barnables says 'Wake up ready to work!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

!

---

Speak with Dagda about getting off the island 0/2 (Brother Island)
_Inside the instance, hail Dagda until the task updates._
Dagda Icefury laughs. 'Do you see blood on my sword? I didn't kill them! Not that I didn't want to, but the sirens got to them first.' Dagda holds out her thumb and forefinger. 'This close to battle for the first time in a while, and someone steals my kills!'
Dagda Icefury points down the beach. 'Have you spoken to Dabner and Sedgethwait? We need to... ' Dagda's voice is drowned out by a song flowing from the sea. She shouts to be heard as sirens creep up the beach. 'Salt and blood go well together. Finally my sword will be sated!'
_At this time, 3x "a siren seeker" spawn and wander up to Dagda Icefury's location. They do not see invis._

---

Kill the sirens 0/3 (Brother Island)
_These are splittable, mezzable, and stunnable. They are Enchanter mobs and of average difficulty for Brother Island (hit for a max ~13,000). Upon the last death, you see:_
The sirens' song grows louder.

---

Speak with Dagda 0/2 (Brother Island)
_Hail her until the task updates._
Dagda Icefury flings the blood off her sword with a snap of her wrist. 'You never forget the moves; you only lose the speed. But as long as these stay strong --' Dagda points to her head and then her heart -- 'this will remain strong.' Dagda raises her sword.
Dagda Icefury says 'We must warn Sedgethwait, \_\_\_\_\_. He's our passage off this prison, and I know his men were never ones to resist the charms of beautiful women. Let's find him before we have to crew the Swift Fin ourselves!'

---

Head to Captain Sedgethwait 0/1 (Brother Island)
_Location update - Captain Sedgethwait is found in the same place as in the non-instanced zone (the inlet on the east-central coast of the island). He is under some duress._

---

Kill the sirens attacking Captain Dedgethwait 0/5 (Brother Island)
_Kill 3x "a siren seeker" (as before) and 2x "a siren snatcher". These are all, again, straightforward and fairly simple to split. The snatchers hit a bit harder (~16,000) and seem to be Warrior class mobs._

---

Speak with Captain Sedgethwait 0/1 (Brother Island)
You say, 'Hail, Captain Sedgethwait'
Captain Sedgethwait says 'Half my crew... on the seas for months without women... they never stood a chance...' Sedgethwait stuffs the feathers more tightly in his ears. 'I saw a group of sirens heading for the dome!'

---

Head to the main building 0/1 (Brother Island)
_Location update - the tower in the southern part of the zone, where you'll find another problem._

---

Defeat the sirens attacking Quigley and Saldar 0/10 (Brother Island)
_Kill 4x "a siren snatcher" (as before) and 6x "a siren slasher" (hit for a max ~12,000; AE rampage; lots of hitpoints; mezzable; immune to stuns)._

---

Speak with Quigley 0/5 (Brother Island)
_Hail him until the task updates._
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Quigley Barnables glares at Dagda. 'Too many brothers have already been carried off. They took Jameswell and Sprudd. The sirens were heading to the eastern beach!'
Dagda Icefury shouts 'And what of Dabner?'
Quigley Barnables shouts 'The sirens can have him! What did he do to pay back our gold? Eat our food? Read our books? He was taken with the others, and good riddance!'
Dagda Icefury shouts 'If a siren could even stomach you, I'd feed you to them myself!' Dagda slashes at the nearby vegetation. 'Dabner will never be able to resist a siren's song! I've seen how easily he becomes attached. I always thought he should break his obsession, but I didn't want it to happen this way!'
Dagda Icefury says 'To the beach! We cannot leave our brothers to this fate!'

---

Rescue Brother Sprudd from the sirens 0/4 (Brother Island)
_Head due east to the coast, and kill 3x "a siren snatcher" 1x "a siren slasher" (as before)._
Brother Sprudd shouts 'No, my pretty ladies!'

---

Save Jameswell from the sirens 0/5 (Brother Island)
_Just next to the area is Jameswell Dagmire. Kill 3x "a siren snatcher" 2x "a siren slasher" (as before)._
Jameswell Dagmire runs toward Dagda, eyes wide and unseeing. 'My love, my beauty, you are the siren that has captured my heart! There is no other that can compare with you, Dagda!'

---

Speak with Jameswell 0/3 (Brother Island)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Jameswell Dagmire staggers around, trying to shake the fog from his head. 'Are you... real? Or just another siren?' He reaches out his hand, trying to grab Dagda.
Dagda Icefury aims her sword at Jameswell's good arm. 'Easy there... you don't want to lose that one too!'
Quigley Barnables points beyond the shore. 'Look, out in the water: It's the barbarian's toy, Dabner!'
_A drowning Dabner Drednever flounders about offshore._

---

Wait for Dagda to reach Dabner 0/1 (Brother Island)
Dagda Icefury cups her hands to her mouth. 'Dabner, it's Dagda! Swim back! I'm not a siren!'
Dabner Drednever's voice replies faintly, sounding hollow, wounded, and exhausted. 'I must follow the beauty. So much beauty out there, everywhere, all over Norrath... beauty I can hold... beauty less distant... beauty less cold...' Scaly hands rise out of the sea and pull Dabner into the depths.
Dagda Icefury rushes into the waves, not bothering to remove her armor. 'Dabner!'
Dagda Icefury shouts back to the shore, 'I'm going to find him!' Dagda disappears into the sea.
_Both of them disappear into the sea..._

---

Speak with Quigley 0/5 (Brother Island)
_Hail him until the task completes. A chest also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Quigley Barnables groans. 'Don't be stupid. It would be suicide to follow her in the dark underwater! You'd never find the cavern entrance!'
Jameswell Dagmire shouts 'I'll go! When daylight comes I'll follow Dagda into the deepest darkness of the sea!'
Quigley Barnables shouts 'You're not going anywhere! Look at yourself all wide-eyed and panting! You're not fit to be anywhere near women!'
Quigley Barnables wipes his brow and looks at you. 'This could be the chance for you and Firiona's companions to redeem themselves. I lost many brothers tonight, and many more have already been taken. If you can rescue them, the debt is forgiven. I've already sent Horace the Resolved to the caverns, but as of yet I've heard nothing from him. Perhaps you won't fail.
Quigley Barnables says 'At first light I will send Brother Dilgen and Brother Visius to the caverns. Meet them there, and together bring back my brothers!' Quigley blows his nose on his kerchief. 'If you fail, I can always see how much the Swift Fin can fetch at the Barren Coast.'
_Rewards:_
150 platinum

_This solo task begins with Brother Dilgen ( **click for map image**) in the cave just north of the zone-in._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
_Say "find" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Brother Dilgen'
Brother Dilgen yells at you, his voice sounding muffled beneath his large helmet. 'What?! Sorry, I can't hear you that well. My chastity helmet is one of the more padded ones Quigley has made. I keep the Brothers well [stocked], so Quigley thought I would be useful on this [rescue] mission.
You say, 'Stocked?'
Brother Dilgen says 'Chastity helmets, light daggers for close combat, harpoons and rope -- everything you would need for fighting sirens! I've been with the Brothers for two years now, and I've proven I can craft and fight. I can handle myself. You'll never find ME in a compromising embrace!'
You say, 'Rescue mission?'
Brother Dilgen says 'I slept in my chastity helmet the night of the attack, just like Brother Saldar is always saying we should do. I'm glad I did! Who else would be left to save our brothers? Quigley sent me down here, saying I should find Brother Horace the Resolved. But the first person I saw was that barbarian woman, [Dagda].'
You say, 'Dagda?'
Brother Dilgen says 'She asked if I had seen a gnome dressed in a robe, with graying hair. Then she dove into the water and swam through the northern tunnel. You should probably [find] her before she gets herself killed.'
You say, 'I'll find her'
Brother Dilgen says 'Good luck! Keep your ears closed and eyes downcast!'
You have been assigned the task 'Loyal is the Heart'.
Dagda hastened Dabner. Find her and see if she could use your assistance in rescuing her friend.

---

!

---

Find and speak with Dagda 0/9 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Dagda is found in the caverns east of the zone-in area (in between the spineback and crocolisk clearings)._
Dagda Icefury smiles ruefully. 'So you followed me, \_\_\_\_\_. Not the smartest thing you've done... though maybe waiting until rested and fed was smarter than the course I took...' Dagda shifts her body. A gash in her leg separates and pours out thick blood. She grabs the wound, grunts, and applies pressure.
Dagda Icefury says 'Like I said, the first thing to go isn't your skill, it's your speed. There was a time when four sirens at once wouldn't have stood a chance.'
Dagda Icefury is silent for a moment, looking at the cave floor. Seawater drips from her hair. 'I keep seeing his head disappear beneath the waves. Foolish as it might be, I thought he would always cling to me no matter what songs might tempt him. You think you know a man after so many years...'
Dagda Icefury says 'Blood seeps around Dagda's fingers. 'In darkness I descended, ears and lungs bursting. The first air I tasted was in this dank lair. I could not see Dabner in the pitch black. I wandered unsleeping, searching.'
Dagda Icefury says 'I found the lair: sirens awaiting my blade. One fell, then two, and then two more. When you're surrounded, you move fast, you kill fast. But I wasn't fast enough. A temptress's claws are sharp, sharper than steel... and they cut deeper.'
Dagda Icefury says 'I stumbled. One grabbed my sword. Another ripped away my shield and tossed it in the water.' Dagda bows her head. 'If I didn't owe my friend my life, I would have thrown it away rather than retreat.'
Dagda Icefury laughs. 'So this is my return to battle and adventure: wounded in a dingy cavern after my first real fight. Now isn't the time to sit and moan, not when your friends are in danger.
Dagda Icefury says 'Just my sword and shield... skin will mend, but I need steel to fight. You're here now, so I know you care at least that much, whatever your intentions. Look for them in the lair of the sirens, near the towering coral.'
Dagda Icefury grimaces. 'And sea turtle fat... for the pain.'

---

Defeat the siren that stole Dagda's sword in the coral formation 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Find Dagda's shield beneath the coral formation 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Find sea turtle fat to dull Dagda's pain 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_For the sword: Head into the siren caverns east of Dagda's location where you'll find a giant purple coral inside which is "a sword thief" which is leashed to the room in which it's found. Its damage output is standard for the zone, but it has a lot of hitpoints. DPS it to 10% for the sword update:_
a sword thief runs her finger down Dagda's sword. 'This is such a lovely blade, a regal gift for Queen Silisia! Your filthy presence in her cavern has made her so cross! But maybe this will soothe her. Though I can't sing, I can be useful in many ways!'
_For the shield: On the cavern floor far below this giant coral at about is a clickable shield that sticks out of the ground (location is random, but will be somewhere on the floor in the large underwater cavern beneath the coral). When you click this, a mob spawns (doesn't see invis). It summons from 100% health; is stunnable but immune to mez; and is leashed to the cavern in which it's found. Kill it and loot it for the shield update:_
As you reach for Dagda's shield, a gigantic tentacle wraps around it.
a giant octopus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
_"Sea Turtle Fat" is a common (maybe 100%) drop from "a sea turtle" mobs in this same area. They don't appear to be social with other sealife and are of regular difficulty for the zone (mezzable but immune to stuns)._

---

Tell Dagda what happened to her sword 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury curses at the name of Silisia. 'A siren queen? Either Dabner rests at the bottom of these watery tunnels or she has him. She takes my friend and now my weapon... I swear I'll bring her a fitting gift!
Give Dagda her shield 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Dagda Icefury receives the cracked shield and looks upon it with sadness. 'It was a gift from Ognit and Dabner, forged after Firiona was lost to us.' Upon the steel a maiden stands, sword upraised. On either side of her, two small figures, hands outstretched, emit strands of magic that enwreathe the maiden and her blade. Cracks now rive the shield, separating the figures.
Give Dagda the sea turtle fat 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Dagda Icefury rubs the viscous fat over the gash in her leg and binds it with cloth. 'I can stand. I still feel the pain, but that is good. Pain never lets you forget, never lets you rest.'

---

Speak with Dagda 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
You say, 'Hail, Dagda Icefury'
Dagda Icefury says 'Quigley wants you to save his brothers? I don't care. They deserve nothing from me. Quigley speaks of debt. The only debt I owe is to my friends. Firiona will understand. Go back to Dilgen if you are so eager to fill the pockets of pirates. I'm going to save Dabner.'
_Rewards:_
Experience
60 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

This solo task begins with Brother Dilgen ( **click for map image**) in the cave just north of the zone-in.
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
