Say obtain to request the task.
You say, 'Hail, Brother Dilgen'
Brother Dilgen says 'Then Dagda will not help [rescue] my brothers? Quigley told me you owed us... maybe you're someone who actually knows how to honor a debt.' Dilgen's fist clenches. 'Honor... and what does Horace know of such a word? He kills his own brothers! If a brother falls, we must [raise] him to his feet not bury him in the ground!'
You say, 'How do we raise them?'
Brother Dilgen says 'We often find sufficient force can knock a brother's senses back into him. But right now I don't have enough weapons to arm the brothers you save. They were taken in the middle of the night, and most will be defenseless. They'll need a way to [fight back] and defend against any more attempts the sirens might make to enthrall them.'
You say, 'Fight back?'
Brother Dilgen says 'The undead that wander the caverns won't miss their weapons, and the hides of crocolisks will provide protection. Can you [obtain] these?'
You say, 'Yes... I'll obtain them...'
Brother Dilgen says 'Good. There may still be hope for my brothers!'
You have been assigned the task 'Leave No Brother Behind'.
Brother Dilgen wants you to collect supplies and help save his brothers from the sirens.

---

!

---

Loot swords for the brothers from the skeletons in the caverns 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
"Salty Rusted Sword" can drop from any regular skeleton or floating skeleton mob (very common or 100% drop rate).
Give the swords to Brother Dilgen 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Brother Dilgen says 'My brothers will gladly wield these once their minds have been freed.'
Take hardened hides from the crocolisks 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Crocolisks are found in the same cavern as Brother Dilgen as well as the above-water area to the east. They tend to hit for a max ~17,000 and are mezzable.
Give the hardened hides to Brother Dilgen 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Brother Dilgen flexes the tough hides. They barely bend. 'These will do. They won't be proper helmets, but they'll suffice.'

---

Speak with Brother Dilgen 0/2 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Hail him until the task updates.
Brother Dilgen hands you a set of crudely constructed helms that resemble the helmets worn by the brothers. 'These aren't up to the quality standards of our everyday chastity helmets, but we have no other choices. You might notice the lack of visor. Tell the brothers to watch their feet.'
Brother Dilgen says 'We're still missing Brother Stanlop, Brother Vek`nal, Brother Jurgum, and Brother Strapp. Knock some sense into them and make them put on the helmets.'
You receive a Chastity Helmet Set [item=119525].

---

Weaken Brother Stanlop and place the helmet on him 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Weaken Brother Vek`Nal and place the helmet on him 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Weaken Brother Jurgum and place the helmet on him 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Weaken Brother Strapp and place the helmet on him 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
These steps are all open at once. Three of the Brothers are in the cavern to the west, and another is in the cavern to the north. Each Brother is leashed to within about 100' range of their spawn point, hits for a max ~12,000, and is immune to mez. Each has 1-2 sirens next to him (hit for a max ~6,000; immune to mez; sirens are NOT leashed). All three mobs are aggro-linked.
a brother's possessor is mezzable
Killing the sirens is optional (they respawn rather fast and have a lot of hitpoints). DPS the Brother to under 50% health, and then click the helmet on them (if you're in a group, this updates everyone who's on this step):
Brother Stanlop covers his eyes and ears. 'Must not look! Must not hear!' He runs for the cavern entrance without looking.
NOTE: If you need these task updates, you must be on the Brother's hate list or else it won't update for you.

---

Speak with Brother Dilgen 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
You say, 'Hail, Brother Dilgen'
Brother Dilgen says 'The brothers are safe, and Quigley will be pleased. But what of the next attack that will surely come, and the one after that? I have been thinking about this Queen Silisia of whom you spoke. She must be stopped. Brother Visius has some... interesting ideas...'
Reward(s):
Experience
75 platinum
This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request.

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

_This is a solo mission that begins with Brother Visius ( **click for map image**) in the cave just north of the zone-in._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Say "bring" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Brother Visius'
Brother Visius's laughter rings high and light, like chimes. 'What lies under my robes, my skin, my voice? Male or female? Does it [matter] to you?'
You say, 'It does matter...'
Brother Visius smiles. 'Oh, really? Examine me, and you'd say I was neither. But isn't it all an illusion anyway, a [game] dreamed up by Brothers and Sisters?' As you look at Visius' face, it seems to shift from feminine to masculine and back again.
You say, 'A game?'
Brother Visius says 'At which do you play? Oh, it does not matter now, does it?' Visius touches your cheek. 'Strength and vulnerability, hate and love -- I see them all in your [vessel].''
You say, 'My vessel?'
Brother Visius laughs again. 'A spirit so attached to your fleshy garment! And I suppose you think the clothes make the spirit?' Visius' visage blurs once more. 'So few of you bother to change your [clothes]. No wonder the world reeks!' Laughter flows from Visius' mouth.
You say, 'What clothes?'
Brother Visius says 'Yes, yes, appropriate dress for appropriate times. You would look lovely in [blue scales].'
You say, 'Blue scales?'
Brother Visius says 'The dress of the caverns, of course. You might call me a connoisseur of clothing. The garments I weave are worn under the [garments].' Visius laughs.'
You say, 'What garments?'
Brother Visius says 'Only the finest materials will do for you, my dear! Some possess the beauty of voice; others possess the beauty of form. But jealousy allows no other beauty. Form was exiled beneath the waters; voice remained above but grew silent. For you we must have [both].'
You say, 'Both?'
Brother Visius says 'The skin of the swimmers, the vocal chords of the singers: [Bring] them to me, and I shall confuse your dichotomy.'
You say, 'I'll bring them to you'
Brother Visius says 'You are a dear, aren't you?'
You have been assigned the task 'Two Sides of the Shield'.
Brother Visius can help you take on the aspects of the sirens. Then you might be able to get closer to rescuing Dabner.

---

!

---

Take the vocal cords of sirens 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Take the skin from sirens 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Head to the east cavern and kill sirens until you've looted 4x "Siren's Vocal Cords" and 4x "Siren's Skin"._

---

Give the vocal cords to Brother Visius (Caverns of Endless Song)
Give the skins to Brother Visius (Caverns of Endless Song)
Speak with Brother Visius 0/3 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Brother Visius strokes the skin and vocal chords. 'Of course, you seek an audience with the queen, wearing your new and beautiful raiment? And who is not touched by beauty? It draws or repels, and often enthralls.
Brother Visius touches the siren skin and vocal chords to your body. You feel changed.
You feel different.
Brother Visius touches your face. 'It suits you well. Go and be with the sirens. Speak of their queen and know her. When the time comes for an audience, she shall be yours.'
_You've now taken the appearance of a siren, and will /con warmly to any sirens and pirates in the zone._

---

Speak with sirens while in disguise to find out more about Silisia 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Hail five different siren mobs._
a silenced enchantress says 'Another one! Sisters, the queen has taken another!' The siren touches your shoulder. 'Raise not your voice, my sister. Though your song has ended, let not your words anger the queen.'
a silenced witch says 'Be humble and do as the queen commands. Serve her well, and in time she may share her thralls with you.'
a silenced enchantress frowns. 'Aren't you a pretty thing? Don't show your face around me again!'
a silenced siren says 'We have all searched for Silisia's lost lover, but we have not found her. If Silisia finds any of us have hidden her... I won't even imagine.'
a silenced evoker says 'Have you presented yourself to Nilsara? All young things like you must go before Nilsara. She will judge your worth to Silisia.'

---

Speak with Nilsara while in disguise 0/10 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Nilsara is found at the southern boundary of the main siren caverns (above the crocolisk/spineback tunnels that open up into it). Hail her until the task updates._
Nilsara looks upon you warmly -- it appears to be quite formidable.
Nilsara wraps her arms around you. 'I welcome you in the embrace of Silisia, queen and mistress. I trust our sisters treated you gently and brought you here unharmed? True, your song is no longer your own, given to Silisia as are you. As are we all.
Nilsara says 'Do you know why you are here? The story changes depending on the teller, but this is how I know it:'
Nilsara says 'A young siren sang in a voice so beautiful it reached the ear of an adventurer sailing upon the sea. The adventurer abandoned her quest and swam to the arms of the siren.'
Nilsara says 'That the feminine could hear the song -- this surprised the siren. She thought it was love, not song, that bound the adventurer to her. But as you must know, love is not a gift given to sirens.'
Nilsara says 'The siren fled to this grotto, escaping the disapproval of our kind. She ceased her song, trusting in love to keep the adventurer by her side. But love failed.'
Nilsara says 'The adventurer recovered her wits and escaped, taking the siren's heart with her. And so the siren began to sing again.'
Nilsara says 'She sang for her lost love, sang that the adventurer might return. But love did not return. The siren grew jealous: Had the adventurer been ensnared by another?'
Nilsara says 'Others might be more beautiful; others might be more sonorous. The siren could not stand it. The songs had to be hers. She would sing a chorus that would not fail to win back her love. She would sing until the sea dried up and there was no one left to hear. '
Nilsara says 'And so we are here... or so I have heard. Now, you may not be able to sing anymore, but I can plainly see you still possess your beauty. Silisia will not be pleased. '
Nilsara says 'Present yourself before our queen in the throne room above, but be prepared for her displeasure.'

---

Speak with Brother Visius 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_This will open up an instanced zone for you. Say "go" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Brother Visius'
Brother Visius says 'The throne above, the seat of love or pedestal of despair? Ascend, my dear one. [Go]. Find what you seek and release us from our burden.'
You say, 'Let's go...'
Brother Visius nods silently.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

Follow the song to the throne room above 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_This step updates upon zoning in._

---

Speak with Dagda 0/7 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Head up the spiral ramp in front of you at the zone-in, where you'll find Dagda Icefury along with 10 scowling thralls (not aggro yet, however)._
Dagda Icefury halts her sword mid swing. She spits blood. Her wounded leg is swollen and oozing. '\_\_\_\_\_? I know the voice but not the face. So you came hidden...'
Dagda Icefury says 'I can't hide these wounds. The leg festers.' Dagda laughs and spits again. 'In Halas I prayed for battle, prayed for a true contest for my life, prayed for death on my feet, with a sword in my hand. Sometimes you must answer your own prayers.'
Dagda Icefury struggles to her feet, using the stolen sword to steady herself. She yells out, 'Siren queen, you stole my sword and shield; you stole my friend; and you try to steal my life! Cease your song and show yourself that I might steal your tongue and then your head!'
Silisia's song pulsing through the cavern coalesces into mocking laughter. 'Seeking lost love? Searching in desperation? Have I taken something that is yours? Such cries are sweet music...'
Silisia shouts, 'I too have lost love. Are you here to return it to me? No, I see the hatred in your eyes. You would take what you think is yours...'
Silisia shouts, 'But all is now mine. In each of my thralls I can see a glimmer of her face, the light of the one who will yet return...'
Silisia shouts, 'You will leave with nothing, least of all your life. My new sirens, I see you there. Aid my thralls and remove this temptress from my sight!'
_Three of the thralls attack Dagda Icefury._
Silisia shouts, 'What are you doing, sirens?! You are a silent sister; obey me or die with the barbarian!'

---

Defend Dagda from the thralls 0/3 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Take all the thralls on at once if you want a bit of a challenge, otherwise you can stand back and Dagda Icefury will keep them tanked (locks at 10%). These mobs are mezzable and of average difficulty for the zone, albeit with a lot of hitpoints._

---

Defeat the next wave of thralls 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Repeat as above._

---

Defeat the final wave of thralls 0/3 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Repeat as above._
Dagda Icefury thrusts the rusty sword through the gut of a thrall. As she twists it, the blade snaps in half. Before she can turn, steel slashes through the frayed armor covering her back.
_Upon the death of the last one, an irritated Siren Queen makes herself known:_
Silisia's form flickers into view atop the throne. 'Sirens, cease your combat and approach! How dare you kill my thralls?!'

---

Speak with Silisia 0/4 (Caverns of Endless Song)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Silisia says 'You try to outdo my beauty, you kill my thralls, and you aid this barbarian? I will show no mercy.' Silisia laughs. 'She searches for her lover? Then I will let love destroy her! Come, little one!''
_The chest next to her throne breaks open..._
Dabner Drednever breaks through the chest and climbs out of its remains.
Silisia says 'The little one kept asking where Dagda was. Oh, he certainly could describe her in detail! But by now he will never recognize her. Dabner, show them how much you love your mistress. Kill the barbarian!'
Silisia's laughter rings as she disappears. Dabner lunges at Dagda.

---

Defeat Dabner 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Dabner attacks. He is permanently rooted, hits for a max ~15,000, has a lot of hitpoints, and AEs "Burning Light" (26,000 dmg fully mitigated)._
_DPS him to 10% health (requires ~5.5 million damage) for the update._

---

See what happens between Dabner and Dagda 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Dagda Icefury's skin drips with blood and perspiration. Dabner's mouth glistens with saliva. Barely able to stand, Dagda pushes him back. 'Don't you remember... your love? If you won't stop for anything else, remember who you truly loved...'
Dagda Icefury begins to sing, 'The only scrap to float was love,'
Dagda Icefury sings, 'And this is what it read:'
Dagda Icefury sings, 'Be light, my dove, and do take heed:'
Dagda Icefury sings, 'To curse the seas there is no need.'
Dagda Icefury sings, 'From heavy armor do be freed'
Dagda Icefury sings, 'And take the cloth instead.'
Dabner Drednever's attacks slow. Confusion spreads across his face. He shakes his head. 'But Dagda... would never come...' Dabner raises his sword for another attack.
Dagda Icefury can hold out no longer. Swinging the broken sword, she cuts the strap holding the crude mail against Dabner's chest. 'I dove into the sea... for you...' Dagda plunges the blade into Dabner's shoulder.
Dabner Drednever lowers his weapon. He looks down at the sword sticking out of his shoulder and the blood trailing down his arm and chest. He sees Dagda on the ground. 'Dagda... I've been stabbed...'

---

Speak with Dabner 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Hail him to complete the task. Note: This mission does NOT spawn a loot chest. This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Dabner Drednever pulls out the blade, sending another gout of blood running down his chest. He ignores his pain, seeing Dagda on the ground breathing hard, wounds covering her body -- wounds he inflicted. 'Dagda... why did you come? Why didn't you just let me go? It would have been better than this...'
Dagda Icefury coughs but manages a laugh. 'What choice did I have? Who wouldn't try to save a friend, a brother? I said before it's best to die with a sword in hand, on your feet. Maybe I was wrong...'
Dabner Drednever kneels beside Dagda and touches her shoulder. He pauses a moment. 'Yes... brother... and I'll do all I can as a friend, brother, and healer. You will not be dying under my care, Dagda.' Dabner begins casting his healing spells on Dagda.
Dagda Icefury looks at you and shakes her head. 'I'm in no shape to travel with you to meet Firiona...'
Dabner Drednever says 'You say Quigley will let us sail if you saved his brothers? You must find Thubr. I must return to Brother Island and heal Dagda. He took the portal on the island -- to where, I do not know. Quigley Barnables on Brother Island will know. Perhaps by the time you have found Thubr, Dagda will be well enough to travel.'
_Rewards:_
150 platinum
Experience

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Thubr Axebringer ( **click for map image**) in the western wing of Degmar._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Say "priestess" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer keeps his eyes closed as he waves you away. 'Let me 'lone. I shouldna been there...' Thubr's breath smells of ale. 'Failed [again]...'
You say, 'Again?!'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Only so much can ya take... only so much can ya do...' Thubr's red eyes open. '[Dagda] sends you?''
You say, 'Yes, Dagda sent me...'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Tears trickle down Thubr's cheeks as you tell him what happened in the caverns. Thubr slams his fist into the stone. 'Just like the elves! Couldna stopped [it]!''
You say, 'It?'
Thubr Axebringer says 'We killed 'em all... all the elves. Dwarves and elves, brothers and sisters, all of them... shoulda cared more. How do ya get [forgiveness] for that, \_\_\_\_\_?! What'll [Firiona] say ta that, eh?!''
You say, 'Firiona?'
Thubr Axebringer says 'No, she can't see me yet!' Thubr covers his face. 'The elves, the shame! Gotta make it right! Gotta help the [priestess]!''
You say, 'How do you get forgiveness?'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Gotta atone, get atonement... make it right. A funeral. Everyone gets a funeral. Everyone comes. As it shoulda been. Make 'em understand. Show some [respect].'
You say, 'Respect?'
Thubr Axebringer waves his hand at Priestess Glihelin. 'She knows. You ask her. She knows I'm gonna show 'em respect, make the dwarves understand. Tell 'em, [Priestess]...' Thubr lies back and closes his eyes.
You say, 'The priestess...'
Thubr Axebringer mumbles, 'Yes, help the priestess...'
You have been assigned the task 'The Sacred Profaned'.
Thubr wants to perform a ceremony, and he needs your help to collect several sacred objects that have been lost or scattered in the war between dwarves and elves.

---

!

---

Speak with Priestess Glihelin 0/9 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_She shouldn't be too hard to find... Hail her until the task updates._
Priestess Glihelin looks sadly upon Thubr. 'His spirit longs to atone for something. He has chosen to begin his penance here.
Priestess Glihelin says 'Perhaps you have spoken with others: Damton, Ranpodia, Neckchi. Maybe you know the tragic history of this place: how elves and dwarves once lived peacefully together under the waves. We worshipped together until the madness came upon the dwarves. My people were killed. I am the last elf.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Thubr heard the tale. He has not stopped talking about it, raving about how the dwarves should have cared for the elves, saying how he will show them what they have done.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'When first he spoke with me, he was calm, though a bit unsteady on his feet. As the days passed, he spent more and more time in the brew hall stealing drinks. He'd stamp around this chamber for hours, talking about his plans to make everyone show respect for my murdered people before passing out curled around Tunare's statue.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'This is my home and has been for hundreds of years. Though my people are gone, I cannot leave. I would reconcile myself to the dwarves if I could, but they have not listened to reason. I thought Thubr might sway them...'
Priestess Glihelin says 'But he frightens me sometimes -- the anger in his eyes as he talks about what happened in Degmar, the sudden changes in the volume of his voice. He has told me he will not let me leave here, even to move about the castle, until the dwarves understand.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'One night he told me of his life before he came to Degmar. He was called the Alebringer. He haunted the taverns of Kaladim, doing whatever he had to for the next drink. He said that when Firiona came to him, he thought she was a ghost, much like the ones that roam these halls. But though he set sail for adventure, he is still haunted.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'I told him of the book from which we once read when we held worship services for Brell and Tunare together. He wants a copy of this book. If we can help him perform his ceremony, his spirit may find the release it could not find at the bottom of a cup.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'We kept a copy of the book, The Sacred Rites of Forge and Forest, in the castle's library.'

---

Pick up the sacred book of Tunare and Brell in the library rotunda 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_"Sacred Rites of Forge and Forest" is a ground spawn in the room that extends northeast from the library. "Dead Apostle" spawns when you touch the book (undead mob; not auto-aggro, but will obviously aggro you if it can see you). Take out the wandering dwarf before you spawn the Apostle. Spectres won't assist unless you hit them._
A restless elven spirit appears behind you, angered that you have touched the sacred book.

---

Defeat the Dead Apostle you angered by touching the sacred book 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Nothing special here; just a lot of hitpoints._
Dead Apostle has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
You open The Sacred Rites of Forge and Forest and read a few lines: ... the ground and its flora: these two are inseparable. From both we are blessed with life and livelihoods. For these blessings we give thanks to Brell and Tunare, the Father and Mother. We, their children, honor our parents. As brothers and sisters, dwarves and elves sing praises with one voice...

---

Give the sacred book to Thubr 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Speak with Thubr 0/3 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Thubr Axebringer clutches the book to his chest. 'Did ya read it?! Did ya see what it said?! Don't ya know?!' Thubr lowers his voice. 'The elves and me and the dwarves, we was brothers, sisters.' Thubr raises his voice. 'But we smashed 'em!' Thubr smacks the stone.
Thubr Axebringer says 'They just want to live and laugh and drink... now they're DEAD! Do you know what that means?! Nothing! To them it means nothing!'
Thubr Axebringer wipes his mouth. 'Look at her... poor Tunare... they break you here; they break you with Brell... it's all broken...' Thubr grows silent.

---

Speak with Priestess Glihelin 0/4 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail her until the task updates._
Priestess Glihelin points at the broken statue of Tunare. 'We had a smaller one in Brell's temple that we used during our dual ceremonies. After the mob of dwarves swarmed this temple and destroyed this statue, they stormed into Brell's temple and smashed the smaller statue, saying it had defiled the home of Brell.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'The dwarves spread the pieces of the statue throughout Degmar. I know one piece was thrown into the entry hall moat. Another was tossed into the prison. The other, I believe, Commander Degmar kept for himself as a reminder of supposed elven treachery.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Thubr has talked of restoring the statue. He talks much; he drinks more.'
Priestess Glihelin says 'If you find the pieces, fire the statue in a kiln. Smiths and artisans will surely have clay.'

---

Pick up the legs of the statue of Tunare in the moat 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Pick up the torso of the statue of Tunare in Degmar's bedroom 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Pick up the head of the statue of Tunare in the armory 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_The items you're looking for are "Statue of Tunare Legs", "Statue of Tunare Torso", and "Statue of Tunare Head" - all ground spawns. The legs are found in the room just south of the zone-in, underwater. The torso is found in the bedroom (behind the Sub Commander's / Commander's throne room). And the head is found in the armory (down the hall west of the throne room)._

---

Find clay in the possession of forge artisans 0/6 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_"Degmar Artisinal Clay" seems to be a 100% drop (two at a time) from "artisan" and "smith" mobs in the workshop. There are several smiths and artisans around the edge of room who can be soloed in their spot, no need to pull and get unwanted assists._

---

Repair the statue of Tunare in a kiln - combine the 3 statue pieces and 6 clay pieces 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_There is a Pottery Kiln just inside the workshop, to the left. Combine these nine items to produce a "Repaired Tunare Statue"._

---

Give the repaired statue of Tunare to Thubr 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Speak with Thubr 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Thubr Axebringer strokes the face of the statue. 'Unbroken... ya put it back together... I never coulda done it... tried so long... they must see!'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Ya can't put the dead back together! Gotta see! Gotta remember!' Thubr taps the statue's forehead. 'See the face in yer mind! Know what ya did! Show 'em the dead!''

---

Find an elf skeleton in the prison cells 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Kill the corpse desecrators 0/4 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Kill Jailor Muggroff 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_These five mobs are located in the prison (southwestern part of the zone), are in close-quarters with each other, and have a fast respawn timer (100 seconds). Nothing is leashed or aggro-linked. The desecrators are mezzable; Jailor Muggroff is not. All five mobs are of average difficulty for the zone, except the Jailor himself has a lot of hitpoints. Stand in empty cell to east of Jailor cell. Jailor and desecrators are all non social, so you can pet pull singly to side cell. I couldn't get them to come in cell but if you shuffle about you can nuke them through the bars or just assist pet with additional damage shields. Don't stand in cell door to get line of sight or the dwarves will all pile in and kill you before you can say "Click my Bulwark"._
Jailor Muggroff says 'What's it ta you what we do with a pile o' dead trash?! Me own brother was killed by elves. Me father was blamed and executed in this very prison! Yer damn right I helped slaughter them devils, and every year when I honor me father and brother by striking this trash, I wish I had killed even more!'

---

Inspect and retrieve the skeleton of the elf 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_/inspect the dead elf to update this step (mobs can be alive for this step and you can do it while invis - you just need to have completed the previous kill updates)._
You retrieve the skeleton of the elf.
_You pick up "Elf Skeleton"._

---

Give the elf skeleton to Thubr 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Speak with Thubr 0/6 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Thubr Axebringer closes his eyes and won't look at the body. 'No, I can't. Not yet. We shall all see... I'm so sorry...'
Priestess Glihelin reaches her hand out for Thubr. 'Hush. It was not your fault. The dwarves of the castle did this to us. You cannot bring the elves back.
Thubr Axebringer shouts, 'You must pay the dead their proper respects!'
Priestess Glihelin says 'And what will you do for the living?'
Thubr Axebringer shouts, 'I will make 'em pay! We must pay for what we did!'
Thubr Axebringer beckons you closer. '\_\_\_\_\_, \_\_\_\_\_, almost time... time to [show] 'em...'

---

---

_This solo task begins with Thubr Axebringer ( **click for map image**) in the western wing of Degmar._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
**\- Completion of "Castle Relic" (non-progression Degmar Heroic Adventure)**
_Say "make" to request the task._
_Upon completion of the previous task, you saw:_
Thubr Axebringer beckons you closer. '\_\_\_\_\_, \_\_\_\_\_, almost time... time to [show] 'em...'
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer keeps his eyes closed as he waves you away. 'Let me 'lone. I shouldna been there...' Thubr's breath smells of ale. 'Failed [again]... Will [show] them...'
You say, 'Show them?'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Yes, we'll... gather 'em. Come together in worship... ask forgiveness. Unused scrolls, glue, and blood... invite 'em. [Make] 'em look.'
You say, 'I'll make 'em look...'
Thubr Axebringer closes his eyes and hits himself in the forehead. 'Make 'em remember...'
You have been assigned the task 'Crying for the Dead'.
Thubr wants you to proclaim the ceremony to the dwarves. To do so, you must gather the items to create announcements and then place the announcements around Degmar.

---

!

---

Take a blank scroll from a librarian 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Take a quill from the lost scribe 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Take an inkwell from the lost scribe 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Take drops of blood from the dwarves and combine them in the inkwell 0/10 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Pick up some sticky mud in the library 0/5 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Create announcements. Combine the full inkwell, sticky mud, and quill in the scroll 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Any "librarian" (undead or live) can drop a "Blank Degmar Scroll"._
_The "Lost Scribe" is an undead mob found on the upper level of the library's rotunda. It's social with the other undead; is immune to root/snare; and has a lot of hitpoints. It drops "Degmar Inkwell" and "Degmar Quill"._
_Any Dwarf mob (except, apparently, the librarians) can drop "Degmar Dwarf Blood" if you're on this task._
_"Sticky Mud" is a ground spawn on the floor scattered in the library. It is prelootable._
_Once you have all the components in hand:_
\- Inside a "Degmar Inkwell", combine 10x "Degmar Dwarf Blood" to produce 1x "Full Degmar Inkwell"
\- Inside a "Blank Degmar Scroll", combine: 5x "Sticky Mud" + 1x "Full Degmar Inkwell" + 1x "Degmar Quill" to produce "Memorial Ceremony Announcement"

---

Place a memorial announcement in the throne room and defeat opposition 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Place a memorial announcement in the armory and defeat opposition 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Place a memorial announcement in the brewery and defeat opposition 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Place a memorial announcement in the forge and defeat opposition 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Place a memorial announcement in the prison and defeat opposition 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Head to the above areas and right-click the announcement. There doesn't appear to be specific location requirements other than being inside the room (other than not having any guards too close), so you can be choosy if you want. Doing so spawns 1x "a \_\_\_\_\_ guard", a fairly straightforward fight (mezzable; lots of hitpoints; Levels 105-106)._
You stick the announcement scroll to the wall.
_Throne Room:_ an angry guard
_Armory:_ an enraged guard
_Brewery:_ an outraged guard
_Forge:_ an incensed guard
_Prison:_ an offended guard

---

Speak with Thubr 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer says 'You'll see... now they'll come... We'll show 'em all at the [memorial]. We'll make 'em see. Ya can't forgive unless ya see...'
_Rewards:_
Experience
150 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

_This group mission begins with Thubr Axebringer ( **click for map image**) in the western wing of Degmar. As this takes place in an instanced zone, it is timed (six hours)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Say "memorial" to request the task and "go" to zone in._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
NPC behaviour update (2015):
an antendee - mez: yes, root: no
a disgusted attendee: mez: yes, root: no
a dolorous attendee: mez: no, root: yes
an angry attendee: mez: yes, root: yes
Thubr Axebringer says 'You'll see... now they'll come... We'll show 'em all at the [memorial]. We'll make 'em see. Ya can't forgive unless ya see...'
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer turns restlessly on the stone pedestal. 'Ya cannot rest without forgiveness... ya can't forgive unless ya see... the [memorial]... we can rest...'
You say, 'A memorial?'
Thubr Axebringer says '[Go]! You come too, priestess! You must see!'
You have been assigned the task 'Burden of the Past'.
The time has come for Thubr to perform his ceremony. Will the dwarves of Degmar listen and understand?
You say, 'Let's go...'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Yes...'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

Speak with Thubr in the Sanctuary 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer is breathing heavily, hands on his knees. 'They come. It's time for us all ta see... take 'em. You'll know when it's time ta lay down our sins.'

---

Speak with Thubr and hear the commencement of the memorial 0/7 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task updates. You can be sure the dwarves in attendance will love it._
Thubr Axebringer raises his raw voice. 'Come! The blood runs down our hands! Lay 'em before Brell! Let our tears wash 'em clean!
Thubr Axebringer waves the The Sacred Rites of Forge and Forest. 'It be a great sin above our heads! In the book... dwarves and elves together, as it should be!'
Thubr Axebringer opens the book but doesn't look at it. 'See what it says?! Brothers and sisters! But where are they?! Where are yer brothers and sisters?!'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'The pews... too empty... too many missing. Why're you here?! Because they're not!'
Thubr Axebringer breathes heavily again, his words sticking in his mouth as he tries to read from the book. 'Brell and Tunare, hand-in-hand... walk the caves and copses...'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'QUIET!' Thubr slams shut the book as he hears a dwarf fidget in a pew. 'This is for you, for us! SHOW RESPECT!'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'Guilty, all o' you! Rage, weakness, unfaithfulness... You let go o' Tunare's hand! Then ya cut off her hand! And then her head!' Thubr throws the book to the ground. 'You are guilty of MURDER!'

---

Walk near Thubr to lay down the corpse of the elf 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Now you have a dead elf on hand._
Tell Thubr to continue 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Thubr Axebringer'
Thubr Axebringer's cheeks glisten. His voice fades to reverence. 'See what we did... corpse o' the elves... corpse o' their memories... corpse of our dignity...'
an attendee shouts 'The long-ear deserved it!'
a disgusted attendee shouts 'What about my family?! They deserve an apology from the long-ears!'
_Four of the dwarves in attendance attack Thubr Axebringer._

---

Defeat the angry dwarves 0/4 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Kill the four dwarves for the update. Thubr Axebringer will tank them, so you can pull them one by one if needed instead of taking all four on at once. They are of average difficulty for Degmar, are mezzable and rootable/snareable. A few of them may have a lot of hitpoints._
_NOTE: These mobs (and all mobs in this task) are leashed to the Sanctuary._

---

Defeat the next group of angry dwarves 0/5 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Same as above, only five this time._

---

Tell Thubr to continue 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'See what ya bring without forgiveness?! More death!'
Thubr Axebringer pounds the floor. 'Yer all hollow as an empty mug! Who will make amends?! Remember when we was once full...'
an angry attendee shouts 'Remember when me father was alive?! Remember how his screams turned ta gurgles when the long-ears slit his throat?! I'm proud o' the number I killed!'

---

Defeat another group of angry dwarves 0/4 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Repeat as above._

---

Tell Thubr to continue 0/3 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'Brothers, stop! Don't throw yer lives away! Just confess!' Thubr covers his mouth as through holding something back. 'Just... confess...'
Priestess Glihelin yells at Thubr, the concern visible on her face. 'End this madness before everyone is killed! What is done cannot be changed! There are some things for which you cannot atone!'
Thubr Axebringer coughs as though trying to dislodge something from the back of his throat. 'Why won't they listen? Why won't they confess? Let Tunare see the truth...'

---

Walk in front of Thubr to place the statue of Tunare 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Tell Thubr to continue 0/15 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task updates._
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'You broke the statue and the covenant! Now look again! Look at Tunare's face!' Thubr points with his whole hand at the statue of Tunare. 'Who will confess?! Who will REPENT?!''
Thubr Axebringer does not say a word. The temple is silent except for Thubr's anxious breathing.
Thubr Axebringer opens his eyes. 'No one?' he says to the floor.
Priestess Glihelin calls out gently but with a quaver in her voice. 'Thubr, what do you want to confess? What do you want to tell Tunare?'
Thubr Axebringer's breaths begin to sound more like sobs. 'I told 'er, though she weren't there... we said goodbye ta Firiona... I touched 'er gravestone and made a promise: I'll protect her people.'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'But I was a LIAR! Drunk that night, and the next, and the next! Nobody needed me axe; nobody needed me protection! Nobody needed a drunk!'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'The name was 'Alebringer' in Kaladim! Oh, there's Alebringer begging for coppers in the streets, how sad! Spare another pint for pity! 'Twas always pity or laughter!'
Thubr Axebringer's eyes swell as the tears roll. 'They laughed; I drank -- drank 'til the laughter was faraway thunder. The night was so dark. Not enough lamps. I stumbled.'
Thubr Axebringer finally looks upon the face of the dead elf. 'Couldn't see his face; couldn't see his hands. Just a shadow of a cloud caught in the streetlamp... then a glint... a flash of lightning ta strike me down?
Thubr Axebringer sobs. 'I used ta be Axebringer... and I brought it. The cloud vanished. The silver pieces rained on the cobblestones.'
Thubr Axebringer touches the chest of the skeleton. 'That elf... no one else ever tried ta give me silver.'
Thubr Axebringer says 'Thubr traces a line down the elf's skull from forehead to jaw. 'A valley full of dark rain... Nobody saw. Not enough lamps. He rests under the North Kaladim road, with his pack and silvers. Tunare never saw. Firiona never saw...''
Thubr Axebringer cries as he strokes the dead elf's face. 'I'm sorry... now ya know... Tunare knows... Firiona, I'm sorry... I didn't know. I didn't know... forgive me...'
Thubr Axebringer leans over suddenly and retches. Breathing heavily, he lies down and closes his eyes.
Priestess Glihelin looks nervously at the door of the room. 'Thubr, Tunare hears you... but we need to leave. The residents of Degmar do not share your heart. They do not feel the guilt you do. Let us leave before they add my blood to that which has already been spilled!'
_Captain Gurshock and five guards appear (non-aggro at this time)._

---

Speak with Captain Gurshock 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Upon hailing, the guards rush off to attack Priestess Glihelin while Captain Gurshock stays and fights. (The guards may attack you, instead, due to assist range if the Captain aggros you right away.)_
You say, 'Hail, Captain Gurshock'
Captain Gurshock Still alive and incitin' a riot, Priestess?! I'll do to ya what we shoulda done a long time ago, even before yer kind went crazy! You tried ta kill us! Ya even got Degmar, but ya can't get all of us! We'll get ya first!

---

Defeat Captain Gurshock and her guards 0/6 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Some of them AE rampage. Captain Gurshock hits for a max ~19,000 and casts "Double Axe" (proc for 32,500 dmg mitigated). If any guards are still alive while she's under attack, she summons them to her aid:_
Captain Gurshock says 'Defend your captain!'
_**The Captain has line-of-sight aggro**; the guards are of standard aggro. Guards are mezzable, but immune to root/snare. The Captain is immune to all crowd control. All of them see through all forms of invisibility and most have a lot of hitpoints. All of them 'corpse camp' (meaning they remain wherever they are pulled). Like all mobs in this task, they are leashed to the room in which they spawn (if a mob is leashed, it drops all aggro and warps back to its spawn point)._
_NOTE: Due to the Captain's line-of-sight aggro, if you fail the fight and have to zone back in, and she's within sight of the zone-in point, you will have her immediate attention upon zoning back in._

---

Speak with Priestess Glihelin 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Priestess Glihelin'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Aventurers, we should leave. Let Thubr alone. He has caused enough trouble already...'

---

Speak with Thubr 0/8 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Hail him until the task completes. A chest with loot also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Thubr Axebringer sits up, holding his head in his hands. 'Fine job, Alebringer... Priestess, I'm sorry. I just want ta make things right...'
Priestess Glihelin sighs. 'Then stop heaping your mistakes on top of each other. You have told your truth, but we are not the ones who need to hear it. Go with Firiona; she needs you.'
Thubr Axebringer says, 'But what about you? What about the dwarves? And Neckchi wanted help with his dome... and...'
Priestess Glihelin says, 'I told you: The dwarves do not share your heart. Look at the dead! No, there will be no workers for Katta Castrum; there will be nothing left of Degmar after insanity runs its course.'
Priestess Glihelin says, 'But I will remain here. It was my home, and it will likely be my grave. As I said, there are some things for which you cannot atone. Some things cannot be undone or made right.'
Priestess Glihelin says, 'You are not without hope. You are not of this place. Go where you were meant to go, and say what you were meant to say. Your life is not over, even though you ended one. Go.'
Thubr Axebringer wipes his mouth and nose on his sleeve. 'I'll go with Firiona... after one more sleep. Adventurers, speak with Quigley on Brother Island, and find Lyirae and Galeth. They took the ghost ship from the island... Priestess, I will come back for you...'
Priestess Glihelin says, 'Don't forget the last time you made a promise to an elf.'
Priestess Glihelin is visibly shivering. 'I shouldn't be here... We should [leave]... The dwarves are numerous, and I'm defenseless. Thubr is still drunk. He dragged me out of the temple, saying I had to see this, that this was a memorial for my people. I do not want it to become my actual memorial...'

_This group mission begins with Galeth Veredeth ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
_Say "stop" to request the mission and "proceed" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Galeth Veredeth'
Galeth Veredeth says 'The [search] has taught us much. Now it is the [moment] to [stop] the enemy.'
You say, 'The moment?'
Galeth Veredeth watches a stick insect slowly crawling across his metal hand. 'Time moves so swiftly here... I forgot the urgency of the mortal. When I see the rain falling and the creatures creeping, or I hear the clicks of my clockwork gears, they all rush [together].
You say, 'Together?'
Galeth Veredeth says 'We spirits gather here for but a short time. The ones who have known nothing but the mortal cannot see how swiftly it travels, and they fear the vastness of time beyond their flesh. Lyirae feels this fear. She wishes to fight alone, thinking to [protect] me from the vastness.'
You say, 'Protect you?'
Galeth Veredeth says 'Each of us will pass from the river to the sea. Some quibble over time, but all mortals reach it in a blink. Lyirae should not protect me. She should look to the stream. Protect the flow, the current upon which all time is carried.' Galeth touches a leaf and allows the stick insect to crawl from his hand. 'Time is both infinite and short. Let us [stop] the enemy.'
You say, 'We'll stop them...'
Galeth Veredeth says 'The enemy forces amass on the beach. Any moment they will march into the jungle. \_\_\_\_\_, Lyirae fears the inevitable: the abandonment of flesh and the infinity of spirit. Let us give her no cause to fear for us. [Proceed] now. We will face them together.'
You have been assigned the task 'Fit for the New World'.
The Combine are trying to capture a large creature on the island. They are about to send reinforcements, and Galeth thinks this is the time to stop them.
You say, 'Let's proceed'
Galeth Veredeth says 'Indeed.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Thuliasaur Island.

---

!

---

Speak with Galeth 0/3 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Hail him three times for the update, and then watch out because a group of mobs makes its way towards the area from the south (they do not see invisibility)._
Galeth Veredeth says 'Lyirae could not wait. She told me to stay behind. She has followed a line of reinforcements pushing into the interior. Still my joints constrain me, and I could not keep up with her pace.'
Galeth Veredeth says 'Lyirae thought I would be easy prey with my current capabilities of locomotion.' Galeth wheezes through his steam vents, which sounds almost like laughter. 'She is kind for worrying about the fate of a spirit inside a metal body.''
Galeth Veredeth says 'She would have cause for sadness if we did not move south. Other groups of reinforcements have landed on the shore and begin to push north. We must cut them off.'

---

Defeat the landing party marching northward 0/13 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Average difficulty mobs, but have a fair amount of hitpoints and are fairly close together. They are mezzable and rootable/snareable. If you aggro and fade "a squad leader" after having killed a few troops, they may respawn. This doesn't matter, though, as any 13 of these mobs will update and progress the task._
_Upon killing the last one, you see:_
Galeth Veredeth says 'We should find Lyirae. The soldiers are not the only ones deserving of reinforcements. She followed them to the northwest.'

---

Speak with Lyirae on the southern hill facing the western river branch 0/4 (Thuliasaur Island)
_She is found along the western boundary of the raptor area (no raptors in the instanced version of the zone, but it's in the same area)._
Lyirae Oakwynd shouts 'What are you doing here, Galeth? The rain -- you'll grind to a halt and get yourself killed!'
Galeth Veredeth shouts 'You forget I am a spirit of Growth. I cannot die like you imagine. This metal body...'
Lyirae Oakwynd fires another arrow through the rain, keeping the Combine squad pinned down. 'You're here now. I can see you, touch you, hear you. If you were far away and I could not see your fate, perhaps it would be different. But nothing will happen to you under my watch!'
Galeth Veredeth readies his sword. 'I could say the same. I will not let your life spill into the sea just yet. Keep them occupied.' Galeth rushes toward the Combine squad.
Lyirae Oakwynd shouts 'Galeth! Adventurers, help him!'
Galeth Veredeth's arm slows as he swings his sword. His joints grind and freeze as he turns to parry a strike.
Defeat the Combine squad 0/10 (Thuliasaur Island)
_These mobs are of the same variety as previous._
Speak with Lyirae 0/3 (Thuliasaur Island)
Lyirae Oakwynd looks ruefully at Galeth. 'I told you this would happen...'
Galeth Veredeth manages to lower his arm a few inches. 'I should have a word with Ognit about the quality of his craftsmanship...'
Lyirae Oakwynd pours a small flask of plant oil onto Galeth's joints and tries to loosen them.
Speak with Galeth 0/3 (Thuliasaur Island)
Galeth Veredeth's arms and legs start to move more freely. 'I can keep going, but I don't know how much longer my body will be useful in this rain.'
Lyirae Oakwynd yells, 'Galeth, stay here! I don't want to see you destroyed today!' Lyirae runs further into the forest.
Galeth Veredeth says, 'Though my body slows I shall not abandon her.'

---

Move west, up the river to Lyirae 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Lyirae Oakwynd frowns as she hears the clanking of Galeth's approach. 'He is going to get himself killed...'
Lyirae Oakwynd points to the group of nearby Combine. 'Do you see them slaughtering the creatures? What are they doing? Those creatures have done nothing... we must stop this!'
Defeat the Combine squad 0/11 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Same as above. Upon the last kill, you see:_
Galeth Veredeth points westward down the river. 'This is just the bait. There are older, larger things that lurk in the sea.'

---

Reach the clearing at the end of the river to the west 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Speak with Galeth 0/10 (Thuliasaur Island)
Galeth Veredeth raises his hand to you. 'Hold and listen.'
Praetor Venatos shouts 'The stench of these creatures is enough to lure every fly from Odus! Una better arrive soon or she'll be late to supper!'
Una Lucem's eyes wander around the forest, adjusting to the dim light. She does not blink as the rain strikes her pupils. Her gaze comes to rest on the mound of corpses. 'Looks like the courtyard after training. But these have all their pieces.'
Una Lucem stares at Venatos. He looks at the ground. 'Where's the giant? Father said to fetch a giant.'
Galeth Veredeth keeps looking at the ground. 'It's sure to come soon... we killed so many... and the stench will surely attract the beast...'
Una Lucem cocks her head, still looking at Venatos. 'You didn't finish setting the plate.' With a swing of Una's sword, a shockwave fans out and cleaves the skin of the remaining dinosaurs.
Lyirae Oakwynd whispers to you and Galeth. 'She's a monster, just like the others!' Lyirae nocks an arrow and looses it at Una.
Una Lucem , without more than a glance, flashes her sword like lightning amid the raindrops. Lyirae's arrow falls in twain. Una's eyes stare into the forest's gloom, marking the arrow's genesis.
Lyirae Oakwynd silently springs to action, darting through the undergrowth without a word, running at Una Lucem.
Galeth Veredeth shouts, 'Lyirae!' Galeth clangs after her.

---

Speak with Una Lucem 0/9 (Thuliasaur Island)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Upon the ninth hail, be ready for a fight._
Una Lucem emits a blast of magic, throwing Galeth and Lyirae to the ground. Galeth's frame and leg joints crack.
Una Lucem looks at you impassively. 'You came to see me again. You must admire me.'
Praetor Venatos glances around as the ground begins to shake. 'The creature is coming!'
Una Lucem keeps looking at you. 'I see what father meant about weakness. The old races aren't as strong as I am. Their skin always burns off too quickly, or their limbs won't stay on.'
Praetor Venatos cries out. Tree limbs snap as a gigantic lizard crashes into the clearing. As it lands, it crushes Venatos and his troops.
Praetor Venatos died.
a vanguard died.
a razer died.
a scout died.
Una Lucem raises her head to look up at the lizard towering above her. 'This one looks stronger. It will please Father.' Una Lucem begins casting a spell.
Lyirae Oakwynd calls out weakly. 'No, you must stop her! The creature is innocent...'
Una Lucem turns her attention upon you. 'Yes? Why do you pester me? I am doing the good will of my father. He wants to bring perfection to the world. You don't want that? You don't want your children to have the strength of this creature? See how strong it is!'
Una Lucem releases a spell upon the creature. It glares at you hungrily.

---

Defeat the Thuliasaur 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Thuliasaur summons from 100% health; sees through all forms of invisibility; hits for a max ~24,500; AE rampages; single-target rampages; and has an AE called "Massive Stomp":_
Massive Stomp: PB AE 100', Magic (-975)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 60000
2: Stun (3.00 sec)
_DPS it to 5% for the update._
See what happens to the Thuliasaur 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Lyirae Oakwynd shouts 'No, don't kill it!'
Una Lucem touches her fingers to the symbol on her breastplate. 'Oh, this creature is weak. It will never please Father.' She outstretches her other arm and points two fingers at the lizard. She drops her fingers toward the ground.
For a moment, the lizard is lit by a faint blue glow before a pillar of magic streaks out of the sky and consumes it.
Lyirae Oakwynd cries out and covers her head as the magical flames destroy the lizard and singe the trees.
Thuliasaur died.
Una Lucem looks at you and blinks. 'I must meet Father's dark woman at the Dredge. You will come and watch me?' Una Lucem disappears in a pillar of light.

---

Speak with Lyirae 0/5 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Hail her until the task completes. A chest also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Lyirae Oakwynd puts her hand over the rent running down the front of Galeth's frame. 'I won't add to the salt... I don't want to ruin your joints...' Lyirae brushes her cheeks, though the rain still falls.
Lyirae Oakwynd looks at Galeth's legs, which have broken off below the knee joints. 'I knew this would happen... I told you to wait. This is how it always ends...'
Galeth Veredeth's face remains expressionless as always. 'You know my metal feels no pain. And still my spirit lives. Take heart, Lyirae. We must go to Firiona. There is still time before we reach the sea.'
Lyirae Oakwynd picks up Galeth and bears his weight as best she can. She turns toward the beach. 'We will journey with you to Firiona, though...' Lyirae looks around at the burning stumps, the carcasses, and then the untouched jungle. 'I almost wish I could stay here -- remove myself from all the strife to come, all the death...'
Lyirae Oakwynd listens to Galeth speak as he rests on her back. 'As I said, there is still time. Our courses do not end on this island, in this river. We have a long way to go once we [leave] here. We should meet with Captain Sedgethwait on Brother Island and set sail together.
_Rewards:_
150 platinum
Experience (3-4% of Level 105)

_This solo task begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in the Combine Dredge, located at the south dock._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
_Say "find" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks troubled. '\_\_\_\_\_, you arrived, at last. And [sorrow] sailed with you.'
You say, 'Sorrow?'
Firiona Vie looks over the water to the horizon. 'I did not think this journey would be so trying. We came to the Buried Sea to heal Tsaph Katta, but now we are the ones who need healing. [Dabner], [Dagda], [Ognit], [Galeth], [Lyirae], and [Thubr] -- they sailed here aboard the Swift Fin, but for now only their [spirits] can accompany us.
You say, 'What about Dabner?'
Firiona Vie says 'He does not sleep. He barely eats. His only concern is [Dagda]; he tends to her wounds day and night. I fear his craft has suffered from years of disuse since we last journeyed together. I hope ardor makes up for neglect.'
You say, 'What about Dagda?'
Firiona Vie says 'She talks little and sleeps much. Her leg swells and inflames her fever. That such strength could lie helpless upon a sickbed when I knew her as one of the fiercest warriors in Norrath...' Firiona's brow darkens. 'You were there. Was there nothing more you could have done? I only hope [Dabner]'s healing skills are enough...''
You say, 'What about Ognit?'
Firiona Vie says 'He says he has given up magic and that he can never be what Al`Kabor would have wanted him to be. Tinkering shall be his sole pursuit. For [Galeth], this is a boon. Right now Ognit works to repair Galeth's damaged body with what few materials we have on hand. It will not be simple.'
You say, 'What about Galeth?'
Firiona Vie says 'My father... it is strange to see him in that metallic frame. I can no longer read the expressions of care or tenderness in his face. It is almost as though his body now matches his spririt... He has been long away from the physical world, and to him both the past and present lie in a dim fog that has yet to burn away in the daylight. [Ognit] works to repair my father's damaged body, but I wonder if he can repair my father's connection to this world...'
You say, 'What about Lyirae?'
Firiona Vie says 'Her heart is caring but careworn. The peace she seeks cannot be found on this journey, I suspect... At times she sits with [Dagda] or [Galeth], silently watching over them. Other times she sits alone on the stern, looking off into the sea. Sometimes I think she would rather be where I found her, wandering the forests of Faydark.'
You say, 'What about Thubr?'
Firiona Vie says 'He almost talks less than Dagda. I've been told he slept through the voyage. He wakes to eat and drink but soon retreats to his bunk. Though he has held onto his wits more tightly than when we landed on Brother Island, he remains sullen. He speaks of a promise made to the Priestess of Degmar, but he won't say what that promise was.'
You say, 'What about their spirits?'
Firiona Vie says 'My companions hurt in body and spirit. As you know, they have not fared well on this journey.' Firiona looks at the ground. 'I should have been there for them, leading them. Alone I left them to suffer, like I did before... But what choice did I have then? And what [choice] do I have now?'
You say, 'What choice?'
Firiona Vie says 'We were not ready for this journey, and my companions are not yet ready to continue it. Already we have failed each other and the people of the Buried Sea. And now Lanys is set to destroy what little work we have done here along with anything else she can touch with the Lifeguide's power! And what of our capricious [friends], Al`Kabor and Dreezil?' Firiona shakes her head. 'If it were not for the words of Tunare, I would say coming to the Buried Sea was a [mistake].'
You say, 'What about those friends?'
Firiona Vie says 'This is the location Al`Kabor specified in his cipher. I arrived days ago and have been scouting these strange [formations] and looking for [Station 10].'
You say, 'What strange formations?'
Firiona Vie says 'Whole sections of the seabed rise to the surface, pulled by what I can only surmise is concentrated geomantic magic. Soldiers dressed like the ones that attacked Katta Castrum patrol the walkways. Perhaps they also seek [Station 10]...'
You say, 'Station 10?'
Firiona Vie says 'Domes dot the islands, most having been raised from the sea. They must be ancient Combine structures. Perhaps one of these is Station 10. [Dreezil] thought as much when I last spoke with him, but that was days ago.'
You say, 'Why was it a mistake to come here?'
Firiona Vie says 'I saw my gravestone. Under moonlight I walked to the grove. The autumn leaves had fallen. I brushed away the stone. In the center my likeness stood, arm raised, Lifeguide in hand, sending rays of light arching over the stele. Beneath the rays my companions were arrayed, looking to me with unchangeable faces, wondering where my [light] had gone.'
You say, 'What light?'
Firiona Vie says 'The moon was clouded. The dust was thick. The leaves were thicker. The grove was abandoned. Not a soul had stood before my grave since I had vanished from the world. I touched the cold [stone].'
You say, 'What stone?'
Firiona Vie says 'My finger found an indentation, a hole where once a jewel might have been placed: the light, the gem of the Lifeguide, was a dark pit. Perhaps the light lay beneath the leaves, beneath the press of [time]. The moon crept lower. The grove darkened.'
You say, 'Time?'
Firiona Vie says 'We grew old. You have seen my companions: their autumn heads are now dappled with snow. By now I am sure the grove is deep in drifts. That woman, with her staff and stalwart friends, lies buried and forgotten -- a relic of ages past. I never expected the [spring] to come.'
You say, 'The spring?'
Firiona Vie says 'Yet we must bloom at Tunare's behest.' Firiona sighs. 'I only hope the spring is true and this is not our winter. We must walk on, like the snow joins the river and then the sea.''
You say, 'Where is Dreezil?'
Firiona Vie says 'He seemed [distracted] when we spoke, and he wouldn't look me in the eye. That night I slept on the dock. He kept watch. When I awoke he was gone, and I have not seen him since.'
You say, 'Distracted?'
Firiona Vie says 'Really?' Firiona narrows her eyes as you tell her what occurred between Dreezil and the Brothers. 'No wonder...' Firiona ponders for a moment. 'Things would have gone much differently had he stayed put and kept his hands in his own pockets. We need to [find] him. He might know more about what is happening here and where Al`Kabor might be.'
You say, 'I'll find him...'
Firiona Vie says 'He spoke of a landlocked ship to the east. Supposedly Lanys sailed it to the Buried Sea. I would start your search there. Take the first path east, across the submerged patch of seabed.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Fickle Wind Blows'.
Firiona has been exploring the Dredge, where she has determined where Al'Kabor's coordinates lead. She is still trying to determine where Station 10 is, and Dreezil might be able to help. Unfortunately, the halfling ran off, and you will need to search for him.

---

!

---

Head along the eastern path toward Dreezil's last known location 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
Speak with the bridge minders 0/3 (Combine Dredge)
_This update triggers the spawn of two auto-aggro mobs._
a bridge watcher says 'Another recruit to our ranks? Does hatred flow through your veins? Raise the bridge, and we shall find the halfling together, as Holmein commands!'
a bridge minder says 'I don't take orders from fanatics. Calix told us we're here to mine for resources, not look for halflings. I'm not going to countermand his orders and risk his daughter's wrath! Besides, this creature doesn't look half as insane as you do.'
a bridge minder says 'Guards!'
a bridge watcher says 'Yes... Guards, kill it!'
Kill the bridge guards 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
_These two mobs each hit for a max ~14,500. They are immune to stuns, but mezzable._
a bridge guard has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a bridge guard's corpse says 'You can't stop us all!'

---

Cross the walkway gap using the geomancy orb, and investigate the ship to the north 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
As you board the ship, you hear a voice call out to you from behind the tiller. 'You don't look like one of Lanys' followers or a Combine soldier. Come, come, we must talk! Please?'

---

Speak with Captain Mewes 0/6 (Combine Dredge)
_The Captain stands on the ship's deck._
Captain Mewes's pupils retreat from his eyelids. He breathes deeply and rapidly. 'You sailed from Odus? Are you with the halfling? Are you after Lanys? Quiet! Not too loudly... We can't let her know...
Captain Mewes glances around. 'Too long to [explain] when eyes and ears are upon you! She took our ship when we were about to set sail. I'm just a merchant! She killed my mercenaries! And now the things she makes me do!' Captain Mewes' hand trembles.
You say, 'Explain yourself'
Captain Mewes looks around to be sure the two of you aren't being observed. `Yes. Lanys, that... that...' Captain Mewes mumbles something so no-one can hear. 'Lanys and her Doomscale cronies captured my ship and killed all my guards and much of my crew. Then, once we got here, she made me do [horrible] things!
You say, 'What horrible things?'
Captain Mewes says 'I'm just the captain of a merchant ship. Sure, I like to dress like a pirate, but I'm not! And she... She [makes] me torture cultists who don't give the proper prayer and obedience. She even makes me execute her prisoners!'
You say, 'She 'makes' you?'
Captain Mewes says 'Okay, okay, I'm not brave. I'm a coward. But if I don't do what she asks me to, she will to do it to me, instead. I just want to go home!'
Captain Mewes says 'And the journey... A ship full of hateful things burning and piercing their flesh. The stench! We anchored here, and the land closed about us. The Combine swarmed, and Lanys obliterated them. She made a pact with their leader, and now we wait. But if only waiting were all that Lanys would ask of me...'
Captain Mewes adjusts his cap nervously. 'Lanys must be stopped... I can't take much more of this.'
Captain Mewes's hands won't stay still, and his eyes dart about. 'The halfling, Dreezil? He was here, yes... is here? He came days ago, asked where he was. Offered gold to store his belongings. I told him what Lanys might do; I told him...'
Captain Mewes says 'He gave me the gold and promised more. He promised help! Are you that help?' Captain Mewes shakes his head. 'I gave him a hidden compartment under the prow. Told him it was safe... maybe he's still there? Maybe he's still safe?''

---

Speak with Dreezil on the ship 0/9 (Combine Dredge)
_Dreezil is found on the east side of the ship, just below the main deck._
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens'
Dreezil Stickens darts his hand to his dagger. He stops short, laughs, and shows his teeth; but his eyes don't smile. 'Oh, hello, and you are? \_\_\_\_\_? So Mewes continues to be his old mewling self?
Dreezil Stickens says 'After the gold I paid him... A stern look or a prick of pain, and that crying babe would sic a soldier on his own mother!' Dreezil waves his hand.' Lanys can have him!'
Dreezil Stickens smiles and taps his dagger hilt. 'Oh, yes, the gold. So you've been hearing stories? And you trust that lot of thieving, woman-fearing 'brothers?''
Dreezil Stickens says 'I'll tell you how it went: I was out for a stroll in the evening, and I took a turn by Dagda's tent. You know they had her stuck out there, under guard, just for being a woman?' Dreezil laughs. 'Well, one of those guards who probably never saw a woman up close just had to get closer.'
Dreezil Stickens says 'He actually got to touch her -- at least his face got to touch her fist! The brute went stomping off, but I didn't think he'd had enough. I followed him and gave him a double thrashing for the sake of all women! And to compensate me for the lesson I had taught him, he so generously gave me his coin purse.' Dreezil smiles and mimics the flick of his dagger. 'Just a little bit of gold.'
Dreezil Stickens says 'Well, the embarrassed bugger bawled to Quigley. Now Old Man Masculinity wants my head. What is he going to do, come clean and admit his modest and merry man got caught trying to steal some sweets and had to pay the price? No, he makes up a story about a thieving halfling raiding his coffers!'
Dreezil Stickens says 'I couldn't let my companions take the blame, so I took myself elsewhere, to this 'fine' place. No need to bother Firiona with such things. She has plenty more important things to deal with out here.'
Dreezil Stickens says 'I didn't sign up for a campaign against Lanys T`Vyl and an army! Don't mistake me, it's no problem; I can handle it. I just thought it would be a nice little island jaunt with maybe a bit of pirate booty here and there. Now Al`Kabor is hiding out here, chased by a crazed cult and mad geomancers? The trouble Firiona gets herself into!'
Dreezil Stickens says 'Well, Firiona knows me: always ready to help!' Dreezil flips a coin and catches it. 'If she wants domes, I'll give her domes. You take the northwestern-most domes, and I'll take the ones slightly north of those. I think between the both of us we can keep Miss Firiona happy.'

---

Kill the Combine and Doomscale near the northeastern domes to find clues about Station 10 0/7 (Combine Dredge)

You take a section of stone with writing on it from the corpse. You see the number '1'.
You take a section of stone with writing on it from the corpse. You see the number '3'.
You take a section of stone with writing on it from the corpse. You see the number '4'.

Speak with Dreezil at the northern domes 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
You say, 'Hail, Dreezil Stickens'
Dreezil Stickens smiles, unhurt and unphased by the activity of the Doomscale and Combine. 'See what I've been dealing with here? Dreezil doesn't run away from danger; he runs into it! Firiona will be glad I sailed here by the time this is said and done. Did you know I've known her the longest out of all her companions? Yes, Dreezil's always there, doing the dirty business that must be done! Come on; I'm going to check the large dome to the southeast.

---

Find the dome in the center of the Dredge 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
You hear Dreezil whisper as loudly as he dares. 'Hey, over here! Behind the arch, in the bush!'
Speak with Dreezil near the large central dome 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
_He'll be hidden in some flora, so look carefully in the area._
Dreezil Stickens says 'Look at all of them gathered like they're waiting for theater doors to open. Must be a popular show, and Al`Kabor quite the star! He always did like the attention.'
Dreezil Stickens says 'Let's be sure, though. We can't have you showing up to the wrong play, can we? There must be markings like the other domes. Why don't you take a look? Discreetly, of course... And while you're at it, have a listen to the pre-show gossip.'

---

Defeat the demolishers attacking the large dome, and examine the clues they are carrying 0/4 (Combine Dredge)
_Mobs called "a demolisher" can be found along the outside of the center dome's wall. The general zone population in this area is mezzable, but the demolishers are not. These mobs hit for a max ~15,000._
_Note: There are some Shroud of Stealth spawns in this area ("a Doomscale assassin" - can't see these without SeeInvis2, and they, themselves, see invisibility)._
You take a section of stone from the corpse's hands. It is blank.
You take a section of stone from the corpse's hands. It is blank.
You take a section of stone from the corpse's hands. It is blank.
You pick up the last piece of stone and see symbols on it. They read 'Station 10.'

---

Listen to the Combine and Doomscale near the large central dome 0/4 (Combine Dredge)
_Move back out towards the entrance/ramp area leading up to the dome, where you'll then see:_
You hear a shout of rage bellowing from the front of the dome. Holmein the Hated places his sword in his palm and begins dragging the sharp edge across his skin. 'By my blood, I swear!' Holmein flicks his wound at the nearest Doomscale recruit, spraying blood on his face. 'This door will open, or the multitude of rents and fissures I open in your flesh will turn the sea red!'
Holmein shouts, 'Dorell, I'll start with you, liar! Your worthless geomancy can't open a simple door!'
Dorell's voice tries to maintain control, but he can't look at Holmein's disfigured body. 'It will happen... Just need more time and more magic. Something inside the station is keeping us out, sustaining the door with magic. We'll need to increase the power output of the crystals...'
Holmein shouts, 'Look at my flesh! This is my mistress's mercy, her instruction... You will have no mercy.' Holmein licks the blood off his palm.

---

Speak with Dreezil near the large central dome 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
Dreezil Stickens grins after an involuntary shudder passes through him at the name of Holmein. 'That one's a piece of work... literally. Lanys' plaything, her flesh mannequin. The man in charge of the Dredge Doomscale. Used to be in the Qeynos guard, or so I hear. Never was too stable. Joined with the Doomscale when he thought the world was going to end.'
Dreezil Stickens produces a dagger and holds it out to you. As it lies in his palm, you can see half-healed marks on his wrist where the skin was cut. 'Maybe he's angry it didn't end. Maybe he's mad Lanys put a few more holes in his flesh last night. Well, maybe you can take this dagger, find a spot for another hole, and solve his existential dilemma.'

---

Stab Holmein the Hated with the dagger Dreezil gave you 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_You'll be ported away to the southeastern part of the zone._
As you lunge at Holmein with the dagger, he turns, raises his hand, and lets the blade stab through his wounded palm. He licks the blood off the dagger's tip. 'So the halfling sends you with hate in hand. You don't know the meaning of hate. Do you know where the staff is? Where Firiona is? How to enter the station? You will tell us, but we will never be done with you. You will know the meaning of hate.' Holmein twists his wrist and snaps the blade of the dagger. With the flat of his sword he knocks the side of your head.

---

Speak with Amlis the Torturer 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
You say, 'Hail, Amlis the Torturer'
Amlis the Torturer holds the meat hook near your skin. '[Talk] to me. Give me meat to chew on. I'm hungry.' Your torturer taps the tip of the hook on your arm. 'And I know the sharks and nagas are also hungry. [Talk].'
_Rewards:_
Experience
150 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._
