_This solo task begins with Amlis the Torturer ( **click for map image**) in the Combine Dredge, located in the far southeastern corner of the island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)
_NOTE: Upon requesting this task, you will be ported to the air above the water. If you have levitation on, you'll have time to quickly invis yourself before you fall into the water. If you fall in the water without invis, you will probably aggro some of the sharks in it. Be prepared in case this happens. Also note that sharks don't drop their aggro if you leave the water. The mobs here can be glitchy and warp around some._
_Say "talk" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Amlis the Torturer holds the meat hook near your skin. '[Talk] to me. Give me meat to chew on. I'm hungry.' Your torturer taps the tip of the hook on your arm. 'And I know the sharks and nagas are also hungry. [Talk].'
You say, 'Hail, Amlis the Torturer'
Amlis the Torturer says 'You going to [talk]?'
You say, 'I won't talk!'
Amlis the Torturer says 'You refuse? When the sharks' teeth get rid of your flesh and begin chipping your bones, you'll be begging me to talk!'
You have been assigned the task 'What Hate Begets'.
You have been captured by Holmein the Hated and the forces of Lanys. You must escape from your torturers, find Firiona, and construct an object that will destabilize the Combine magic.

---

!

---

Defeat the sharks to survive the southeastern pool 0/15 (Combine Dredge)
_The sharks here each hit for a max ~5,000; don't have too many hitpoints (relatively); and are mezzable and rootable/snareable. They have a fast respawn timer. Kill 15 of them, and then get out of the water._
Firiona shouts from the ridge overlooking the cauldron of torture. 'I have scoured the Dredge looking for you! Thank Tunare you are safe! Meet me on the path below; there is much to discuss!'
Speak with Firiona at the bottom of the path to the southeastern shark pool 0/6 (Combine Dredge)
Firiona Vie places her hand on your shoulder. 'I know what it is to be captured, imprisoned... tortured...'
Firiona Vie says 'If I had only known you were being held by Lanys' forces, I would have come for you...'
Firiona Vie looks around and lowers her voice. 'You saw Dreezil? He did not mention that he spoke with you...' Firiona frowns. 'I have known him for a long while. I thought I still did. But this Dreezil seems almost a stranger...'
Firiona Vie says 'Dreezil returned to me at the docks. He said he found Station 10 and that Lanys' forces were trying to break through a magically sealed door. Al`Kabor must be inside.'
Firiona Vie says 'If Dreezil's information can be trusted, the Doomscale and the geomancers are trying to concentrate the magic of the dredging crystals to rip open the door. We must disrupt those crystals if we are to have a chance at saving the staff and Al`Kabor.'
Firiona Vie says 'Now, to disrupt the crystals... We need magi-potent ore from the rock quarry and salt-laden rock from patches of the seabed. Refine both at the smelter complex, if you can find your way inside. And see if you can find out anything more about Lanys' plans. Hurry; I do not think we have much time.'

---

Defeat enemies in the northeastern rock quarry that might hold Magi-potent Ore 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
Defeat aquatic enemies and regrua that might hold Salt-laden Rock 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
Loot Magi-potent Ore from enemies in the northeastern rock quarry 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
Loot Salt-laden Rock from aquatic enemies and regrua 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Ore can drop from any mobs that spawn in the quarry in the northeastern part of the zone. The more players in your group that are on this step, the more ore that will drop per mob._
_The same can be said for regruas, crabs or any aggressive creature really, for the rocks._

---

Forge a Magi-potent Crystal on the second floor of the large northern power station 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_This is the northernmost tower of the three towers. On the second floor are two forges in different rooms. Each forge tends to have a see-invis spawn next to it, so you can probably plan on a bit of clearing or mezzing. In the forge, combine the 10 items to produce a "Magi-potent Crystal"._

---

Pick up Holmein's logbook on the top floor of the large southern power station 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
You open the stained book and leaf through the pages. '...The knife burrows. It is a maggot under my skin, eating the putrid remnants of weakness. All that remains is the burning purity of hatred. The mistress smiles and pushes deeper... The bones, the pins -- each is a reminder of the purity we share and the hate the mistress feels. She names them as she shoves them in: Thubr, Dagda, Firiona. They will feel as I feel. The pain, the hate... The staff shall be hers, and tonight she will again teach my flesh to obey, to burn, to hate...
_This triggers the spawn of 2x "a flesh disciple" which aggro you, kill them._
a flesh disciple says 'Come, join the watery dead.'
a flesh disciple says 'Come, join the watery dead.'
Kill Holmein's flesh disciples that found you looking at Holmein's logbook 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
_They are of average difficulty for the zone (hit for a max ~15,000; reported as no longer mezzable)._
a flesh disciple has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a flesh disciple's corpse says 'I hate you!'

---

Speak with Firiona on the dock 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
Firiona Vie shuts Holmein's book in disgust and flings it onto the dock. 'To think someone like that was once in the Qeynos Guard... How long does it take to really know a person?'
Firiona Vie says 'We must [strike] now with force, before Lanys knows we are here!'

_This group mission begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in the Combine Dredge, located at the south dock._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)

_Say "lead" to request the task and "go" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie says 'You were able to [find] Dreezil. Now we must [strike] before Lanys can find us.'
You say, 'Strike?'
Firiona Vie hesitates. '\_\_\_\_\_, you were there in Katta Castrum. I... failed. My mission, the time I spent healing Tsaph -- all that was taken from me without my being able to raise my sword to stop it. I was [unprepared].'
You say, 'Unprepared?'
Firiona Vie says 'For Lanys, the invasion, the death and injury of good people and friends. And perhaps I was unprepared to lead. Sending my companions off into the sea when they themselves were not ready -- that was my true failure. I [brought] this upon us.'
You say, 'How was it brought upon us?'
Firiona Vie says, 'Together we remain [strong], but we had spent so much time apart. Everyone had moved on, forgotten... how could it be the same? How could we be the same? How deep the snow lies...' Firiona clenches her fist.
You say, 'We are strong...'
Firiona Vie says 'True, Lanys would love to see me crushed by weakness and remorse.' Firiona raises her sword. The sun glints off its blade. 'But the snows will cover all of Norrath before that happens. I will not look back. I will [lead].'
You say, 'Lead the way...'
Firiona Vie brings her sword closer. She can see herself in its cold reflection. 'Lanys has part of the Lifeguide. She knows we are here. She wishes to take the last of the staff and crush us, to undo her failure in Kithicor and finish what she started. That will not happen. I will be the head of the hammer that crushes her first. Let us [go].'
You have been assigned the task 'Deep Lies the Snow'.
Now that you know where Station 10 is and have a crystal that can disrupt magic, you will need to shut down the power sources before Lanys and her forces can open the dome and attack Al'Kabor.
You say, 'Let's go'
Firiona Vie says 'Good.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

!

---

Speak with Firiona 0/6 (Combine Dredge)
Firiona Vie says 'Are you ready, \_\_\_\_\_? Bringing the magi-potent crystal in contact with the geomancy crystal should disrupt the magical streams flowing to the transmitter at Station 10. This is our chance. The staff and Al`Kabor's life are at stake.' Firiona looks down at the halfling, her eyes trying to read his face. 'Dreezil, are you ready?''
Dreezil Stickens smiles, looking over Firiona's shoulder. 'Of course, have I not always had your back?' Dreezil covers the hilt of his dagger. 'The incident on Brother Island... it was nothing. You know that, right? I was watching over Dagda. Bygones and all, right?'
Firiona Vie squints at the low-lying sun. 'The past hurls us downstream, over forests and fields we'll never see again, but there are innumerable forks ahead.'
Dreezil Stickens shrugs. 'What's that supposed to mean?' He laughs quickly. 'You always were the serious one!'
Firiona Vie says 'Take it as you like. I will know what it means to you by the tributary you take.'
Firiona Vie turns to you. 'We strike the geomancers and disrupt the crystals. Now!'

The structure shakes as the geomancy crystal's power is disrupted. You hear the grinding limbs of a stone worker sent to stop you!
a crystalguard worker rotates towards you and bounds forwards.

Dreezil Stickens breathes heavily. 'No, no, I'm fine. Let's get on with it.' You hear coins clinking in his pockets.

Defeat the crystal geomancers 0/9 (Combine Dredge)
_Repeat as above, except for the top floor where you'll find "a crystalguard golem" alongside 2x "a crystal geomancer". The golem has a floor-wide aggro range, but does not see invisibility; it hits for a max ~20,000 and doesn't cast anything. The two geomancers are mezzable._

a crystal geomancer's corpse moans, 'Why, why do you persist?'
a crystalguard golem's corpse collapses in a heap.
a crystalguard golem has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!

Touch the geomancy crystal to disrupt its power 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
Speak with Firiona 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
Firiona Vie shouts 'The geomantic power has been stopped! Al`Kabor's magic will hold while we deal with Lanys' forces. Hurry to Station 10!'
Dreezil Stickens drags his feet. 'You run along, and I'll be there soon. Don't mind my little legs; they tire so easily!'

---

Meet Firiona at the large central dome 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
Speak with Firiona 0/12 (Combine Dredge)
Firiona Vie holds up her hand. 'Stay. Wait and listen. Something is happening.'
Holmein the Hated stands with his sword raised. Blood drips down his shoulder from a freshly inserted bone chip. He roars. 'Dorell, the beam fails! When Lanys arrives, your useless geomancy will raise a stone pike for your head!'
Lanys T`Vyl breathes the sea air, charged with salt, magic, blood, and tension. 'I can taste Firiona's desperation and sorrow; it is so near!' Lanys laughs. 'Where is Firiona's humiliation, Holmein? Where is the staff?!'
Holmein the Hated shouts 'Mistress, the halfling stabs us in the back. His heart does not bear enough scars! The ship...'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts, 'Has been taken care of -- as you will be tonight. For every failure comes fresh punishment, Holmein.' Lanys smiles, watching the blood leak from Holmein's shoulder.
Dreezil Stickens mumbles, 'Firiona, I... Don't believe their lies. A rogue still knows who his friends are...' Dreezil drinks from a potion and disappears.
Firiona Vie speaks as loudly as she dares. 'Dreezil!' She turns to you. 'He would not... No, I will not believe it!'
Una Lucem looks around, eyes unblinking. Her face does not register a change as she watches Holmein's agitated breathing, Lanys' sneering smile, and the frightened features of the rank and file. 'What's wrong with your faces?'
Lanys T`Vyl laughs, 'That would be shame, Una, and fear at what I will do to them for leaving our positions so woefully undefended. Once the dredging crystals and beam are restarted, we will--'
Una Lucem says, 'Oh, you want the beam?' Una Lucem places her hand over the symbol on her breastplate. With her other hand she points to each of the dredging complexes.
Your hair begins to rise, and you smell a charge in the air. A low hum thrums in your gut. The dredging complexes throw blue fire as pillars of magic descend from the sky. The beam leaps to life, and magic envelops Lanys and Una.
Firiona Vie shouts, 'Not even Al`Kabor's magic can stand up to such power! We must stop them!' Firiona dashes at Lanys but is held back by the swirling magic.

---

Speak with Lanys 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
Lanys T`Vyl smiles at Firiona. 'She emerges from hiding!' Lanys laughs. 'Firiona, you can't hide anything from me: your fears, your weaknesses, your anger, your friends, your staff, or your life. They are mine to toy with, mine to take, mine to end!'
Una Lucem looks at you and tilts her head. 'You came to watch me again.' Una Lucem points at the door of the dome. A beam of pure magic fires from the pylon. The door's magical protection is demolished.
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'Holmein, bring me Firiona's downfall!'
Lanys T`Vyl sneers. 'Shouldn't you stop him, Firiona? I've already taken so much from you; do you want me to take the rest?'
Firiona Vie shouts '\_\_\_\_\_, stay by my side! Protect Al`Kabor and the staff!'

---

Speak with Al'Kabor inside the dome 0/9 (Combine Dredge)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Be ready for a fight after a short delay following the last hail._
Al`Kabor sits in silence, Lifeguide staff in hand. He looks at you, Firiona, and Holmein. He raises an eyebrow. 'You are keeping strange company these days, Firiona. Did you grow bored without me?'
Holmein the Hated growls. 'The staff, or the pain will last longer than you'll wish your life had!'
Al`Kabor waves his hand and rolls his eyes. 'Yes, rip out my fingernails, pierce my kneecaps, unspool my intestines -- all of those trite things that scare provincial minds who have never seen true terror.'
Al`Kabor continues. 'Have you ever destroyed the world... Simple One? Yes, I shall call you 'Simple One,' for I see you employ your emotions and your flesh far more than your mind.'
Al`Kabor says, 'You see, Simple One, this mind --' Al`Kabor taps his forehead -- 'has seen the destruction of Norrath, its lands broken and drifting in an endless void. This mind, with its immeasurable power, nearly brought about the end of all life.'
Holmein the Hated begins sliding his sword edge along his damaged palm, making it bleed afresh. 'Your life is about to end! The staff will--'
Al`Kabor waves his hand. A hard layer of ice forms over Holmein's nose and mouth. He begins clawing at it, struggling to breathe. Al`Kabor continues, 'I was not finished, Simple One. Be quiet and listen. I shall make it even simpler for you. It was a dream, an allegory: my mind, afraid of its own power, was broken.
Al`Kabor says, 'But the Lifeguide has shown me the truth: fear is all that can break a mind, not power. And my power is greater than any fear, greater than any self-mutilation. I shall never be a snake eating its own tail.' Al`Kabor rises to his feet. He tosses the Lifeguide staff to Firiona.
Holmein, unable to chip the ice from his face, still fighting for breath, raises his sword to his neck. He slides the blade along his windpipe. He lets out a burbling gasp as air rushes into his lungs. His sword moves again, this time seeking Al`Kabor's neck.

---

Defeat Holmein the Hated 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Holmein the Hated hits for a max ~24,000 and AE rampages. Every 25 seconds, he casts the spell "Tainted Blood" on a random target (targeted 15' range AE for 75,000 dmg + DOT for 8,333/tick)._
Lanys T`Vyl shouts, 'You have not yet grown strong enough in hatred!' Lanys turns to Una Lucem. 'Hasten this.'

---

See what happens to Holmein 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
Una Lucem says, 'The weak never survive citadel magic.' Una Lucem points at the open door of Station 10, directing the energy of the dredging crystals.
Another blast of magic tears through the open doorway. Holmein catches the brunt and falls to the ground, scorched by the beam. Al`Kabor, Firiona, and you are dazed by the attack.
You are incapacitated by a massive magic blast.
Lanys T`Vyl wrenches the Lifeguide staff out of Firiona's grasp. 'Your heart will not survive outside the glass cage. It will shatter as it throws itself against my granite hatred. You will break yourself and your companions, like you broke your staff and your promise. You should have remained in the clutches of Discord. All would have been safe.'
Lanys T`Vyl looks at Holmein and sighs disappointedly. 'I thought I built you more sturdily than this. I carved you into a model of hatred, but you're made of talc!'
Holmein the Hated groans through the cut in his throat as his flesh smolders.
Una Lucem stares at you, then Firiona, not paying attention to Lanys or Holmein. 'You survived again. You are strong. I want you to visit my father's home. I want to try things on you.'
Lanys T`Vyl smiles, 'Yes, it would be disappointing and pathetic if we never saw Firiona again. We have so much left to discover together, so much to learn about ourselves! See to your companions, and I will see you soon, Champion of Balance!'
Lanys T`Vyl's gleaming smile fades as she teleports away with Una Lucem and Holmein.

---

Speak with Firiona 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_Hail her until the task completes. A chest also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
Firiona Vie slams her sword to the ground and thrusts her finger at Al`Kabor. 'I told you to sail to Brother Island, to warn the others, to... to keep the Lifeguide safe! Now Dagda, Thubr, Galeth... you have no idea how much trouble you caused or how much trouble we will be in because of your selfish actions!'
Al`Kabor brushes off his robe. 'We fight powerful foes, but now my mind is clear. To have my undistracted mind in your arsenal is worth a Lifeguide and a few bruises. I did what had to be done. If you do not recognize this, it is your own shortcoming.'
Firiona Vie's eyes close, and her fists clench as she tries to quell her anger. 'At least you haven't changed... but I must remember that I cannot trust you with precise instructions. My lapse in judgment has nearly cost us everything...'
Al`Kabor crosses his arms. 'Do not trust your own understanding. Your precision is not my concern. You can always trust me to do the right thing.'
Dreezil Stickens emerges from the shadows. 'Firiona, I...' He tosses a closed bag to Firiona, who almost drops it in surprise. 'Remember how you once told me it was undignified to loot the pockets of the dead? You won't have to tell me again. I won't forget this time...' Dreezil slinks back into the shadows and disappears.
Firiona Vie slowly unties the top of the bag and looks inside. She lets the bag fall to the ground. As the contents spill, you see the glint of a gem -- small, shaped like the head of the Lifeguide, chipped on one side, dull as a cloudy winter day. Around the gem you count thirty pieces of platinum, worn and tarnished.
Firiona Vie says, 'The leaves and snows, how deep they lie...' She quietly picks up the gem.
Firiona Vie retrieves the bag's final contents: a glowing stone, shaped like a small pillar. 'A key? We have seen a pillar like this below the northern dredging complex...'
Al`Kabor says, 'Who better to pilfer the key of a home unattended than a pickpocket?'
Firiona Vie looks at the gem. 'I hope he finds whatever home he seeks...' She turns to you. 'I must return to the Swift Fin and see how our companions fare. [Leave] here. Go to the glowing pillar beneath the northern power structure in the Dredge. With this key, we shall meet on the other side of the portal, wherever it may lead.'

_This solo task begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis, located at the zone-in._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "What Hate Begets" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Deep Lies the Snow" (Combine Dredge)
_Say "speak" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks up at the massive Arx Mentis citadel. The glowing lights glitter in her eyes. 'The ship... why didn't I protect the [ship]?'
You say, 'What about the ship?'
Firiona Vie says 'As we fought for our lives, so did they... Lanys knew. She knew exactly where to [strike].'
You say, 'How did they strike?'
Firiona Vie says 'The vermin crowded the dock and swarmed the ship. They were injured, [unprepared]--' Firiona looks away. 'As was I...'
You say, 'Why were you unprepared?'
Firiona Vie clenches her jaw. 'I had forgotten how it feels to see those you love [injured] and ripped away from you -- the crashing waves of helplessness, sorrow, and rage. And to know the current upon which they are [carried]... I would sail after them to the ends of Norrath... to where the stars meet the sea.' Firiona gazes at the citadel.
You say, 'How were they injured?'
Firiona Vie says 'When Al`Kabor and I arrived at the Swift Fin, we found the deck smoking and splintered, the hatch smashed. I never wanted to see what waited in the hold, below the water line, beneath the sunlight, in my darkest [fears].'
You say, 'Where were they carried?'
Firiona Vie's eyes burn as she stares at the citadel. 'They are in that monstrosity, trapped in the skies at the end of the seas. But when we arrived, we did not find what we [expected] in the city below.'
You say, 'What are your fears?'
Firiona Vie says 'The stars you have known, those by which you have navigated the darkened seas... were they extinguished you would be lost. We descended below. The hold was vast, dark, empty, filled with smoke and shadows. And there stood [Ognit], smeared in blood and soot.'
You say, 'What was expected in the city?'
Firiona Vie says 'Companies standing at the ready, waiting to destroy us; citizens as bloodthirsty as their armies; regimented lives and minds as straight as these walls and spires -- we found [none] of these things.'
You say, 'None of those things?'
Firiona Vie says 'The people toil beneath the citadel, knowing little of the machinations of their rulers that hover high above them, out of reach but never out of sight. What more do the people know than how to stay fed, sheltered, and supplied with diversions and [promises] from the skies of ever brighter futures?'
You say, 'What promises?'
Firiona Vie says 'Al`Kabor, Ognit, and I have been speaking with the citizens, looking for a way into the citadel. The citizens are agitated. They talk of disappearances and strange events in the citadel: its initial closure from all traffic and then the movements of researchers and troops through the portals -- and the sightings of a dark elf woman accompanied by mutilated figures. And there is also the [Servus Libertas].'
You say, 'Ognit was hurt?'
Firiona Vie says 'He wiped the blood from his eyes. 'They're gone,' he told us. He dropped to his knees and placed his hand on [Sedgethwait]'s chest. It did not rise.'
You say, 'What about Captain Sedgethwait?'
Firiona Vie looks at her hands. 'This was not how I had hoped he would be repaid for all he has done for us... He had been pierced by three swords as he rushed to block the hatch when they burst inside the hold. The [others] could do little...'
You say, 'What about the others?'
Firiona Vie says 'How could they fight? Dagda still lay abed, feverish. Dabner would not leave her side as she was carried away. Galeth's legs were still detached. Thubr could not be awakened. Lyirae was struck by the hatch as it broke inward. Ognit fought [alone].'
You say, 'How did he do alone?'
Firiona Vie says, 'He did all he could... he struck out with his staff and what magic he could recall. As he was beset on all sides, the enemy carried our friends away... Ognit lit his staff aflame and kept swinging. Still more attacked. There was no one left to defend. He said the words, and fire burst around him, consuming his attackers and blazing through the hull. It [burned], as now we do...' Firiona makes a fist.
You say, 'Burned?'
Firiona Vie says 'He spoke the words of ice, and the flame died, leaving only darkness and death. He found Sedgethwait, pierced dead, with the [note] pinned to his chest by a dagger.'
You say, 'What was on the note?'
Firiona Vie's eyes are steel as she recites the message from memory. 'Firiona, we are twins being born in a beautiful act of creation. You create me. I create you. Side by side, from the forests of Kithicor to the womb of Discord, we create each other. My sister, you have created in me the wonderful gift of hatred. Now I shall create that [gift] in you.'
You say, 'What gift?'
Firiona Vie continues reciting the message. 'Your friends have come with me, eager to join the act of creation. And Calix will guide them. He also quests for purity and creation. He and I will forge your friends anew and bestow upon your heart the purity of hatred.' Firiona is quiet for a moment. 'My companions were [carried] away, and we followed.'
You say, 'What about the Servus Libertas?'
Firiona Vie says 'Apparently they are an underground group of citizens that have been advocating for change in the city. It would be wise to [speak] with them, as well as Ognit and Al`Kabor, to see what they have discovered.'
You say, 'I will speak with them...'
Firiona Vie nods. 'This is not easy, planning and plotting calmly while you imagine all the horrible things being done to those you care about... After all we have been through, all we have suffered, all Lanys has done to us -- this must end. Soon.'
You have been assigned the task 'Free Us From Ourselves'.
Lanys has captured many of Firiona's friends, and Firiona has asked you to talk with Al'Kabor, Ognit, and the locals in order to find out more about the Citadel and where Lanys might have taken Firiona's friends.

---

!

---

Speak with Al'Kabor 0/4 (Arx Mentis)
Al`Kabor stands with his arms behind his back, gazing at the architecture of the city, the multitudinous lines of magic, and the glowing citadel in the sky. 'Magnificent... I can feel the power emanating from the citadel. The flow of magic -- it is like nothing I have felt before.'
Al`Kabor says 'It must be the culmination of centuries of Combine talent and practice, the ultimate summation of their magical knowledge -- everything I have desired to learn through my studies of their civilization.'
Al`Kabor says 'Citizens speak of the citadel's leader, Calix. Some say he is great man, possessed of the vision that will lead the Combine into a new age upon Norrath. Others say he has gone mad and tries to destroy his own people with abductions and experiments. Others say he is dead, not having seen him outside the citadel in years.'
Al`Kabor says 'I say he is a man to whom I must speak. To control the greatest society of magic that has ever lived upon Norrath -- he must have knowledge worth my knowing. It is a shame he keeps company with Lanys. And it would be a greater shame to dismantle this beautiful city searching for our companions.'

---

Speak with Ognit 0/6 (Arx Mentis)
Ognit Eznertob holds out his hand. A segmented metal snake flips around and lifts up its head, looking at you with glowing eyes of magic. 'They sell these as though they were loaves of bread...' Ognit shakes his head. 'If this city weren't tarnished by Lanys' dark presence and my own stormy thoughts, I would call it wondrous.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'I had never imagined that such advanced levels of tinkering and magic could coexist, let alone exist. It took all my skills to fashion Galeth's body, and you've seen how well that has fared...'
Ognit Eznertob looks ruefully at a burn mark on his wrist, a remnant of his battle aboard the Swift Fin. 'Magic? Tinkering? How can I call myself a wizard or a tinkerer if everything I touch burns and crumbles? Al`Kabor might not be fit as a mentor, but he has been right about me so far...'
Ognit Eznertob shakes his head. 'We are not the only ones to lose our loved ones to the citadel. I spoke with a woman who said her brother once worked as a mechanic within the citadel, repairing contraptions that route magical energy. Though he used to visit the lower city often, she has not seen him in almost a year. This story is similar to ones we have heard from many people.
Ognit Eznertob says 'Perhaps that's why the Servus Libertas exists. The sister of the mechanic spoke of them, as have others. It is said they fight for the freedom and equality of all people in Arx Mentis. Through them we just might achieve the freedom of our companions...'
Ognit Eznertob says 'From what I've gathered, I think those cloaked figures in the corner to the east of here have ties to the Servus Libertas.'

---

Speak with a Servus Libertas Member 0/5 (Arx Mentis)
a Servus Libertas member says 'My \_\_\_\_\_ brethren, I have heard the questions you ask. I have heard the questions in your heart. Do you believe in the liberty of the subjugated? Do you believe in the right of a people to share in the bounty and the rule of an empire? Do you believe in the equality of all races, not merely the equality of their overlords?'
a Servus Libertas member says 'Then in your heart you wish to join the Servus Libertas. You see the ghosts of the missing: the empty chairs at the tables, the unopened stalls at the markets, the unoccupied sides of the beds. People enter the citadel, never to return.'
a Servus Libertas member says 'See the past clearly, and you shall see what the future can be. See Tsaph Katta walking among his people, commiserating with all races, acknowledging all stations. See the future of the Combine shining brightly for all people of Norrath.'
a Servus Libertas member says 'Now see the citadel floating above us, blocking out the sun and stars, shining only for Calix and those who call themselves our superiors. Then imagine the clear skies, the citadel trembling and crashing into the sea. Do you share in our imagination, in our wish?'
a Servus Libertas member says 'Then prove it to us. Praetor Janelus watches us, trying to discover our identities, trying to dismantle the Servus Libertas. We would prefer that he were the one to be dismantled. The Servus Libertas surely has a place for those who share in our wishes.

---

Kill Praetor Janelus 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Praetor Janelus circles the zone-in area. He hits for a max ~20,000 (no rampage; no flurry). Every 25 seconds, he casts "Deft Slash" (frontal AE for 57,000 dmg)._
Praetor Janelus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Praetor Janelus's corpse falls to the ground, an anguished expression twisting into a grin, 'Ethernere, I'm coming.'

---

Speak with a Servus Libertas Member 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
You say, 'Hail, a Servus Libertas member'
a Servus Libertas member nods. 'You have acted where many people would not. Already our brethren walk more freely with Janelus dead. You have done a great deed for the Servus Libertas, and now you are one of us. You are ready for the greater mysteries, the greater knowledge. The Helper shall lead the way.'
Movement catches your eye: a tall, cloaked figure waving at you near the central pyramid.

---

Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper 0/3 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper barely acknowledges your presence. He does not speak; he merely turns toward you. Dark folds of cloth recede into his hood, and you cannot see his face. You can smell him, as though he has not bathed in weeks: blood, smoke, and sweat.
The Unseen, Unheard Helper gives you a note. You unfold the note and read it. 'Remove your darkened hood. Open your eyes. See in the light that all things have flaws. See that true strength lies in the purity of purpose. See that in this purity all flaws and considerations burn away in the blinding light. What five things shall move you to purity? Soon you shall know. And when you are ready, inside the citadel the Unseen, Unheard Helper shall show you the true meaning of purity.'
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a package.

---

Speak with Firiona 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Firiona Vie reads the note you received from the Unseen, Unheard Helper. She frowns. 'I do not like how this sounds... The five guides to purity?' She looks at what you are carrying. 'I heard a commotion in the square, and you carry a package...'
Give the Helper's package to Firiona Vie 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Firiona Vie opens the package hesitantly. 'Dabner's necklace, Dagda's ring, Thubr's cracked breastplate, Lyirae's broken bow... and Galeth's foot...'

---

Speak with Firiona 0/3 (Arx Mentis)
Firiona Vie's hands tremble. 'Lanys' gifts... when I find her the only gift she will receive from me is a swift death...'
Firiona Vie closes her eyes. Her hands stop shaking. 'We must find them. We must know what is happening in the citadel and where they are. I do not trust this helper, but we must see where he leads. I will not leave my friends to the fate I suffered at the hands of Discord... or worse...'
Firiona Vie says 'Our [path] lies ever higher, above the seas and into the sky. You should speak with the nearby noctis viratum. He claims to know how to enter the citadel unharmed.'

_This solo task begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis, located at the zone-in._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "What Hate Begets" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Deep Lies the Snow" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Free Us From Ourselves" (Arx Mentis)
_Say "path" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks up at the citadel. 'Our [path] lies above us, and the nearby noctis viratum knows how to enter unharmed. We will rescue our friends who were taken from the [ship].'
You say, 'What path?'
Firiona Vie says 'Enter the citadel. Find the Unseen, Unheard Helper. Find my companions.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Beginning From an End'.
The Unseen, Unheard Helper has been offering macabre clues to the whereabouts of Firiona's friends. Follow his lead higher into the Citadel and learn what is in store for everyone.

---

!

---

Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper inside the citadel entrance 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a note. You read it. 'Look at yourself -- closely, closer still, and yet closer. Look until you disappear. All you see are pieces -- pieces of the past piled up like the snows of countless winters, like the countless dead. To know yourself, you must know the past. Only then can you know your true purpose. Seek the study. Delve into its records.'

---

Find the study inside the lower floor of the citadel, near the garden 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Pick up books in the study. Defeat the animated books to read their contents 0/4 (Arx Mentis)
_Clicking on the book triggers the spawn of one auto-aggro mob. Updates are random upon kills of the triggered mob, so you may need to kill more than four. There are two regular zone spawns (with five minute respawn timers) next to the book trigger, and the general zone population assists the animated books. The regular mobs next to the book trigger each hit for a max ~21,000 and are immune to mez. The books ("a grand tome of history") each hit for a max ~18,500 and have a lot of hitpoints. They are also immune to mez._
a grand tome of history wildly flicks its pages as a breeze scatters symbols into the air.
a grand tome of history has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a grand tome of history's corpse slams shut and lands with a thud.
You open the book to a marked page. '... Why would not our forbearers follow Seru to the moon? Why would they let the waves overcome them and remain beneath the sea, abandoning their legacy? They were afraid -- afraid of what it would take to establish an empire to rule over Norrath...'
You open the book to a marked page. '... We were not afraid of the light, of the skies, of the limitless possibilities beyond Tsaph Katta. We were not afraid of the pursuit of perfection -- tinkering, magic, life itself...'
You open the book to a marked page. '... There must be a hierarchy, a ladder of being. Not all have reached the higher rungs. Some must toil until we all may ascend...'
You open the book to a marked page. '... Arx Mentis shall stand upon their shoulders, upon their backs, and reach ever higher into the sky. And then our feet shall no longer touch the ground...'
Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper near the study 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a note. You read it. 'A spark flashes in the dark. The spark lights a fire. The fire spreads into a blaze. An idea -- it spreads from one man to an entire empire, burning with the purity of shared purpose. All other considerations are consumed, forgotten fuel for the blaze. Seek the observation deck. View the Observation Matrix.'

---

Find the Observation Deck two floors above the entrance, containing a large window 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Defeat the Keeper of the Matrix and loot the Observation Matrix 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Note: The Keeper of the Matrix is leashed to the room in which she's found. She hits for a max ~20,000; buffs herself with "Mirrored Magical Coat" (non-dispellable 80% spell reflect for 90 seconds). She is immune to mez, and there are conflicting reports of whether she is rootable/snareable and puntable (may or may not be, but probably not). As mobs in the zone have a five-minute respawn timer, you'll want to be quick about it or bring crowd control with you._
Keeper of the Matrix says 'You dare test my skills?'
Keeper of the Matrix has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Keeper of the Matrix's corpse says 'You'll never make it out... alive.'
Use the Observation Matrix and speak with the hologram it generates 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Right-clicking the item triggers the spawn of a targetable hologram. Hail it for the update._
an instructive hologram begins talking as though picking up in the middle of a memorized speech. '... Calix taught us a truth our ancestors did not know: the lower races are not pure dross to be consumed in our furnaces of industry. Precious metals lie within. They only need to be extracted...'
Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper on the Observation Deck 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a note. You read it. 'Magic is not enough. Beliefs are not enough. Power is not enough. Only purity matters. Purity itself is power, and all other weaknesses, like dross, must be burned away. Find the analysis room. Use the Observation Matrix. Read the notes.'

---

Find the Analysis Room two floors above the entrance, containing magical cages 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Use the Observation Matrix in the Analysis Room and speak with the hologram it generates 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
/loc 54, -307, 83
_Note: This hologram appears at a fixed location on the east side of the room._
an analytic hologram begins talking. '... We have identified and catalogued the races, from barbarians to dark elves, and all of those in between... Strength, night vision, resistance to disease, resistance to poison... For the progress of Arx Mentis, Calix shall bring them all together...'
Pick up the research logbook in the Analysis Room. Defend yourself against angry researchers 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Clicking on this book triggers the spawn of 1x "a trait researcher". Kill these triggered spawns until you get the update (update is not guaranteed, so you may have to kill multiple researchers). These mobs hit for a max ~18,000._
a trait researcher has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a trait researcher's corpse moans, clutching desperately at the air as nearby strands of light flicker and go out.
You read the research notes. '... The subject is stable. We have observed signs that disease resistance is present. However, ultravision and infravision have not expressed themselves. We currently have observed a 60 percent success rate in fostering the expression of these traits in subjects...'
Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper in the Analysis Room 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a note. You read it. 'Purity of purpose gestates. It gives birth. Its offspring change the world forever. Go to the medical chamber. View the record disk.'

---

Find the Medical Chamber four floors above the entrance, containing numerous beds 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Destroy the optic bot and retrieve the Record Disk 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_"an optic bot" hits for a max ~18,000 and casts a short-range frontal AE:_
Cutting Beam: Frontal AE 8', Magic (-975)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 90000
_As with the Keeper of the Matrix, this mob is leashed to the immediate area in which it's found. Loot a "Record Disk" from its corpse._
Use the Observation Matrix and Record Disk in the Medical Chamber. Speak with the recording of Calix 0/8 (Arx Mentis)
a recording of Calix says 'Look at them, Una.' The recording of Calix points to each of the bodies lying on the surgical tables, each one of a different race. 'These are your fathers and mothers, brothers and sisters.'
a recording of Una's expression is as unchanging as the real Una's. 'I don't understand. You are my father.'
a recording of Calix smiles. 'You are right. I am. But they helped make you. Everything that is good, everything I want you to be, came from them and others like them.'
a recording of Calix continues. 'All that was weak or unnecessary was left behind. What remains is perfection: You, my daughter.'
a recording of Una looks at her father. 'I am perfect?'
a recording of Calix says 'You are the summation of my purpose and the start of a new world.'
a recording of Una says, eyes unblinking, 'Is that why everyone else is so weak?'
a recording of Calix smiles again. 'Yes, Una. The weakness shall be purged. I shall teach you all you will ever need to know. And you shall be my champion, my gift to the world.'
Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper in the Medical Chamber 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
The Unseen, Unheard Helper hands you a note. You read it. 'Firiona, do you now understand? Do you know what your companions are? They are fuel for the blaze. They shall refine purity and purpose. Come, try to save them. See what we have all become.' At the bottom of the note is a crude map marking sections of the citadel.

---

Speak with Firiona outside the Citadel 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Firiona Vie crushes the note in her hand and looks up at the citadel. '[Lanys]...'
_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

_This group mission begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis, located at the zone-in._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "What Hate Begets" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Deep Lies the Snow" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Free Us From Ourselves" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of "A Beginning From an End" (Arx Mentis)
_Say "rescue" to request the task and "go" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks up at the citadel. 'Our [path] lies above us. We will rescue our friends who were taken from the [ship]. We shall not be defeated by [Lanys]!'
You say, 'Lanys?'
Firiona Vie cannot quite stop her hands from shaking. 'She would do to them what was done to us by Discord -- and worse. I was not broken by my captivity, but Lanys would break me with the captivity of my friends... it shall not [happen]'
You say, 'What will happen?'
Firiona Vie says 'We shall [storm] the citadel and rescue them. We have their locations. We must not [wait].'
You say, 'Why not wait?'
Firiona Vie says 'Lanys sends us onto the ice, assuring us it can bear our weight. I see the frigid waters beneath us, but every minute we debate brings my friends closer to the cold of death.'
You say, 'How should we storm it?'
Firiona Vie says 'We shall be the thunderheads that gather above Lanys. Our cold shall bury her in snows that pile up to the stars. First we shall [rescue] our friends. Then the storm shall begin.'
You say, 'All right, we'll rescue them...'
Firiona Vie says 'Then we shall [go].'
You have been assigned the task 'All Rivers Lead to the Sea'.
Lanys and Calix are holding Firiona's friends in the citadel, torturing and experimenting upon them. Firiona is leading the charge to save them from their fates.

---

!

---

_This is a fairly straightforward task. Hail, follow the Quest NPCs, kill mobs to rescue an NPC, repeat until finished._
Speak with Firiona 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie says, 'We divide here. Find the rooms on the map. I do not know what awaits us... Prepare yourselves. Adventurers, you are with me.'

---

Meet Firiona in the Clockwork Construction Room to look for Galeth 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Ognit Eznertob looks around at the scattered pieces of clockworks and other constructs. 'They have already broken him... Galeth's pieces must be in here somewhere...'

---

Kill the tinkerers and constructs to find out what happened to Galeth 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
_These mobs are splittable from each other and mezzable. They each hit for a max ~22,000. The plated constructs in particular have a lot of hitpoints._
Speak with Ognit about the pieces of Galeth's body 0/3 (Arx Mentis)
Ognit Eznertob says 'He's badly damaged, but I'll do as much as I can.'
Ognit Eznertob says 'If only I had more skill...'
Firiona Vie says 'Father!'
Speak with Galeth 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
Galeth Veredeth twists his limbs around and flexes his fingers. 'Still stiff, but what can a man with metal joints who has just been vivisected expect? I sail on, though my fingers may not close around the oar.'
Ognit Eznertob says, 'This is the best I can do with the time we have. It is quick and unstable. Please, Galeth, keep yourself together until I can do it properly...'
Firiona Vie looks at the cracks and gouges in Galeth's body. 'Father... what did they do to you? What did they do to our friends?'
Galeth Veredeth's eyes shine with their unchanging light. 'There is nothing they can do to me. I am beyond physical pain. The only pain I felt was that of seeing pain inflicted on our companions' flesh as Lanys' minions beat them and gave them over to the researchers. That was the last I saw of them, but still the pain lingers -- a feeling I have not recalled for a long time...'
Firiona Vie looks away. 'I am sorry I brought you from your home, back to a world of pain...'
Galeth Veredeth continues to look at his daughter. 'I never wished to feel such things again. The emotions had faded slowly over the years, and I was at rest. But Tunare has called us, and her will must be done. Only then may we return to her and rest in her peace.'
Ognit Eznertob looks at the ground. 'If only I were better at magic and tinkering, none of this would have happened. Things would be different.'
Firiona Vie says, 'If only so many things had happened differently... If only I had killed Lanys in Kithicor... If only we had both remained in our prisons for eternity. But the snows fall and feed the rivers that run into the sea...'
Firiona Vie says, 'We keep moving. We have no other choice. We find Thubr. Come.'

---

Find Thubr in the Teleporter Room 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
As you near the teleporter room, a guttural roar rips through the hallways, accompanied by the clash of steel.
Kill the guards attacking Thubr 0/3 (Arx Mentis)
_These three mobs are aggro-linked and immune to all forms of crowd control. They each hit less than standard mobs for the zone (about a max ~10,000), but have a lot of hitpoints._
Speak with Thubr 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
Thubr Axebringer is covered in blood. He shakes with rage and shock as you approach him. He almost swings the sword at your head before recognizing you. 'I killed 'em, each one of 'em, and I'll kill the rest! And this time they've all earned it...'
Thubr Axebringer's eyes are clear. He is breathing hard. 'It's nearly gone... the sin, the mistake, the flesh that brought me misery on that night...' Thubr holds up his right arm. His hand is maimed and covered in blood.
Firiona Vie reaches out. 'Thubr, your hand...'
Thubr Axebringer looks at the jagged flesh. 'Sorry, Firiona, I won't be much use to ya now...'
Firiona Vie begins binding Thubr's wound. 'Do not say that... You are alive, and that is all I ask of you.'
Firiona Vie finishes binding Thubr's wound and staunching the bleeding. 'Where do these portals lead?'
Thubr Axebringer lowers the sword and holds his injured arm. 'I stepped on 'em while fighting, but nothing happened.'
Ognit Eznertob says, 'They must be controlled by a keying system.'
Galeth Veredeth says, 'Leave them be. Let us find Lyirae.'

---

Find Lyirae at the Feeding Chambers 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
You hear a shout from the corner of the corridor, and you find Lyirae struggling to defend herself against a massive lizard.
Kill the Atrox Lacerta 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_This mob is leashed to its spawn point and sees through invisibility. It hits for a max ~23,000; summons from 100% health; single-target rampages; and casts the 35' range PBAE "Flesh Rip" (57,000 unmitigated damage). If pulled from its spawn point, it warps back, drops all aggro, and strips its debuffs._
Speak with Lyirae 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
Lyirae Oakwynd bows her head. 'You came for me... You risked your lives. You should not have...'
Lyirae Oakwynd continues. 'It is fate that robs me of my friends... Do not let it be my own safety that does so...'
Galeth Veredeth says, 'You are a drop in the stream, Lyirae, as are we all. You cannot climb upon its banks and watch us all pass
Lyirae Oakwynd looks up. 'Galeth, you are whole! Thubr... your hand!'
Thubr Axebringer grunts, 'Lost in the stream, as Galeth might say.'
Lyirae Oakwynd says, 'Where are Dagda and Dabner? Dagda was still unconscious when they dragged us into the citadel and the guards started beating us...'
Firiona Vie says, 'We'll find them -- our friends and those responsible for their suffering. Lyirae, did you see Al`Kabor pass by here?'
Lyirae Oakwynd says, 'No... Why would he pass by if you sent him to help?'
Firiona Vie scowls. 'We must find him. He has already run off once before on this journey, and the consequences were dire. I cannot have this happen again.'

---

Find and speak with Al'Kabor 0/6 (Arx Mentis)
Al`Kabor nods. 'Such an unparalleled accomplishment in the magical arts -- this citadel is the apex of learning. To destroy it or ignore all that it could teach would be a shame.'
Firiona Vie's eyes are stern. 'This is not the time. Dagda and Dabner are in danger. I do not care about what you want. Your whims and intellectual curiosities threaten us all! Have you learned nothing?!'
Al`Kabor raises an eyebrow. 'Firiona, what I learn here might save everyone. You must learn to recognize your enemies. I shall remain here and study the citadel. I trust you have enough bodies and righteous anger to save Dagda and Dabner.'
Ognit Eznertob slams his staff on the floor. 'I can't believe I ever trusted you as my teacher! You... you only think of yourself! How could you put a few books and scraps of knowledge above the lives of your friends?! I'm done trying to emulate someone like you!'
Al`Kabor says calmly. 'That is smart. You were not good at it.'
Firiona Vie looks coolly at Al`Kabor. 'You have caused nothing but trouble this entire journey. You can stay here and do what you wish. Those who actually care shall save Dagda and Dabner.'

---

Find the Cyro Chamber 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Once you enter the basement, you won't be able to exit it._
Kill the cyro operari 0/8 (Arx Mentis)
_These mobs have low aggro and assist ranges (you can pull each of them single, except for a pair in the back of the room). Each hits for a max ~20,000; is mezzable; and casts "Thick Ice" (single-target stun + nuke for 40,000 dmg)._
Speak with Dabner 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
Dabner Drednever is shivering. The blood trail beneath his nose has frozen. 'Firiona... is it you?' Dabner struggles to sit up. 'Where is Dagda?'
Dabner Drednever yells, 'Dagda!' Her skin already turning blue, Dagda lies next to Dabner, her wounded leg frozen. 'Do something! She's still breathing!'
Ognit Eznertob hastily casts a spell of warmth. The ice begins to melt, and Dagda's wounds start to bleed afresh.
Dabner Drednever begins casting the most powerful healing spells he can remember, but Dagda's wounds remain.
Dagda Icefury groans, and her eyes open halfway. Her voice is a whisper. 'Dabner, you're safe... we're all safe... safe in the warmth... just let it embrace you. Don't fear it.'
Dabner Drednever's voice quavers. 'Yes, you're safe now. Firiona is here; I'm here. We're going to get you out, back to the Swift Fin.'
Firiona Vie looks away from Dagda. 'Back to Halas, back to your home. Back to fishing, drinking, and fighting. Back to a time before I brought you to this dark place...'
Dagda Icefury clothes begin to darken with blood. Her companions can now see the cuts and slashes under her clothes, the patches of skin pierced with metal. She tries to laugh but can only sigh. 'We cannot go back... I was already too old, and now... too broken. The snow lies too deeply upon me...'
Dabner Drednever wipes away a tear and tries uselessly to stop the bleeding. 'It's not too late! We can still...'
Speak with Dabner again 0/13 (Arx Mentis)
Ognit Eznertob kneels on the other side of Dagda and tries applying pressure to her wounds. Lyirae and Firiona tend to Dagda's leg, but the dying flesh is cold to the touch.
Dabner Drednever is silent. His eyes do not leave Dagda's face. 'Dagda, you know I love you. I always have. I... can't let you die without hearing it from my lips...'
Dagda Icefury's eyes open all the way. They are bright, like a river in sunlight. 'I know... You are my brother, Dabner... and you, Ognit...' She clasps their hands. 'You know I loved you both... as family... I was your shield, and you my light...'
Dabner Drednever's tears begin falling. 'I'm sorry... I ran from you... I couldn't do more... I couldn't...'
Ognit Eznertob shouts 'Apply more pressure! Cast another spell!'
Firiona Vie shouts 'Keep talking to us, Dagda!'
Lyirae Oakwynd shouts 'Dagda, don't close your eyes! Don't!'
Thubr Axebringer shouts 'Strength! You were always the best fighter of us two!'
Galeth Veredeth says calmly, 'Dagda, the river flows, and you are a part of it. It carries you. Feel its warmth. It carries you to the sea, where we will all meet you again someday, my friend.'
Dagda Icefury's eyes struggle open. 'I... always wanted to die with a sword in my hand. But... now I have found something better... to die with my family's hands in mine...' Dagda closes her eyes.
Dabner Drednever's tears stream down his face. 'Dagda! Dagda...' He and Ognit continue holding Dagda's hands, even after her hands no longer hold theirs.
Lyirae Oakwynd shakes her head. Another lost beneath the waves...
Firiona Vie stands up. She cannot keep her vision from blurring. 'Yes, friend, someday together again in that vast sea...'

---

Speak with the Unseen, Unheard Helper 0/5 (Arx Mentis)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_Be ready for a fight._
You read out loud the note the helper hands to you. 'Firiona, how do you feel at this moment? Have you received my gift? Do you feel the hatred? Perhaps you will when all your friends are dead -- and your family, and your people, and your god...'
You continue reading. 'You are nothing without hate. Our lives have been guided by it, shaped by it. You despise it, but it has made you. Now you shall embrace it.'
You continue reading. 'You see this man before you, unseen and unheard? He is the hatred that knocks at your heart. He is the figure that creeps up to your windows at night and raps on the panes, searching for a way inside.'
You continue reading. 'He selected the blades and the metals, made the incisions in Dagda's flesh, loosed the poisons into her body, and made her a gift for you. He can no longer speak or see, but since when does hatred rely upon the senses?'
You continue reading. 'Now I give him to you: another gift for my twin!'
_Holmein the Hated appears at its location and immediately aggro's._
Kill Holmein the Hated 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Holmein the Hated has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Holmein the Hated twitches as his blood pools around him. Before he dies he dips his finger in the blood and writes, 'Citadel Apex.'
_Holmein the Hated hits for a max ~24,000 and AE rampages. Every 25 seconds, he casts the spell "Tainted Blood" on a random target (targeted 15' range AE for 75,000 dmg + DOT for 8,333/tick). Every 25 seconds, he casts "Open Wound" on a random target (nuke for 61,000 dmg + debuff that increase "Tainted Blood" nuke to 105,000 dmg)._

---

Speak with Firiona 0/9 (Arx Mentis)
_Hail her until the task completes._
Firiona Vie watches Holmein until his body stops twitching and his chest stops rising. She turns to Dagda and feels the tears start to form again. Dabner is weeping. 'Lanys will die for this, just as Holmein has.'
Galeth Veredeth looks at Firiona with his unchanging eyes. 'Do not forget that you are the Champion of Balance.'
Firiona Vie's eyes are hard. 'I will not forget anything.'
Dabner Drednever's tears fall. 'We must take her back to the Swift Fin, back to Halas... We can't leave her in this place... Need to give her a funeral... a nice one, like you had, Firiona...'
Firiona Vie looks around at her companions: Dabner crying inconsolably, Lyirae staring into the distance, Thubr's wound beginning to bleed again. 'Thubr and Lyirae, take Dabner and Dagda back through the portal, to the ship. Sail back to Brother Island, away from here. That's an order!'
Ognit Eznertob says, 'Firiona?'
Firiona Vie looks at Ognit. 'You can still fight?'
Ognit Eznertob nods. 'Yes.'
Firiona Vie says, 'Good. You and Galeth are coming with me. We are bringing down the citadel -- and Lanys with it. Let us [leave].'
_A chest with loot also spawns at this time. The task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
_Rewards:_
112 platinum, 5 gold
Experience
_Possible Chest Loot (1 item + 0-1 armor templates + 0-1 Briny Essence 0-1 Valiant ornament):_
Arx Medallion
Buffer of the Quastori
Core of Caldarius
Romance's Runic Rampart
Sparking Bauble
Highwater Armguards
Highwater Boots
Highwater Bracer
Highwater Gloves
Highwater Helm
Highwater Leggings
Highwater Tunic
Median Briny Essence
Greater Briny Essence

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Gregor Olmstead ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple. He is found at /loc 110, -345, 6 (east-central coastal area of the island)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "hold" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Gregor Olmstead'
Gregor Olmstead says 'Keep your head down \_\_\_\_\_! It can't have escaped your notice that the wind's blowin' up a gale today! Well today and every other day that I'm stuck on this accursed island. There's precious little of my sanity left after weeks of dodging flying rocks and angry reef crawlers. Any longer here and I fear this keen mind I hold dear will finally give out. Be that as it were, you're a sight for sore eyes. I have a plan to get out of here, but I need a partner, which is where [you] come in.'
You say, 'What do you mean, 'you'?'
Gregor Olmstead says 'As you can see, I've managed to put together a seaworthy vessel from the various commodities at my disposal on this island. I must have sailed away from this quagmire a dozen times already, but the blasted storm keeps spinning me around and bringing me back here. Now then, I can't imagine you're too familiar with nautical terms, being a land lubber and all, but every large vessel carries with it a navigation device called a 'compass'. I'll spare you the details, but suffice it to say it'll help me get off this rock. An individual of such discerning perceptions will have certainly noticed that rather large shipwreck nearby. It's certain to have one of these 'compasses' somewhere in its [hold].'
You say, 'Its hold?'
Gregor Olmstead says 'Aye, somewhere in the bowels of the great slain beast lies the ticket to my...our salvation \_\_\_\_\_! If I were you, I'd start by searching the crates in the stern of the ship. If it's not there, then one of the crew must have it. I suppose it's possible that the compass rests in the icy embrace of Prexus' depths, but let's be honest, we both know my luck's too good for that to be true.'
You have been assigned the task 'Of Pirates and Men'.
Gregor Olmstead needs a compass in order to escape from the island of Tempest Temple. He believes there might be one to be found in the haunted shipwreck.

---

Break open the crates in the ship's cargo hold 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
Check the cursed pirates for the compass 0/10 (Tempest Temple)
Find the ship's navigator 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_The ship is northeast of Gregor Olmstead's location and full of undead. You'll be dealing with some awkward mob pathing here. There are damp crates scattered throughout; all you need to do for those is /open them (no tricks)._
_Kill 10x "a cursed pirate" while you're at it. Note: The "a sea banshee" mobs in this area proc a stun and are immune to stuns, but mezzable. Upon the 10th kill, you receive "Old Metallic Compass" on your cursor._
_"Navigator Henry" is a zombie that spawns on the ship's deck (the western half of the broken ship's topside), doesn't appear to be any more difficult than standard mobs (stunnable and mezzable). Killing this mob updates the "ship's navigator" step._

---

Give the Old Metallic Compass to Gregor Olmstead 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
Gregor Olmstead's normally placid stare takes a turn for the strange as you hand over the compass. His lips curl and he begins to cackle evilly, 'Finally! The compass of the ancient Combine sailors is mine! They all said I was crazy to try and find you, but who's the crazy one now DAD?! This compass will lead me to the land of riches beyond the dark horizon. I will go there and find my fortune and rule as did the kings of old! The Combine, the Shissar, the Rallosians, all will fade from history in the shadow of the grandeur that will be my golden empire! Fear not Gregor Olmstead, for I will remember your service when I come into my seat of power, and woe be to thee who opposes the will of their lord and master!' He continues to ramble on for quite a while, but you mostly stop listening and stare at him with a mixed look of befuddlement and apprehension.

---

Speak with Gregor Olmstead 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Gregor Olmstead'
Gregor Olmstead snaps his head and glares at you with undisguised contempt. 'What! How dare you interrupt the pontification of your lord! The heavens shall rain down terrible vengeance upon those who fail to respect the absolute authority of my divine glory!' He raises the old compass above his head and continues to yell at you as the wind begins to quicken. Soon the looming encroachment of thunder rolls through the air as you feel the prickle of static skitter across your skin. A massive bolt of lightning parts the clouds and strikes.
Gregor Olmstead died.
_(Oops.)_
_Rewards:_
Experience
90 platinum

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the first in a series of three. It begins with Jessup Stormwind ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple. He is located at -340, -280, -156 (on the elevated island in the southeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "constitution" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Jessup Stormwind'
Jessup Stormwind says 'Hail and well met \_\_\_\_\_. Have you come to embrace the chaos of the natural order and bear witness to the mighty storm that the kedge have summoned to this place? Truly an awe inspiring example of the power of the tempest lord, Karana. So tragic that its orchestrators must be struck down, for the methods by which they created this tempest are far from natural. While I accept that the kedge must be dealt with, it does not fall to me and mine to terminate their offense. Instead I will focus on the other side effects of their meddling, namely, the sea naga and their ill kept goblin minions. For while the storm rages, their [malicious intrusions] continue unabated.'
You say, 'Malicious intrusions?'
Jessup Stormwind says 'Indeed \_\_\_\_\_, I could not have said it better myself. As you no doubt know, the coral forest and the rest of the shallows on the far side of the isle were raised by the kedge to provide an environment that they were more fond of, though they seemed to have largely abandoned it to the meddling of the naga. This had the unfortunate side effect of granting the naga access to coral and other deep sea creatures that they would normally be unable to obtain from the depths. It didn't take them long to plunder the reef and throw its ecosystem into disarray. There's much work to be done \_\_\_\_\_, if you believe you have the [constitution] for it.'
You say, 'I have the constitution for it...'
Jessup Stormwind says 'Excellent. We'll begin with goblins in the shallows. You'd be amazed at how much damage their stampeding feet can do in large numbers. Kill as many of them as you can find. Additionally, while you're surveying the reef, take note of the state of the coral. There has certainly been much damage done to them, and I'll need some scouting information if I'm to devise a way to undo the naga's wrecklessness.'
You have been assigned the task 'Coral Crushers'.
Jessup Stormwind wants you to make the goblins pay for the damage they've done to the coral and surrounding ecosystem. Put an end to their destruction and assess the damage to the coral.

---

Kill goblin shamans 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
Kill goblin warriors 0/10 (Tempest Temple)
Inspect a red coral plant 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
Inspect a green coral plant 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
Inspect a yellow coral plant 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Goblins are found in the far western part of the zone._
_Coral plants are strewn about this same area. Red and Yellow coral is partially submerged while Green coral seems to be on the little islands. Spawn points might be random, but here are a few map locations for "tempesttemple.txt" map files:_
P -684.9475, -143.2248, 2.3793, 0, 127, 127, 1, yellow\_coral
P -648.4603, -47.1860, 1.6401, 0, 127, 127, 1, yellow\_coral
P -640.2911, -27.0615, 1.5328, 0, 127, 127, 1, red\_coral
P -641.9035, 65.9698, 6.4391, 0, 127, 127, 1, green\_coral
P -540.3406, 117.5983, 1.4587, 0, 127, 127, 1, red\_coral
P -728.4587, 292.9606, 8.6677, 0, 127, 127, 1, green\_coral
_This isn't an exhaustive list - they're scattered all over. No tricks here; just /inspect them:_
The coral lies broken and mangled. It seems as if the goblins were harvesting something from it, as there are many missing branches.
This coral tree is incredibly vibrant and much larger than any of the others you've encountered. It seems to pulse with energy, almost as if it has been infused with magic.
This coral has been trampled and smashed. Hardly any of it could be considered intact.

---

Speak with Jessup Stormwind 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Jessup Stormwind'
Jessup Stormwind smirks as you recount the tale of goblin genocide and the state of the reef. 'Such is the justice of nature \_\_\_\_\_. Pray that you never fall victim to her ire. Though it be very odd that the green coral has not seen the same damage as the other species. I can only conclude that the goblins and the nagas have some reason for not trampling it. From the traces of magic in the coral you felt it seems likely that the hags are empowering them somehow. Though I am usually grateful for any creature that assists with the cultivation of the wild, I have a [bad feeling] about this.
_Rewards:_
Experience
90 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the second in a series of three. It begins with Jessup Stormwind ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple. He is located at -340, -280, -156 (on the elevated island in the southeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "Coral Crushers"
_Say "hand" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Jessup Stormwind smirks as you recount the tale of goblin genocide and the state of the reef. 'Such is the justice of nature \_\_\_\_\_. Pray that you never fall victim to her ire. Though it be very odd that the green coral has not seen the same damage as the other species. I can only conclude that the goblins and the nagas have some reason for not trampling it. From the traces of magic in the coral you felt it seems likely that the hags are empowering them somehow. Though I am usually grateful for any creature that assists with the cultivation of the wild, I have a [bad feeling] about this.
You say, 'A bad feeling?'
Jessup Stormwind says 'The naga are clearly up to something nefarious with their magically infused coral. I can't say definitively, but I suspect it may have something to do with the rather large ocean serpents that have been wandering through the shallows lately. From prior experience I can tell you that they are decidedly larger than they ought normally to be. Perhaps the nagas are feeding them the magically infused coral. That would be odd, as the serpents normally subsist on a diet of fish and small crustaceans. Which reminds me, those crabs down below are a bit massive too aren't they? Never mind that for now, back to the matter at [hand].'
You say, 'What's at hand?'
Jessup Stormwind says 'Even if the nagas' motivations are altruistic, the presence of so much coral of a single species is not good for the ecology of the reef. I'm loathe to say it, but the best course of action seems to be for you to crush some of that coral. I doubt that will be enough to put an end to it though. You'll also need to thin the ranks of those nagas significantly. If you could remove them from the situation my instincts tell me that the goblins would break ranks and stop disrupting the reef. Be careful though, if any of them are using that coral for nefarious purposes you could be in for a rude awakening.'
You have been assigned the task 'So Many Serpents'.
Jessup Stormwind wants your help destroying some of the overabundant green coral in the shallows around the Tempest Temple. As the nagas are the source of the real problem, he also wants you to thin out their ranks and keep an eye out for anything that might provide some information about their larger plan.
Destroy green coral plants. You'll likely attract nearby serpents when you do, so be careful.

---

Crush green coral plants and kill small serpents 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
Slay several sea nagas 0/10 (Tempest Temple)
Collect infused coral extracts from the nagas 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
_Head back to the western part of the zone. Note that sea nagas are stunnable and rootable/snareable, but immune to mez._
_When you 'kill' a green coral, "a small serpent" spawns at its location. This mob has to be killed for updates. It has less hitpoints than the average and is mezzable, stunnable, rootable, and snareable. It also flees at low health._
a green coral plant crumbles beneath your attack, as a small sea serpent emerges from the nearby water to attack!
a small serpent weaves forward with lightning speed.
_NOTE: "Infused Coral Extract" is the item you're looking for. This drops from the sea nagas, but may only have a chance to drop if everyone in your group is on the same task and step. If this is the case, you can get around this issue by having anyone who does NOT have this task disband from the group before a mob dies. The item 'seed' check is at the time of death._

---

Bring the infused coral extracts to Jessup Stormwind 0/3 (Tempest Temple)
Jessup Stormwind carefully peers through the green liquid as he taps lightly on the side of the glass vial. 'A potent growth enchantment has been cast on this coral for certain, \_\_\_\_\_. They seem to have extracted all the nutrients of the coral into this liquid.' He pauses from his study of the vial to look at you before continuing, 'You say the serpents came to aid the nagas when you attacked them? This must be the reason for this new 'alliance' of sorts. This can't be the end game though. The serpents, though larger still do not pose a significant threat to the rest of the islands' inhabitants. Unless of course there's some kind of even bigger [monstrosity] that they've been feeding?'
_Rewards:_
Experience
90 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
