---

_This shared task (1-6 players) is the third in a series of three. It begins with Jessup Stormwind ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple. He is located at -340, -280, -156 (on the elevated island in the southeastern part of the zone)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completed "Coral Crushers"
\- Completed "So Many Serpents"
_Say "monstrosity" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Jessup Stormwind carefully peers through the green liquid as he taps lightly on the side of the glass vial. 'A potent growth enchantment has been cast on this coral for certain, \_\_\_\_\_. They seem to have extracted all the nutrients of the coral into this liquid.' He pauses from his study of the vial to look at you before continuing, 'You say the serpents came to aid the nagas when you attacked them? This must be the reason for this new 'alliance' of sorts. This can't be the end game though. The serpents, though larger still do not pose a significant threat to the rest of the islands' inhabitants. Unless of course there's some kind of even bigger [monstrosity] that they've been feeding?'
You say, 'What monstrosity?'
Jessup Stormwind says 'The vials of infused coral essence must serve some higher purpose. Here, take this collection of vials and place them on the ring of druidic runes on the beach near the giant nautilus shell. The runes will amplify the scent of the coral and draw out whatever creatures the nagas are controlling with it. Obviously you should be ready for anything. If there's something out there in deeper water that they've been cajoling with it's likely to be large.'
_You receive "Coral Extract Vial Bundle"._
You have been assigned the task 'Bigger Fish'.
Jessup Stormwind believes that the nagas have been growing a much larger sea serpent off in the depths beyond the island. Use the infused coral vials to tempt it towards the island and survive the assault.

---

Place the infused vials on the druid circle 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
_An object called "a druid circle" ( **click for map image**) is found on the southwestern part of the island._
_Here, you'll need to kill off 40-50 serpents that approach the druid circle two at a time (waves are separated in intervals beginning slowly, then building up more frequently). You'll need a fair amount of DPS to deal with this - likely a full group of active players. A mezzer helps._
_When ready, turn in the "Coral Extract Vial Bundle" to the druid circle._
a druid circle begins to glow softly as you set the vials down in the circle. It isn't long before you begin to hear muffled hissing creeping towards you over the droning sounds of the wind and waves.

---

Survive the serpent assault 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Shortly after the event is started, your first two mobs arrive: 2x "an enraged serpent". Then another two follow. And then more. And more. And a lot more._
_Two known mob types:_
\- "an enraged serpent" (lower-than-average health & max hit; mezzable/stunnable)
\- "a hungry serpent" (normal health & max hit for the base zone population; mezzable/stunnable)
_At some point, you'll get a named (Falindar), which is tougher. Once Falindar appears, focus your DPS on it because the serpent adds keep spawning. Everything despawns once Falindar dies._
_Emotes you'll see signaling the spawn of more mobs:_
The waves hitting the beach swell as several larger serpents approach.
A louder cacophony of shrieks rumbles across the water.
_If any mobs are left idle for any longer than about 60 seconds, the event despawns and you have to start over._
_If you fail it, you can get a new vial bundle by speaking with Jessup Stormwind again._
Jessup Stormwind says 'Be more careful this time, \_\_\_\_\_. There aren't many vials left.'

---

Speak with Jessup Stormwind 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._

## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Where:**

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

**About Heroic Adventures:**
\- They use the task system (steps are shown in task window, but steps are only revealed as you get to them).
\- Scale in difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels (based on your group's highest level player - as low as 85).
\- Tuned for 2-3 players and their mercenaries.
\- Nameds can spawn as you progress through the task (you usually have to complete a few steps before this can happen).
\- Collection items can drop from mobs in the instances, and ground spawns appear midway through tasks.
\- Expected time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- There is some variations/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
**About Our Documentation of Heroic Adventures**
\- We only display task steps we know about.
\-\-\- If you encounter a step that isn't documented here, please post the details (or email us) and **be sure to indicate which step number it was** so that we can display it in its proper place.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Petran Searake ( **click for map image**) in Tempest Temple (located at the dock on the eastern coast)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "fish" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
Kill the crawlers and walkers that infest the coral reef 0/18
\-\-\- Kill update.
Stop the sea goblins 0/22
\-\-\- Kill update.
Banish the restless undead 0/20
\-\-\- Kill update.
**Step #2:**
Silence the hideous sea nagas 0/9
\-\-\- Kill update.
Still the restless elementals 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
Disrupt the chaotic dervishes 0/9
\-\-\- Kill update in the path in the middle of the island.
**Step #3:**
Kill a few coral or ocean serpents 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update.
Take out the sirens or their seduced pirates 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update.
Crush a few reef crabs 0/6
\-\-\- Kill update.
Knock some sense into the Wardens and their Treants 0/5
\-\-\- Kill update on the floating island. (Warning: These mobs are a lot tougher than general zone population.)
Kill 5 Kedge 0/5
\-\-\- Kill update in the path through the mountain.
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
Speak to Petran Searake 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update to finish.

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Serisaria" (or its PH, "a lost siren") spawns after the third step is completed. This mob appears near the entrance to the Caverns of Endless Song (far northern part of the zone in the coral reef)._
_Known loot from this named:_
Level 105
Castaway Bracer
Fused Coral Staff
Serisaria's Pearl Stud
Level 100
???
Level 95
???
Level 90
???

---

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Petran Searake'
Petran Searake sighs, 'Seems your luck is as bad as mine, stranger. You without direction and me without a [catch].'
You say, 'No catch?'
Petran Searake chuckles, 'The weather being what it is, I can't rightly expect there to be an abundance catch, but I haven't caught a [fish] in days now. I suspect what little sea life remains out there is being caught up by the miserable beasts on the isle. Interested in reducing my competition? I may not have fish but I should be able to scrounge up some suiting reward for you.'
You say, 'I'll get you some fish'
Petran Searake says 'Excellent, just tell me when you're [ready] to go.'
You have been assigned the task 'Storm of Sorts'.
Petran Searake has had miserable luck catching any fish lately. He suspects his luck will improve if you reduce his competition around the island.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Kill the crawlers and walkers that infest the coral reef 0/18 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ The crawlers and walkers that infest the reef to the north are far more efficient fisherman than Petran. Take a few of them out so that the fish can survive their trip to the east side of the island.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
=====================
Stop the sea goblins 0/22 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ The sea goblins to the west are uniquely adept at fishing and Petran suspects that they are his primary competition. Go reduce their population to improve his chance of finding dinner.
_it's easiest to pull them to the beach. If you try from the ocean side you can often get huge numbers of adds._
_They summon!_
_But if you keep pulling back, they will eventually follow._
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
=====================
Banish the restless undead 0/20 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ Petran has a hard time concentrating on his fishing duties with the moaning and groaning of the banshees and zombies that haunt the shipwreck just north of his perch. Reduce their numbers so he can focus on his work.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Silence the hideous sea nagas 0/9 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ The hideous nagas that intermingle with the sea goblins on the west end of the island are proving to be a problem. Petran is unsure whether the goblins are in service to these sea hags or if the snakelike women are subservient to the smarmy ocean callers. Either way, reducing their numbers can only be advantageous to improving Petran's luck in getting a bite.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
=====================
Still the restless elementals 0/10 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ The unyielding storm that engulfs this island has empowered the previously dormant elementals. These creatures focus the powers of lava and air and make the area inhospitable to sea life. Take out either the air elementals that feed the wild winds or the lava elementals that boil the stilled ponds.
_If you follow the coast around to the southwest from where you get the quest you'll find enough lava spirits to do this step. They are an easy pull once you move in close enough to avoid the other mobs._
_If You Get This Step: Kill update - "a lava spirit" updates this, as does "an air spirit"._
=====================
Disrupt the chaotic dervishes 0/9 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill update - the dervishes in the pathway through the mountain._
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Kill a few coral or ocean serpents 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
=====================
Take out the sirens or their seduced pirates 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ On the north end of the island is a small band of sirens and the weak-willed pirates that have fallen prey to their alluring song. Clearly these invaders sustain themselves on something and Petran suspects that their diet consists primarily of fish. Turn these creatures into churn to improve Petran's odds of finally catching his own dinner.
_If you go to the far north at the coral barrier, you can pull the seduced pirates to there._
_If You Get This Step: Kill update - any "a seduced \_\_\_\_\_" mob counts towards this step._
=====================
Crush a few reef crabs 0/6 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ On the south end of the island is a group of reef crabs that seem suspiciously well fed. Petrans wonders if reducing their population would increase the population of catchable fish.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update._
=====================
Knock some sense into the Wardens and their Treants 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill update on the floating island. Warning: These mobs are a lot tougher than the general zone population. They tend to hit for the mid-20,000s range._
=====================
Kill 5 Kedge 0/5 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ As far as Petran can tell, the unceasing storm that surrounds this island is the work of the kedge arcanists that have taken refuge near the center of the island. Take a few of them out in hopes that it improves the weather and by extension the willingness for schools of delicious fish to swim near the island.
_If You Get This Step: Kill update in the path through the mountain._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Speak to Petran Searake 0/1 (Tempest Temple)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Petran Searake and let him know that you have done everything he has requested of you.
_If You Get This Step: Hail Petran at the zone-in to complete._
You say, 'Hail, Petran Searake'
Petran Searake smiles contentedly, 'Fine work, \_\_\_\_\_. I'm due for a fine haul now. You're welcome to [leave] whenever you please.'
Petran Searake has high hopes that fish will start biting soon!

---

**TASK REWARDS**
\- 19-21 "Pieces of Eight" (group currency)
1-2% XP for level 99
- Piece of Eight [item=120483]

_This solo task begins with Alchemist Hesia ( **click for map image**) in Katta Castrum: Deluge. She's found at /loc 90, -70, 11 (in the northeastern corner of the temple's main floor)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "saved" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Alchemist Hesia'
Alchemist Hesia blinks at you with a vacant look in her eyes. She's clearly exhausted. 'Can't talk... have to organize these [books]. Don't want to lose any more.'
You say, 'What books?'
Alchemist Hesia says 'Yes, so many books lost. Books full of...words. The shissar took over the library. Who knows what they'll manage to steal from its tomes. So much knowledge in those pages. It must be [saved].'
You say, 'Saved?'
Alchemist Hesia says 'There is much of the magical arts that the shissar have not yet mastered. If they're allowed to learn all the secrets of the library... Everyone else seems so preoccupied with staying alive! Which I suppose is understandable.... Not us though, right \_\_\_\_\_? We can see the forest for the trees! You'll help me keep the tomes out of the shissar hands, right? By the way, you know how to swim, right...?'
You have been assigned the task 'Library Aquatic'.
Alchemist Hesia needs you to recover tomes from the royal library.
The shissar have taken over the building, so be careful. You'll need to go diving to reach the teleporter inside of the library.

---

!

---

Salvage whatever books you can from the combine library 0/10 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Loot 10x "Water Logged Tome" from shissar mobs in the library (common drop), or pick them up as ground spawns._
_Four ground spawns in the library (about a two-minute respawn timer):_
\- bottom floor, far northeast corner
\- bottom floor, far southwest corner
\- top floor, far northwest corner
\- top floor, far southeast corner
_If you don't have the task, you may pre-loot the books if someone else in your group is at this step of the task._

---

Deliver the salvaged tomes to Alchemist Hesia 0/10 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Speak with Alchemist Hesia 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
You say, 'Hail, Alchemist Hesia'
Alchemist Hesia nods happily at the stack of wet books you hand over to her. 'The Combine owes you a great deal \_\_\_\_\_. The shissar have been denied at least a little of the knowledge of the library.' A fairly large puddle is beginning to form at your feet as the books continue to drip. Hesia says, 'I suppose I should decide how I'm going to dry these....'
_Rewards:_
Experience
22 platinum, 5 gold

---

_This solo task begins with Tactician Sodut ( **click for map image**) in Katta Castrum: Deluge. He's found at /loc 265, -15, 1 (just down the ramp extending out of the temple)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "help" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Tactician Sodut'
Tactician Sodut says 'Wait, what are you doing here!? You're from the surface! How did you get down here, none of the portals are working. Nevermind, we'll discuss it later. Right now I need your [help] before the whole city collapses into the sea! By the gods, where's Captain Parvus! We could really use him right now.'
You say, 'I will help'
Tactician Sodut says 'All the mages are working desperately to keep the dome over our heads together, and that's left all of their supposed minions to run amok through the city. We're barely keeping them and those blasted shissar out of the temple, I don't have the men to run them down across the city. \_\_\_\_\_, we need you to go out there and stop them before they turn the city to rubble.'
You have been assigned the task 'Elemental Rage'.
The elementals have gone rogue and they're attacking anyone they encounter while destroying the city. Destroy as many as you can before they cause more deaths.

---

Kill rogue elementals 0/10 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
_Any elementals count towards this step. (Note that "a darkwater eddy" mobs don't classify as elementals.)_

---

Speak with Tactician Sodut 0/1 (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
Tactician Sodut says 'Well done, \_\_\_\_\_! There are now far fewer elementals on the rampage. Although I fear it may not matter if we can't find a way to fix the [real problem].'
_Rewards:_
Experience
52 platinum, 5 gold

---

_This solo task is the second in a series of two begins with Orson Ragweed ( **click for map image**) in the Caverns of Endless Song. He is located in the small area above water just south of the underwater zone-in tunnel._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Friend in Need"
_Say "captain" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Orson Ragweed says 'You found them both and freed them? But where are they? What!? After all that they didn't even come to say thank you? Just like a pir...merchant to not have any loyalty. Ah well, at least I'm free of that awful captain. WHAT?!?! He's still alive and he's got my chronometer? Well that I just can't abide \_\_\_\_\_. We have to free [Julia] from his entranced clutches.'
You say, 'Who is Julia?'
Orson Ragweed says 'What kind of no good, lilly livered pir...trader takes another man's time piece! It's indecent I tell you \_\_\_\_\_! And just plain ill mannered. I'm sure he hasn't cleaned the salty air from poor Julia in weeks! I know I already owe you one \_\_\_\_\_, but if you could see yer way to helping me out once more I'd be eternally grateful. If you can find the [captain] somewhere in these accursed caverns you can get back Julia for me.'
You say, 'The captain?'
Orson Ragweed says 'If I know the captain, and I know him better than most, he'll be wherever the leader of this den keeps her treasure. Captain was never one to shy away from a good pile of gold, or other shiny baubles. He's probably gone and given away our whole cargo to the blasted sea witch. But wait...if he has that means...JULIA! The damnable whore wench of a siren might have her! Oh my poor girl! Go and find her \_\_\_\_\_, and quickly!'
You have been assigned the task 'Oh Julia'.
Orson Ragweed is distraught over the theft and misuse of his trusted time piece which he calls 'Julia'. Helm him rescue Julia from the depths of the Caverns of Endless Song.

---

!

---

Beat some information out of dear old Captain Janesworth 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Task Window Says:_ One of Orson's mates mentioned that Captain Janesworth had Julia last he saw him. Find the captain and beat some information out of him.
_Captain Janesworth is found up the spiral ramp due north of the zone-in. He hits for a max ~20,000 (no special abilities)._
Captain Janesworth says 'Avast! I see more sparklies to be giving to the mistress! Draw yer weapons and prepare to meet yer maker!'
Captain Janesworth has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Captain Janesworth's corpse crumples to the ground with a thud. You notice a small golden chain hanging out of his breeches.

---

Take the broken golden chain from the captain's corpse 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Captain Janesworth drops 6x "Broken Golden Chain"._

---

Bring the broken golden chain back to Orson Ragweed 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Orson Ragweed breaks down and weeps when you show him the broken chain. 'Whyyyy Prexus whyyy! Julia's honor has been besmirched by these hooligans! But where could the rest of her be? The captain must have given her to the sirens hoping for a little more of their lewd company. I hope that she devil cooks him on a spit and then chokes on his bones! We have to find whatever's left of Julia, \_\_\_\_\_. I can't let her body lie in a place like this for all eternity. She must have ended up with the rest of the treasure that was taken from our hold. Search the crates and chests that are certainly hidden somewhere in the tunnels. Save her from this cursed fate \_\_\_\_\_! Save my beautiful girl!

---

Search the cargo crates in the tunnel for 'Julia' 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Task Window Says:_ Search for Julia in the cargo crates scattered across the caverns.
_Cargo crates are found throughout the western cavern. /open these until you receive "Orson's Pocket Watch"._
You rummage through the cargo crate for a time. You find nothing of interest but some fish that are beginning to turn.
You rummage through the cargo crate for a time. At the bottom under a pile of straw, wedged between a pair of flounder is a shiny gold pocket watch. You pop it open to inspect the engraving. Sure enough the inside is inscribed with large flowing letters, 'Julia'.
Loot an ancient idol 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_While you're opening cargo crates, you'll also need a "Water Logged Stone Idol"._
You rummage through the cargo crate for a time. You don't find a pocketwatch, but there's a strange looking stone idol that looks intriguing. You pocket the idol and go continue on your way.

---

Bring Julia back to Orson Ragweed 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Orson Ragweed's eyes light up as you show him the pocket watch. 'Oh Julia! I thought I'd never see you again! I'd come to terms with you having gone to a watery grave, but I couldn't stand the thought of that vile captain or his fishy seductress having their festering mitts all over you. \_\_\_\_\_, Julia and I are in your debt, and we're eternally grateful. What's that bit of stone you have as well? Oh yes that's part of the Combine tre...trade goods we were carrying when we ran aground. That intense fellow [Horace] was asking me about those things when he showed up here not long ago. He'll definitely be interested in it. Tell you what \_\_\_\_\_, you can keep the idol for no charge, and we'll call it square for rescuing Julia for me. Deal? Deal!
You say, 'Horace?'
Orson Ragweed says 'Aye, he's pacing back and forth over there. He says he's from one of the communes on a nearby island. Something about it being almost all men, and all they do is fast, train and meditate all day and night. Sounds like someplace that could use a bit of livening up, and I'm just the gnome to do it!'

---

Take the stone idol to Horace the Resolved 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Horace the Resolved roams the zone-in area._
You say, 'Hail, Horace the Resolved'
Horace the Resolved says 'Leave me be \_\_\_\_\_. I am on a holy quest for my brothers into this cursed den of salaciousness and carnality.'
_Upon turn-in:_
Horace the Resolved says 'Go away \_\_\_\_\_, I don't have time for senseless prattle. Hold a moment...where did you get that statue? It's one of the ancient Combine artifacts! Exactly what I came here to find. Quickly \_\_\_\_\_, tell me where you found it so that I may continue my quest to retrieve these holy artifacts.'

---

Smack around some of Orson's former shipmates 0/10 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Task Window Says:_ Orson also wouldn't mind if you smacked around a few of his old ship mates while you're at it.
_This step is open from the beginning of the task. You'll probably have killed at least 10 mobs by now, but any "a seduced \_\_\_\_\_" counts towards this step._
_Rewards:_
Experience
112 platinum, 5 gold

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the first in a series of three begins with Ellen Kwiklen ( **click for map image**) in the Caverns of Endless Song. She is located in the small area above water just south of the underwater zone-in tunnel._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "food chain" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Ellen Kwiklen'
Ellen Kwiklen says 'Can you feel it \_\_\_\_\_? The angst and tension in the air? As if a great storm is about to break and deluge the world in an angry torrent of cleansing water and lightning. The kedge of the Tempest Temple may be extreme in their methods, but at least they understand that sometimes chaos is the [natural order] of things.'
You say, 'Natural order?'
Ellen Kwiklen says 'Yes...I just said that. Anyway. The order here has been disturbed. The sirens' presence in these caverns has sent the prior inhabitants into a stupefied haze of enraged anger. As if the magic of their songs has muddled their instincts through long exposure. The order must be restored \_\_\_\_\_, here, and everywhere. Will you help me heal the wounds of this [defiled sanctuary]?'
You say, 'Defiled sanctuary?'
Ellen Kwiklen says 'I...wait...is that a yes? I can't tell with you. You just keep repeating my words back to me. I'm going to go out on a limb and assume you'll help me. If we're going to fix the natural order here we'll have to start at the bottom and work our way up. Of the [food chain] that is.'
You say, 'Food chain?'
Ellen Kwiklen sighs heavily. 'It's like listening to my own voice echoing off the cave walls. In any case, the first thing we need to do is get the animals back following their instincts, and for most of the creatures here that means hunting. Normally I'd smack you over the head for killing animals, but we need to jump start their instincts. Collect a stack of spikeback meat, you'll need quite a few good sized pieces. Next you'll need to take those steaks and set them on hornback beetle dens. That should draw them out and remind them of their carrion instincts. Be ready for them to be rather ravenous. If they try to make you their next meal, don't hesitate to defend yourself. It's all in the natural order of things. Once you've handled that come back and we'll talk about what's next.
You have been assigned the task 'Natural Instincts'.
Ellen Kwiklen is very worried about the native creatures of the caverns, and how they've been affected by the sirens as well as the undead in the area. She wants you to help restore the natural order by kick starting the animal's natural instincts.
Start by placing spineback meat on the beetle dens in the caverns.

---

!

---

Collect hornback beetle corpses 0/10 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Kill spinebacks (turepta mobs) and loot "Spineback Meat" (dropped about one in every three spineback kills - confirmed from "an enraged spineback" and "a vicious spineback" - maybe others too). Then 'give' the meat to "a beetle den" or "a beetle nest" that are scattered about the above-water caverns in the zone (a lot of spinebacks and beetle nests are located in the caverns east and west of those that house Brothers Dilgen and Visius)._
_Upon handing in Spineback Meat, 3-6 "a hornrock beetle" spawn:_
A number of tiny bugs begin to squirm their way out of the nest. Tentatively at first, but once they notice the meat they begin to swarm.
_These are trivial mobs (mezzable and stunnable; hit for a max ~800). Drop rate for "Rockhorn Beetle Corpse" may depend on the number of players in the group who are on the same task step._

---

Deliver the hornback beetle corpses to Ellen Kwiklen 0/10 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Well done. These beetle corpses will come in handy for the next step of my master plan. You've done some mushroom farming in the past I'm certain? No?! Well there's no time like the [present].
_Rewards:_
Experience
112 platinum, 5 gold
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

---

_This solo task is the third in a series of three and begins with Ellen Kwiklen ( **click for map image**) in the Caverns of Endless Song. She is located in the small area above water just south of the underwater zone-in tunnel._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "Natural Instincts"
\- Completion of "Fishy Fingers"
_Say "dirk" to request the task._
_At the end of the previous task, you saw:_
Ellen Kwiklen takes the gloves from you and mumbles a spell as she puts them on her hands. They magically resize so as to perfectly fit. 'I'll need a few moments to milk the coral for the venom and mix it with the suspension before its ready.' Ellen goes about her work for a few moments as you look on silently. It isn't long before she reappears with a glistening [dirk] in her hands.
You say, 'What's that dirk?'
Ellen Kwiklen gingerly passes the dirk to you, 'The inferno venom has been distilled, and I've envenomed this dirk with it. You'll need to get in close to use it and it should work quickly. You'll have to keep them busy while it's taking effect. Mind you, I can't guarantee how they'll react once they're free. Most of the poor souls that wound up in here were of dubious moral character to begin with, so they might still attack you of their own volition. Oh and of course as with any poison there's the chance of an adverse reaction, but I don't think that's very likely.
_You receive "Inferno Venom Dirk"._
You have been assigned the task 'Endless Silence'.
Ellen Kwiklen has made you a potent Inferno Venom Dirk which will supposedly cause temporary deafness in humanoids. She wants you to do some field tests with it in the Caverns of Endless Song.

---

!

---

Test the inferno venom dirk on seduced humans 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Test the inferno venom dirk on seduced dark elves 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Test the inferno venom dirk on seduced ogres 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_With any "a seduced \_\_\_\_\_" mob, right-click the Inferno Venom Dirk on them (can click at any time - health point doesn't appear to matter). You'll see an emote, and then shortly after, another emote. Once you've seen the second emote, they either go non-aggro and update (humans), or you need to kill them for the update (dark elves and ogres)._
_Note: **Reportedly, you have to have done damage to the mob to get credit.**_
_\- Human click:_
\-\-\- a seduced druid grimaces in pain as you stab him with the inferno venom dirk. The poison should take effect soon.
_\- Human second emote:_
\-\-\- a seduced druid blinks away the enchantment that once held him. 'Ugh, what a nightmare! You can't imagine the things they made us do for them! I owe you one. I swear I'll never pirate again! I'm getting out of her before that accursed song starts up again. Good luck!'
_\- Dark Elf click:_
\-\-\- a seduced corsair howls in anger as you stab him with the inferno venom dirk. The poison should take effect soon.
_\- Dark Elf second emote:_
\-\-\- a seduced corsair says 'Ah finally that cursed thing's voice is out of my head. Now then, I'll be needing whatever valuables you're carrying before I leave. I can't have come all this way and leave empty handed.'
_\- Ogre click:_
\-\-\- a seduced pirate roars in defiance as you stab him with the inferno venom dirk. The poison should take effect soon.
_\- Ogre second emote:_
\-\-\- a seduced pirate roars with fury as the poison begins to take hold. Apparently all you managed to do was anger him.

---

Speak with Ellen Kwiklen 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
You say, 'Hail, Ellen Kwiklen'
Ellen Kwiklen greets you eagerly as you approach, 'Well, how did it go? Did the venom break the spell of the sirens'? Hmm, well working on almost half of them doesn't seem so bad. Not exactly the silver sword we were hoping for though. Now that you've softened up the sirens' defenses let's take a more direct approach. We need to drive the sirens themselves from the caverns, dead or alive. And let's face it \_\_\_\_\_, it'll almost certainly be dead. I won't hazard to guess what the venom might do to them, but I'm certain it won't be pleasant. Give them the old stabby stab and see what happens.

---

Test the inferno venom dirk on sirens 0/5 (Caverns of Endless Song)
_Repeat as above, clicking a siren, waiting for a second emote, and then killing her._
a silenced temptress hisses in anger as you stab her with the inferno venom dirk.
a silenced temptress begins coughing violently as the venom begins to take effect. "..cough, cough...what have you done to me...\*gasp\* my voice, its returned! Her curse is broken! Now you belong to me fool!"

---

Return the inferno venom dirk to Ellen Kwiklen 0/1 (Caverns of Endless Song)
Ellen Kwiklen says 'You've returned once more. How was your encounter with the sirens? What?! The venom gave them BACK their voices? You mean they were mute before you stabbed them? Then what's keeping all these minions here? Surely you can hear the song of the siren echoing off these walls clear as I can, but where could it be coming from? There may be more at work here than simple enchantments \_\_\_\_\_. That leaves us with a question for another day. For now you have my thanks, as well as the thanks of those that you were able to free.'
_Rewards:_
Experience
90 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout from the time of request._

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

**About Heroic Adventures:**
\- They use the task system (steps are shown in task window, but steps are only revealed as you get to them).
\- Scale in difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels (based on your group's highest level player - as low as 85).
\- Tuned for 2-3 players and their mercenaries.
\- Nameds can spawn as you progress through the task (you usually have to complete a few steps before this can happen).
\- Collection items can drop from mobs in the instances, and ground spawns appear midway through tasks.
\- Expected time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- There is some variations/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
**About Our Documentation of Heroic Adventures**
\- We only display task steps we know about.
\-\-\- If you encounter a step that isn't documented here, please post the details (or email us) and **be sure to indicate which step number it was** so that we can display it in its proper place.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Mough Kyslaz ( **click for map image**) in the Caverns of Endless Song (located in the area south of the zone-in)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "song" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
Clear the tunnels of aggressive sea life 0/14
\-\-\- Kill update.
Eliminate the seduced 0/20
\-\-\- Kill update.
**Step #2:**
Banish the skeletons, spirits, and banshees 0/11
\-\-\- Kill update.
Put the sirens to rest 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update.
Eliminate the enthralled 0/9
\-\-\- Kill update.
Destroy the elementals 0/8
\-\-\- Kill update.
**At this point in the task, collectibles appear as ground spawns and a named (Elizabeth Ruffleberg or its PH a voiceless temptress) has a chance to appear.**

_This solo task begins with Researcher Neckchi ( **click for map image**) at the zone-in of Degmar, the Lost Castle._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- No tasks
_Say "destroy" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Researcher Neckchi'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Oh, thank the gods, a sane person. You would not believe what I've been through. But you're obviously an adventurer, so perhaps you could [care] less what I've been through since you face adversity every day. Let me get to the point. I could use your help on a [task], and I can help [you] along the way.'
You say, 'I care!'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Thank you! So few adventurers want to talk, and instead want to just get to it. I am in quite the [quandary].'
You say, 'What quandary?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'The combine sent me here alone! Assuming all would be well within the habitat, and I could go about my [mission] in peace! But all is not [well]!'
You say, 'What was your mission?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'I was sent through, all but blindly, to set up a return teleport device, and contact the dwarves for their help in repairing the dome in Katta Castrum. While I have no problems setting up the device, things did not go [well] with the dwarves.'
You say, 'All isn't well?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'I thought it odd that this guard house was empty, but it gave me the space and privacy I needed to set up the return device. Once the device was active, I attempted to make contact, but not only was I immediately [attacked] by dwarves, I saw a [ghost]!'
You say, 'Attacked?!'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Yes! They caught me by surprise and dragged me to their Commander. He would not even listen to my pleas that I was on a diplomatic mission. He accused me of being a spy to scout the Castle before an attack, and he threw me into his dungeon. I saw so many [ghosts] I thought I would go mad myself! Luckily, [Damton] Silverhammer over there freed me and protected me from the guards long enough to cast an evacuate spell and get us back here to the guard house.'
You say, 'A ghost!'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Horrible things! The dead should remain dead! And when they pass through you... just, yuck! Please, I don't want to talk about ghosts any longer. Just thinking about them is terrible! Sorry, I know it's not rational, but I have phasmophobia and I just can't seem to conquer it.'
You say, 'Who is Damton?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'She is a very nice dwarf. The only sane one that I've met, though she claims there are others. Speak with her and she may have more information for you.'
You say, 'You, can help me?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Getting right to the point, if you [destroy] some [ghosts] and retrieve two items from the ends of the [branches] in this oddly designed castle, I will give you a device that will teleport you back to this guard room.'
You say, 'What branches are you talking about?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Well, I'm not sure what insane person designed this castle, but there are very few cross passages. Other adventurers reported to me that there are only two main branches, and that once you get to the end of a branch, to return here, you have to come all the way back through the rooms you just exited. Because the [ghosts] don't rest for long, you have to fight your way back again. Very inconvenient and dangerous.'
You say, 'I'll destroy some'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Very good. I've updated your journal with the task particulars, But I can give you more [details] if you wish.'
You have been assigned the task 'Castle Travel'.
Researcher Neckchi assumes that you do not like ghosts, despite the fact that you are braver than he. He will create a device for you that will teleport you back to the guard house if you retrieve the right items for him, and kill some ghosts along the way.
You say, 'Hold on... More details...'
Researcher Neckchi says 'First off, destroy any aggressive [ghost] you cross paths with. That is the basic fee I charge for this device. You're welcome to kill any non-aggressive ghosts if you like. I wish those horrible things would stay dead. You should, of course, defend yourself against the dwarves, but don't kill them if you can avoid it. To make the device function, I will need a focus item from the rooms at the end of each branch. Adventurers have mentioned a [workshop] and a [chapel].'
You say, 'What is the workshop?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Within the workshop, I have heard of some delicate magical ore. A sample of this will be ideal for the focus. Previous adventurers have brought some back, and they told me that it's very sensitive to travel magic, so be sure that you do not use any magic that would cause you to travel, or it might disintegrate.'
You say, 'What is the chapel?'
Researcher Neckchi says 'Within the chapel, you will find some small hammers used as a prayer focus for Brell. Please bring me one of these as the focus for that branch. Don't worry about chapel running out; they seem to have a vast supply of these hammers.'

_This solo task begins with Damton Silverhammer ( **click for map image**), located at the zone-in of Degmar, the Lost Castle._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- No tasks
_Say "fetch" to request this task._
You say, 'Hail, Damton Silverhammer'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Salutations, \_\_\_\_\_ You look like the adventuring type and I wager you'd like work. I can use help gathering [supplies] and seeing if we can overturn my [banishment]. If you are the more conversational type, I can also tell you a little about Degmar's [Castle].'
You say, 'What supplies do you need?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'While I have plenty of room to sleep, supplies are rather scarce. I will reward you if you [fetch] me some supplies.'
You say, 'I'll fetch some'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Thank you. I've made notes of what I need in your journal, but I can give you more [details] if you like.'
You have been assigned the task 'Castle Raid'.
Damton has been banished to the guard house and would like for you to retrieve supplies for her from the castle.
Defeat denizens of the library and collect library books from them, so she has something to entertain her. Go to the dining hall and collect food for her. The elvish ghosts in the hall will attack if you do not dance with them before you collect the food. Spend at least one minute dancing with them. Collect Degmar Ale from the brewery. The dwarfs in the brewery are likely to strenuously object.
You say, 'Give me more details...'
Damton Silverhammer says 'I would like you to [fetch] some [books], [food], and [ale].'
You say, 'Ale?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'The brewery can be accessed through the dining room. Once there, you can retrieve ale however you like, but you may find the bartenders and patrons a bit... possessive, with their ale. They will notice if any goes missing, so be prepared!'
You say, 'Where do I get food?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'To gather food you will need to go west on the upper level of the foyer and through the merchant's quarters to the [dining] hall. While passing through the merchant quarters, if you feel you have time, and if you can find her, let Priestess Gli'helin know that I'm okay.'
You say, 'What about the dining hall?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'You will find the food in the dining hall. Don't worry about depleting the supply as more food is generated magically. But be careful, the elven dancers in that room will want to dance with you, and if you do not appease them, they will attack. You don't need to show skill, just spend at least a minute in their presence.'
You say, 'What about the books?'
Damton Silverhammer says '[Neckchi] over there is a fine conversationalist, but he is often busy, and even when I can get his attention he's likely to drone on and on about esoteric details of teleportation magic. Some books from the [library] will help me pass the time.'
You say, 'Tell me about the library'
Damton Silverhammer says 'The library is immediately east of the foyer on the lower level. Most of the books on the shelves are so old that they'll crumble if you handle them. You will instead need to gain them from the library patrons. I encourage you to take them from the ghostly librarians, but I will not censure you if you take some from the crazy dwarves digging in the library. There is no telling what harm the madmen will cause with their digging.'

---

!

---

1\. Kill 5 Librarians 0/5 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
2\. Collect 2 Library Books 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Kill any mobs with "librarian" in their name (both live and undead count). Library books have a 50% drop rate if you're on this step. Note that undead librarians will use standard mez spells._

---

3\. Dance with the revelers to appease them 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You can just stand between the tables for 1 minute and get the update.
4\. Collect 5 Fancy Foods 0/5 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Don't take any food until the dancers have been appeased.
Outside the party room is Herrul Bakewell. He sells the Fancy party foods (free), if he is kos to you invis/sneak.
5\. Collect 2 Degmar Castle Ales 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Head to the dining area where you'll find numerous tables set up along with a spread of food along the room's north wall. Before you pick at the food, you need to 'appease' the revelers in the room by dancing with them... To do this, all you need to do is move around in the room for at least 60 seconds straight. The task will update, and you'll see an emote:_
The revelers look at you and raise a glass in celebration.
_You can now pick up five food items from the tables. (There are five types; any combination counts.) **NOTE:** These items are actual food; don't eat them!_
_If you DON'T dance with the revelers before picking at the food, they aggro you. You can also draw auto mob aggro if you hang around in the room for more than five minutes without dancing. In this case, you start seeing emote warnings 60 seconds before they'll attack:_
The revelers look at you and frown.
The revelers look at you, disapointed that you have not reveled with them, and attack.
a spectral reveler says 'Why won't you dance?'
You have been slain by a spectral reveler!
a spectral reveler says 'See. You should be dancing.'
_For the Degmar Castle Ales, head to the Brewery (just east of the dining room with the revelers) and pick up 2x "Degmar Castle Ale" from the bars in there._

---

6\. Deliver a library book to Damton Silverhammer 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
7\. Deliver 5 Fancy foods to Damton Silverhammer 0/5 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
8\. Deliver Degmar Castle Ale to Damton Silverhammer 0/2 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
_Upon turn-in:_
Damton Silverhammer is grateful for your assistance.

---

_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum
Faction with Degmar's Commoners (+225)
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._

_This solo task begins with Damton Silverhammer ( **click for map image**), located at the zone-in of Degmar, the Lost Castle._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- No tasks
_Say "resolve" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Damton Silverhammer'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Salutations, \_\_\_\_\_ You look like the adventuring type and I wager you'd like work. I can use help gathering [supplies] and seeing if we can overturn my [banishment]. If you are the more conversational type, I can also tell you a little about Degmar's [Castle].'
You say, 'Tell me about this Castle'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Three to five centuries ago, long before I was born, this castle and everyone inside were transported from the edge of Faydark to this underwater tomb. To protect Degmar from the pressure of the sea, the castle was encapsulated in an exceedingly strong magical barrier. No one, not even the Elven emissaries that were with us at the time could transport themselves out. The Commander Degmar of the time was very [wise].'
You say, 'How was he wise?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'He integrated the elves into the population, treating them as equals, as family even. For many centuries we had peace. Unfortunately, what we did not realize is that the elves are uncomfortable in enclosed spaces, and after hundreds of years of claustrophic isoluation they went insane. A group of the elves started killing the dwarves off, one at a time, leaving evidence that implicated other dwarves. As you can imagine, [paranoia] ran rampant.'
You say, 'Paranoia?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'The situation would have stabilized, except Commander Alast Degmar, an ancestor of the original Commander Degmar, caught an elf attempting to assassinate him. Alast Degmar was already paranoid that the elves were out to get him, and this proved his fears and sent him into a frothing rage. He killed every elf in the castle, though Priestess Gli'helin managed to escape the purge, and has managed to stay hidden since then. And even after slaying the elves, paranoia is still a [problem].'
You say, 'What kind of problem?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'I hate to think of any more dwarf blood being shed, but many of my brothers and sisters are so far gone that they'll attack any stranger they see on sight, even if you are trying to help them. Do what you must, but spare as many as you can.'
You say, 'What happened with your banishment?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'My banishment is a sad story. I can [explain] it if you wish, or you can just help me [resolve] it by reading the notes I'll jot down in your journal.'
You say, 'Yes, explain...'
Damton Silverhammer says 'This [castle] has a long sordid history. Thankfully, I've managed to stave off the rampant paranoia that has affected the other dwarves in these past twenty years. When Neckchi was captured, and don't tell him this, it will just frighten him more, he was sentenced to be executed. I could not allow that to happen, so I [freed] him.'
You say, 'You freed him?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Yes. I freed him. I could not justify the judgment, handed down simply because Commander Degmar is paranoid. By doing this I earned the wrath of Commander Degmar. Luckily, he is so paranoid that he believes the teleport device to be a [bomb]. He ordered his men to clear out of the guard house, and banished me here as a traitor.'
You say, 'It's not a bomb, is it...'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Of course it's not a bomb, but I let the commander think that so he does not send guards here to kill us. The influx of adventurers like you has only served to fuel his paranoia, and he sees you as invaders. But I ramble on. Let me explain the [task].'
You say, 'What about the task?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'It's fairly simple, though dangerous. I'm sending you to the [armory] to retrieve a specific hammer that has [sentimental] value for Commander Degmar. When you go there, I've arranged to have a few enemies of mine [ambush] you. I think by the fact that I arranged the ambush, this will convince Commander Degmar that I'm not a traitor, and he will allow me to return. You must keep this confidential.'
You say, 'Where is the armory?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'The armory is on the lower level. Take the east branch in the foyer, and go through the library and throne room to get to the armory.'
You say, 'What's sentimental about the hammer?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'It's simple. It is his father's hammer. The hammer is unremarkable except for its family heritage. If I return it to him after the "invaders" steal it, it will help ingratiate me with Commander Degmar.'
You say, 'Ambush me, really?'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Yes. I know I'm asking a lot for you to purposefully go into an ambush, and now that I think about it, since you're forewarned, it's not much of an ambush. But... I can tell that a \_\_\_\_\_ like you is a survivor and you'll be just fine. Please don't think me callous for asking you to kill my fellow dwarves. They are our enemies. If they no longer have a voice in Commander Degmar's ear, it will further cement my return.'
You say, 'I'll resolve all this'
Damton Silverhammer says 'Thank you. I've placed the notes you will need in your journal. I'll [explain] more if you like.'
You have been assigned the task 'Castle Crasher'.
Damton Silverhammer has been banished and would like for you to clear her name. Go to the armory and survive the ambush. The armory is on the east branch from the foyer.

---

!

---

Go to the armory and approach the hammer 0/1 (Dregmar, the Lost Castle)
Kill 1 Ambusher 0/1 (Dregmar, the Lost Castle)
_Updates at around -310, 125, -13 in the armory (through the eastern wing of the castle; down the hall leading west out of the throne room). Note: A mob will spawn here, so you'll probably want to clear the area of guards._
a Degmar ambusher says 'It will end poorly for you.'
_Kill 1x "a Degmar ambusher" (of average difficulty for the zone; mezzable; social with other mobs in the room)._

---

This solo task begins with Priestess Gli`helin ( **click for map image**) in Degmar.
Pre-Requisites for Requesting:
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum Apprehensive faction with Degmar's Commoners (complete Damton Silverhammer [npc=47723]'s two tasks)
Say " **collect**" to request the task.
You say, 'Hail, Priestess Glihelin'
Priestess Glihelin says 'May Tunare bless you, \_\_\_\_\_. Your deeds in the castle have floated to my ears. I will not bore you with conversation unless you [desire] it. I wish to add [life] to the temple, and to [retrieve] a confiscated relic.'
You say, 'I desire it, yes...'
Priestess Glihelin says 'I've been in this castle since it was ripped from Faydark. For centuries, everything was fine. We had an endless supply of food from the dining room to sustain us. But alas, despite my prophesy that the castle would return to the surface, the elves did not fare [well].'
You say, 'Not well?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Over the centuries, many elves went a bit crazy because of their confinement in the castle. About twenty years ago, it all came to a head. A group of elves got together, and in their insanity, they started killing dwarves, and implicating other dwarves for the murders. This went on for about a year while the dwarves became more and more paranoid. Then [Commander Alast Degmar], who was already unbalanced because of my prophesy, caught an elf attempting to [assassinate] him.'
You say, 'Who is Commander Alast Degmar?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Comander Degmar is no longer really with us. Paranoia has made him miserable and unreasonable. He is difficult to find, and when he is around, he tends to attack first, and speak later.'
You say, 'Assassinate him?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'With proof that cemented his paranoid beliefs, he went on a murderous spree, slaughtering every elf in the castle. I managed to hide, and have stayed vigilant and hidden from him every since. The merchants know that I am here, but they protect and feed me. While most of the dwarves remain paranoid and xenophobic, a kind of peace has settled over the castle.'
You say, 'How can we add life to the temple?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'You will need to [collect] some supplies and plants from within the castle, return with them, and plant them to add life to my chapel. Let me [know] if you need more details.'
You say, 'I'll collect some supplies'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Thank you. I've written a list in your journal. Of course tell me if you need to [know] more.'
You have been assigned the task 'Castle Growth'.
Priestess Glihelin would like you to help add some life to her chapel. Collect the tools you will need, water, and plants in order to give the place life.
You say, 'I need to know more'
Priestess Glihelin says 'To help with this task, you will need to get some water from the [store room] of the brewery, tools from the [workshop], and plants from the [library].'
You say, 'What about the store room?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'To get to the store room, take the west branch in the foyer to the upper levels of the castle. Go through the merchant rooms and the dining room to the brewery. The store room is the turret. You will find the pure water there.'
You say, 'What about the workshop?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'This is a more difficult task. The dwarves within the workshop will have the tools. I do not endorse killing them, but I will leave it to you to decide how you retrieve the tools from them. You will find the workshop beyond the brewery on the west branch and upper level of the castle.'
You say, 'What about the library?'
Priestess Glihelin says 'The library is in the first room from the east branch of the foyer, on the lower level of the castle. You will find the plants in the areas where the dwarves are digging. You may also find that those dwarves have some plants in their pockets if you can convince them to give it to you.'

---

!

---

1\. Collect 1 Pure Water 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
The Pure Water [item=121439] is a ground spawn in the storage area for the brewery (the small room connected to it). It appears as a glowing blue light in the back of the room.
2\. Collect 1 Digging Tools 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
The Digging Tools [item=119757] drop from a confused smith [npc=48053] and a dedicated artisan [npc=48057] dwarves in the Workshop (must be on this task for the item to drop, with chance to drop multiples if more than one person in group is on the task).
3\. Collect 3 Aquatic Plants 0/3 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
The Aquatic Plants [item=121339] are ground spawns near the diggers in the library (appear as small bushes on the ground).

---

4\. Find a spot to the west and south of Priestess Glihelin to place a plant 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Updates at around /way -75,+230,+42. (You'll see small pots of mud on the ground.)
5\. Find a spot to the east and south of Priestess Glihelin to place a plant 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Updates at around /way -65,+190,+42.
6\. Find a spot behind Priestess Glihelin to place a plant 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Updates at around /way -45,+210,+42.
You place and water a plant only Priestess Gli'helin seems to see.

---

7\. Speak with Priestess Gli`helin 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
You say, 'Hail, Priestess Glihelin'
Priestess Glihelin says 'Thank you. I know you can't see them, but they look magnificent to my eyes. I will take the tools off your hands, and the extra water you have left in order to continue to care for them.'

---

8\. Deliver 1 Digging Tools to Priestess Gli`helin 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
9\. Deliver 1 Pure Water to Priestess Gli`helin 0/1 (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
Priestess Gli'helin is pleased with the new life in her chapel.
_Rewards:_
You gain experience!
37 platinum 5 gold
Your faction with Degmar's Commoners has been adjusted by 25.
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._

_This is a solo kill task that begins with Ranpodia Stonewalker ( **click for map image**) at the zone-in of Degmar, the Lost Castle._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- No tasks
_Say "help" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Ranpodia Stonewalker'
Ranpodia Stonewalker says '\_\_\_\_\_. Have I seen you before? You seem familiar. You look like you aren't out to [get] me. Prove it and [help] me!'
You say, 'I'm not out to get you... Who is?'
Ranpodia Stonewalker says 'Of course. They all are! The overeager [guards]. The complacent [commoners]. The silent [librarians]. The killing [ghosts]. The partying [revelers]. The rude brewery [employees]. And especially the oh so smug [artisans].'
You say, 'The guards?'
Ranpodia Stonewalker says 'Yes. They think I don't notice the way they stare at me. The way they envy me. The way they sharpen their blades. They want to kill me, but Alast will not let them... Alast wants the privilege, but I won't let him near me. Maybe I can get him first.'
You say, 'I will help!'
Ranpodia Stonewalker says 'Yes. Relief from the constant threat! Go [get] them! You don't need any [help]!'
You have been assigned the task 'Paranoia: Guards'.
Ranpodia Stonewalker is convinced that everyone is out to kill him. His solution is to have you kill them first.

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

**About Heroic Adventures:**
\- They use the task system (steps are shown in task window, but steps are only revealed as you get to them).
\- From the moment that you request the adventure, the countdown to request another adventure is 3 hours.
\- Scale in difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels (based on your group's highest level player - as low as 85).
\- Tuned for 2-3 players and their mercenaries.
\- Nameds can spawn as you progress through the task (you usually have to complete a few steps before this can happen).
\- Collection items can drop from mobs in the instances, and ground spawns appear midway through tasks.
\- Expected time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- There is some variations/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
**About Our Documentation of Heroic Adventures**
\- We only display task steps we know about.
\-\-\- If you encounter a step that isn't documented here, please post the details (or email us) and **be sure to indicate which step number it was** so that we can display it in its proper place.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Praetoria Okkirus ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island. It is a spirit found on the north side of the building just northeast of the dock along the coastline._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 85

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "see" to request the task and "ready" to zone into the instance.
**Step #1:**
Clear the western forest of saurek 0/6
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Clear the central forest of raptors 0/7
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Clear the western forest of drogmors 0/6
\-\-\- Kill updates.
**Step #2:**
Kill a few soulsippers and glistenwings 0/7
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Kill a few ebonwings and ebonwing guardians 0/6
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Put a few wandering shades out of their misery 0/9
\-\-\- Kill updates.
**Step #3:**
Take down a few triceratopses 0/5
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Take down a few ankylosauruses 0/5
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Take down a few tyrannosauruses 0/5
\-\-\- Kill updates.
**Step #4:**
Exact revenge upon the Bloodfeather aviaks 0/20
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Exact revenge upon the Kromtus giants 0/20
\-\-\- Kill updates.
Exact revenge upon the Thaell Ew tribe 0/20
\-\-\- Kill updates.
**Step #5:**
Speak to Praetoria Okkirus 0/1
\-\-\- Dialogue to complete.

---

!

---

_Three named mobs will all spawn at the same time after the third step:_
\- Deicoraxius (at /loc 880, -1630, 335, or its PH "a bloodtear ebonwing")
\- Serthuliakar (spawns and roams the wide path, or its PH "a tired Thaell Ew hunter")
\- Rexsaurkarus (spawns at /loc 1015, 1835, 203, or its PH "an old tyrannosaurus")

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, Praetoria Okkirus'
Praetoria Okkirus looks deeply into your eyes with a sorrowful expression, 'I know not why you have set foot upon this island, but I implore you now adventurer, leave lest you suffer the same [fate] as my crew.'
You say, 'What fate?'
Praetoria Okkirus says 'These ruins mark the death of our ambition, of our desire to expand the territory and knowledge of our people beyond the small refuge we huddled wearily within. What we encountered beyond the beach of this impregnable island were creatures stronger than anything we were equipped to deal with. I offer you a chance to [see] it for yourself.'
You say, 'Let me see it'
Praetoria Okkirus says 'When you are [ready], I will show you the land as it once was.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Fateful Arrival'.
The last Combine crew to sail here was ill equipped to deal with the harsh terrain and fearsome creatures that inhabit the island. Relive the land as it was and do battle with the beasts they encountered.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Clear the western forest of saurek 0/6 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Clear the central forest of raptors 0/7 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Several of our scouts made their way into the forest in search of food. Days passed before we learned of their fate, bloodied cloaks buried in the nests of the wandering raptors. Avenge the fallen scouts by putting a few of these beasts to rest.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Clear the western forest of drogmors 0/6 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ A small contingent of our bravest warriors set out to recover whatever resources could be scavenged from the western forest. Only one of them returned, but was mocked when he revealed his party fell to a pack of wild drogmors. Prove your strength by putting a few of the beasts down.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Kill a few soulsippers and glistenwings 0/7 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Scattered throughout the forest are a number of monstrous flying insects that proved to be deadly encounters for our scouts. Exterminate any colossal butterflies or mosquitoes you come across while exploring the forest.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Kill a few ebonwings and ebonwing guardians 0/6 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ On the eastern edge of the forest is a tribe of Aviaks and south of them is the nest of a colossal raven they appear to live in fear of. A tribe of necromantic bird creatures, not unlike the Aviaks themselves, tends to the great beast's offspring. Our crew sent a small party to the nest to try and win favor with the Aviaks by slaying a few of these necromantic creatures but ultimately proved no match for these birds. Make your way to this nest and succeed where they failed.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Put a few wandering shades out of their misery 0/9 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ The members of our crew that did not fall to the beasts of the forest remained here on the beach, trying to maintain hope while awaiting rescue. As hunger and melancholy began to overtake them they grew evermore bitter and hateful. Their souls now haunt these ruins unable to continue on into an afterlife. Put their souls to rest by dispersing their undead remains.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Take down a few triceratopses 0/5 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Every scout that returned to camp after setting out into the forest spoke of only one thing, beasts with the size and fierceness of dragons yet none of the expected cunning. Travel to the northern edge of the central forest and test your strength against these dinosaurs.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Take down a few ankylosauruses 0/5 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Every scout that returned to camp after setting out into the forest spoke of only one thing, beasts with the size and fierceness of dragons yet none of the expected cunning. Travel to the heart of the central forest and test your strength against these dinosaurs.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Take down a few tyrannosauruses 0/5 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Every scout that returned to camp after setting out into the forest spoke of only one thing, beasts with the size and fierceness of dragons yet none of the expected cunning. Travel to the edge of the western forest and test your strength against these dinosaurs.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Exact revenge upon the Bloodfeather aviaks 0/20 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Exact revenge upon the Kromtus giants 0/20 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Just east of our outpost is a village of giants. They were the first of the sentient tribes we encountered here and miraculously they purported to be pacifists. We maintained a peaceful relationship with them until a small band of our scouts set out into the giant village during the night in an attempt to steal supplies. They were discovered and the purported pacifists were quite adept at combat. Their guards descended upon our camp and destroyed every one of our shelters short of our observatory. Travel to the Kromtus village and strike a blow to their tribe in our memory.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
=====================
Exact revenge upon the Thaell Ew tribe 0/20 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Out of everything our crew encountered on the island, nothing proved as deadly as our first and only encounter with the lizardmen to the north. All of our attempts at diplomacy were met with unmitigated savagery. The decapitated heads of our orators were left impaled on spear as a warning to any human that would step into the northern valley. Go north and respond to these creatures in kind.
_If You Get This Step: Kill updates._
========================================================================
STEP #5
========================================================================
Speak to Praetoria Okkirus 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ You have proven better suited to deal with the harsh beasts of the island than the ancient Combine crew. Return to Praetoria Okkirus to inform her of your victory.
_If You Get This Step: Dialogue to complete._

_This solo task begins with Ilsaria ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "willing" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Ilsaria'
Ilsaria looks into your eyes with a sense of distant reservation, 'A living traveler. Such a thing was once a rare sight here. Tell me, stranger, are you as [willing] to aid the dead as we once were?'
You say, 'I'm willing'
Ilsaria forces a smile, though it does little to change her somber appearance, 'Perhaps this land will not bind you as it has us.' She goes on to explain what help you can provide...
You have been assigned the task 'A Drowning Suspicion'.
The spirit of Ilsaria, a Combine provisioner, remains anchored to the land, her spirit unable to rest. She finds herself unable to escape the grief caused by losing everyone she held dear. She hopes to find some peace in learning the fate of the members of her party that remained unaccounted for at the time of her death.
You recall hearing about some human remains deep in the water just southeast of where Ilsaria stands. Seek out these remains and inspect them closely.
On the edge of the river near the raptors' den is a human skull that clearly belonged to a Combine explorer. Find these remains and inspect them to learn what you can about this fellow's fate.
Just south of the ankylosauruses' nest are more human remains. Inspect them to learn what you can.
The pristine coral that grows in the deep water is eye-catching and may be worth inspecting as it could hold some clue about the fate of the party members that swam out to their death at sea.
There is something strangely alluring about these bulb plants that grow on the banks of the river near the stone crossings. Inspect these strangely animated plants to learn the secret of their allure.

---

_Map points for "thuliasaur.txt" map files:_
P 715.3693, 1270.8760, -47.6765, 0, 127, 127, 1, a\_waterlogged\_skull
P 160.3004, 1309.8658, -51.0312, 0, 127, 127, 1, curious\_corals
P -285.5723, -62.7927, 36.4875, 0, 127, 127, 1, a\_notable\_river\_bulb
P -313.8285, -354.8974, 46.0036, 0, 127, 127, 1, a\_notable\_river\_bulb
P 72.3463, -219.5887, 71.8987, 0, 127, 127, 1, crushed\_skull
P -413.3031, -183.3861, 45.3368, 0, 127, 127, 1, crumbling\_skull
!

---

Search the shore for remains 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Find "a waterlogged skull" (/loc -1270, -715, -47) southeast of Ilsaria on the ocean floor and /inspect it. Note that this spawns 2x "a sunken sailor" that auto-aggro you and remain auto-aggro (zonewide aggro) even if you fade or zone or die. As long as the two mobs are up, you are in the zone, and on this task, they will remain aggro on you. They each hit for a max ~15,000; see through all forms of invisibility; and are mezzable and rootable/snareable._
_If left idle for a period of time, the mobs despawn, but can be respawned by /inspecting the object again._
_Note: These mechanics apply to all ambush mobs in this task._
The waterlogged remains of a Combine sailor. You can make out no obvious sign of trauma or injury that would have caused this fellow's demise.
A ghastly howl cuts through the previously peaceful hush of the ocean!
Defeat the skeletal ambushers 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)

---

Search the raptor den for remains 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_"a crumbling human skull" is found at /loc 185, 415, 45 (south of the raptors' nest near the river). /inspect this and be ready for aggro by "a starving raptor". This mob summons._
You take note that every bone in the explorer's body appears to remain here but you can see no signs that they once held a weapon or even any armor. Strange that they would travel out here so unprepared.
A low growl turns into a screeching bark as a raptor comes darting out of the distance to make you its next meal!
Defeat the raptor ambusher 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)

---

Search the ankylosaurus den for remains 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_"a crushed human skull" is found at /loc 220, -70, 71 (a little ways southeast of the Ankylosauruses' nest). /inspect this (no ambush here, but you'll need to dodge the see-invis dinosaurs). (It may be a bit difficult to spot with the flora in the area.)_
The skull is so badly damaged that you're only partially sure it once belonged to a human.

---

Inspect the coral on the beach for clues 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_On the ocean floor on the south beach, find "curious looking coral" and /inspect it. ...And "an alluring siren" aggro's you. This mob hits for a max ~15,000 and is mezzable and rootable/snareable._
You spot a small scrap of burgundy cloth clinging to the coral. You carefully liberate the material and confirm your suspicion, bloodstained clothing.
Your heart begins to race as the most arousing sound you have ever heard in your life begins to consume your every sense!
Defeat the siren ambusher 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)

---

Inspect the plants in the river for clues 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Along the sides of the river, south of the fork in the central jungle, you will find multiple "a notable river bulb". /inspect one of them to update this step (no ambush here)._
You lean over the river bulb and take a short whiff of its perfume. You immediately stagger back recognizing the intoxicating potency of the flower. Such a plant would make for a vile toxin or an unscrupulous addict's ether.

---

Speak with Ilsaria 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
You say, 'Hail, Ilsaria'
Ilsaria nods and seems somewhat relieved to learn that not all of the party members that met their end did so willingly. She forces a smile but her voice retains its sullen tone, 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_, I have much to consider now.'
Ilsaria has gotten one step closer to accepting her own fate in learning how the unaccounted members of her party met their ends.
_Rewards:_
Tiny Parrot's Right Carpometacarpus --OR-- 75 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Right Carpometacarpus [item=119744]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

_This solo task begins with Supplier Thissus ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with The Thaell Ew
_Say "assistance" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Supplier Thissus'
Supplier Thissus says 'You have proven yourself trustworthy, \_\_\_\_\_. I need your [assistance] in shoring up our supplies, as our hunters and foragers have been losing ground to the jungle beasts.'
You say, 'You have my assistance...'
Supplier Thissus says 'Very good, we could use your help collecting the following...'
You have been assigned the task 'A Meal of Greens'.
Supplier Thissus could use your help in shoring up the city's supply of foraged ingredients for both consumption and cooking. Many of these plants grow in specific locations around the island and he's informed you of their general whereabouts.
There is a specific type of root that grows at the bottom of the ponds formed by the rivers around the island. They are called 'Water Shoots' and are integral in flavoring our stews. Submerge yourself in the ponds on the interior of the island and forage for these Water Shoots.
Small patches of red berries grow around the Bloodfeather's village. We have no quarrel with their tribe, nor do they with us, but we have both agreed to keep our distance in order to avoid provoking the great beasts that roam the island. Forage around the center of the Bloodfeather village in order to find Blood Berries.
Jungle Wheatgrass is a common brush that grows right inside of our village just outside of our central temple. When this plant is blanched and sprinkled over a prepared dish it adds the perfect accent to offset the bitter tones in the meal. Forage around the grasslands outside of our central temple but within the walls of our city to find Jungle Wheatgrass.
Among the hardest of ingredients to obtain is a rare plant that grows in the footsteps of the great lumbering beasts of the central jungle simply called Dinobrush. Forage around the central jungle where the dinosaurs roam to obtain some Dinobrush.

---

03. Forage some Water Shoots 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)



_Water Shoots can be foraged underwater in the ponds at the ends of rivers in the central forest._

04. Forage a few Jungle Bloodberries 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)



Can find these at 1535, -1464, 203.




_These are foraged around the Aviak village._

05. Forage some Jungle Wheatgrass 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)



_These can be foraged in the courtyard of the Thaell Ew area._

06. Forage a bit of Dinobrush 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)



_These can be foraged anywhere around the central jungle (confirmed able on the ankylosaurus land that's surrounded by water in the center of the zone)._

07. Deliver the Water Shoots to Supplier Thissus 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)

08. Deliver the Jungle Bloodberries to Supplier Thissus 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)

09. Deliver the Jungle Wheatgrass to Supplier Thissus 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)

10. Deliver the Dinobrush to Supplier Thissus 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)



Supplier Thissus thanks you heartily for your assistance in resupplying his stocks, 'Thank you. Thesssse sssshall make for fine mealssss.'







---




_Rewards:_

Faction with The Thaell Ew (+28)


--AND--


Tiny Parrot's Right Patella --OR-- 25 platinum




_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Right Patella [item=119746]
