_This solo task begins with Supplier Thissus ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with The Thaell Ew
_Say "assistance" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Supplier Thissus'
Supplier Thissus says 'You have proven yourself trustworthy, \_\_\_\_\_. I need your [assistance] in shoring up our supplies, as our hunters and foragers have been losing ground to the jungle beasts.'
You say, 'You have my assistance...'
Supplier Thissus says 'Very good, we could use your help collecting the following...'
You have been assigned the task 'Fortifying the Water Supply'.
Supplier Thissus needs your help in obtaining purified water for cooking as both the freshwater rivers and the temple aquifiers are not suitable sources for his purposes. You will need to use a Tracker's Water Extractor and several Purification Tablets to extract water from the raw materials you gather. The extractor and tablets are available from the Thaell Ew shopkeeper in the eastern temple.
You should be able to identify the Jungle Water Roots by their bluish hue. If you follow the river south of our temple as it winds to the west, you should be able to find several suitable harvesting locations.
Rivercap mushrooms can be indentified by their subtle orange tint. They too grow along the banks of the river south of our temple.
Jungle Moss can be foraged nearly anywhere on the island if you try hard enough.

---

_Map points for "thuliasaur.txt" map files:_
P -916.4562, -534.5851, 179.1740, 0, 0, 0, 1, roots
P -1115.4718, -580.5717, 188.8116, 0, 0, 0, 1, roots
P -682.3957, -521.9448, 122.6322, 0, 0, 0, 1, mushroom
P -542.0610, -515.0436, 94.9149, 0, 0, 0, 1, mushroom
P -316.1603, 44.1357, 36.2869, 0, 0, 0, 1, mushroom
P -366.8631, -183.9913, 36.2938, 0, 0, 0, 1, mushroom

---

!

---

Dig up some Jungle Water Roots 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Ground spawns: blue/green plant-looking things along the sides of the river that runs west-east near the tyrannosaurus' nests._
Gather some Rivercap Mushrooms 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Same area as above, appearing as tiny orange mushrooms in the water._
Forage up a bit of Jungle Moss 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_This can be foraged anywhere on the island._

---

Extract Fresh Water from the Jungle Water Roots 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Now you'll need to do some combines. Visit "a Thaell Ew shopkeeper" in the zone. It sells unlimited Purification Tablets (you'll need five of them) and has a stock of 50 "Tracker's Water Extractor" containers._
_There are two ways to reset his inventory: Either kill him, or complete the task "Resupplying the Sundries"._
_In a "Tracker's Water Extractor": Combine 1x "Jungle Water Roots" + 1x "Purification Tablet" to produce 1x "Freshly Extracted Water"._
_(repeat for two combines)_
Extract Fresh Water from the Rivercap Mushrooms 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_In a "Tracker's Water Extractor": Combine 1x "Rivercap Mushrooms" + 1x "Purification Tablet" to produce 1x "Freshly Extracted Water"._
_(repeat for two combines)_
Extract Fresh Water from the Jungle Moss 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_In a "Tracker's Water Extractor": Combine 1x "Jungle Moss" + 1x "Purification Tablet" to produce 1x "Freshly Extracted Water"._

---

Deliver the Freshly Extracted Water to Supplier Thissus 0/5 (Thuliasaur Island)
Supplier Thissus thanks you heartily for your assistance in refilling the water supply, 'A most gracioussss thank you. Thisss will do perfectly.'
_Rewards:_
Faction with The Thaell Ew (+28)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Right Tarsometatarsus --OR-- 25 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Right Tarsometatarsus [item=119760]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Supplier Thissus ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with The Thaell Ew
_Say "assistance" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Supplier Thissus'
Supplier Thissus says 'You have proven yourself trustworthy, \_\_\_\_\_. I need your [assistance] in shoring up our supplies, as our hunters and foragers have been losing ground to the jungle beasts.'
You say, 'You have my assistance...'
Supplier Thissus says 'Very good, we could use your help collecting the following...'
You have been assigned the task 'Resupplying the Sundries'.
Supplier Thissus has tasked you with gathering the pieces needed to construct the purifiers that the tribe relies on to ensure a continuous supply of potable water.
The porous structure of dinosaur bones, once cleaned, makes them an integral portion of our water purifiers. A bone from any of the following creatures will work just fine: ankylosauruses, raptors, saureks, triceratopses, or tyrannosauruses.
Hollowed out tortoise shells work as an ideal cover in the construction of the purifiers.
The casing of a Small Lantern works perfectly to bring the device together. You should be able to purchase such a lantern from the Thaell Ew shopkeeper in the east temple.

---

!

---

Hunt down some Scratched Dinosaur Bones 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill dinosaurs until you get 2x "Scratched Dinosaur Bone" (will take up to six kills)._
Obtain two Spotless Tortoise Shells 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill "tortoise" mobs until you get 2x "Spotless Tortoise Shell"._
Purchase a Small Lantern 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_"Small Lantern" is sold by "a Thaell Ew shopkeeper"._ You can hand someone a small lantern and that will register as "purchased". So you can just send one of your group to get lanterns for all.

---

Craft an Unfinished Tracker's Water Extractor 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Although a few pieces are missing from the finished product, simply cobble these components together in a fletching kit as best you can and Supplier Thissus will worry about the rest. You should be able to purchase a Fletching Kit from the Thaell Ew haggler that has set up shop in our central temple.
_Inside a "Fletching Kit" (sold by "a Thaell Ew haggler"), combine:_
2x "Scratched Dinosaur Bone" + 2x "Spotless Tortoise Shell" + 1x "Small Lantern" to produce "Unfinished Tracker's Water Extractor"

---

Return the Extractor to Supplier Thissus 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Hand the "Unfinished Tracker's Water Extractor" to Supplier Thissus._
Supplier Thissus looks over the unfinished device and then gives you an eerie lizardman smile, 'Thisss will do nicely. Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_.'
Thanks to your efforts the tribe will have a reasonable supply of water purifiers for the time being.
_Rewards:_
Faction with The Thaell Ew (+40)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Right Digits --OR-- 25 platinum
_It isn't known whether this task is repeatable._
- Tiny Parrot's Right Digits [item=119764]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the second in a series of three. It begins with Historian Fjilnauk ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with The Kromtus
\- Completed "Honoring the Elders"
_Say "willing" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Historian Fjilnauk'
Historian Fjilnauk says 'Welcome back, small one.' The great giant smiles, 'I must say, the diligence and care you displayed in conducting our memorial ceremony has assured me that you are trustworthy. As trustworthy as any \_\_\_\_\_ can be anyway. I have a more perilous task for you, if you are [willing] to undertake it.'
You say, 'I am willing'
Historian Fjilnauk says 'During the night one of the wild jungle beasts made its way into the village and escaped with one of the precious few artifacts that dates back to our first generation. It is rare that the creatures are bold enough to make such a raid, but as pacifists we cannot meet their aggression with aggression of our own. Though we have no rules that forbid accepting the help of outsiders willing to fight on our behalf...'
You have been assigned the task 'Recovering Our History'.
Historian Fjilnauk needs you to help recover an ancient Kromtus artifact that was stolen by the increasingly violent dinosaurs. Given how little of the clan's history has been preserved from the last generations of elders every single trinket and relic holds immense historical value.

---

!

---

Search the stomachs of a few raptors 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ See if the artifact was taken by the roguish raptors.
_Kill any two "raptor" mobs (the named raptor counts for this too -- all are located west of the river in the center of the island)._
a young raptor has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a young raptor's corpse jerks violently as the life falls from its eyes.
It appears the raptors are not in possession of the relic you seek. Perhaps the ankylosauruses have it!

---

Check inside a few ankylosauruses 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Perhaps the lumbering ankylosauruses are to blame for the theft.
_Kill any two "ankylosaurus" mobs. These are located in the chunk of land sandwiched on all sides by the central river._
an ankylosaurus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
an ankylosaurus's corpse screeches in agony before falling silent.
No sign of the relic in any of the ankylosauruses so far. Perhaps the tyrannosauruses have it!

---

Open up a tyrannosaurus 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill any "tyrannosaurus" mob (the named tyrannosaurus counts for this too). These are located in the far western part of the zone._
a tyrannosaurus hatchling has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a tyrannosaurus hatchling's corpse shakes the forest with the cries of its death!
You dig through the tyrannosaurus carcass and retrieve a damaged but distinctively Kromtus looking idol.

---

Return the Chewed Kromtus Idol to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Historian Fjilnauk carefully accepts the artifact, cupping it in both hands as he brings it closer to his face to inspect the damage that has been done to it. He sheds a single tear but his voice remains strong, 'Once again I am indebted to you, small one. When our tribe renounced violence and literature we knew it wouldn't be easy. My anger toward the wild beasts is tempered by the understanding that they do not act out of malice but simply instinct.' He pauses, 'But still... It does not sadden me to see you strike a blow upon them for their transgressions.' He smiles, 'I have a final task to ask of you, if you are willing.'
You're a bit amused by Fjilnauk's willingness to have others draw blood given his purported pacifism, but work is work and it seems there is one last job he might have for you.
_Rewards:_
Faction with The Kromtus (+35)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Cervical Vertebrae --OR-- 50 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Cervical Vertebrae [item=121488]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the third in a series of three. It begins with Historian Fjilnauk ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with The Kromtus
\- Completed "Honoring the Elders"
\- Completed "Recovering Our History"
_Say "willing" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Historian Fjilnauk'
Historian Fjilnauk says '\_\_\_\_\_, I am honored to see you again. I believe it is time I share the intent of my work here. You see, in additional to adhering to the tenet of strict pacifism, our tribe has forbidden all form of written language. Our entire history for several generations has been passed down from elder to child. I fear this means that much of our storied past has been lost. The memory of the elders grows ever more distant each day. I would not defy our laws by scribing what I know to stone but I do suspect there is much that can be learned if any lost artifacts of our tribe can be recovered. I humbly request, if you are [willing], that you set out in search of these relics and return them to me so that I can decipher what the elders cannot recall.''
You say, 'Yes, I am willing...'
Historian Fjilnauk says 'If any artifacts of our people still exist they will probably rest in the hands of other tribes. I cannot say where this may be, but someone as well traveled as you probably has a few ideas of where to start.'
You have been assigned the task 'Memories of the Kromtus'.
Historian Fjilnauk needs you to travel to the lands where the tribes of giants once united in service to Rallos and recover any artifact that may have belonged to the Kromtus clan.
The Dromrek clan that dwells in Goru`kar Mesa are among the most violent and simple giant tribes on Antonica. Scour their caves and see if anything out of the ordinary turns up.
In the foothills of Icefall Glacier in the lands called the Steppes there is an old Krithgorian burial site. Search the site for any relics that bear symbology foreign to the Krithgor clan.
Once you reach the gates of Valdeholm in Icefall Glacier, take note of the totems that stand on opposite sides of the narrow valley leading into the city. These monuments to Rallos are often constructed out of weapons and armor of fallen warriors. See if you can spot anything that might have belonged to the Kromtus clan.
At the far north end of the city of Valedholm you will find King Odeen's manor. Search the king's library for anything that may have some written history about our people.
Below the city of Valdeholm are the treacherous catacombs of their ancient dead. Take care as you delve deeper into this burial site. Look for the frozen Warlord as he oversees a small arena of battle. Search the statue's feet for any relic that has resisted the bitter cold.

Unearth a Kromtus Runestone 0/1 (Goru`Kar Mesa)
_Head to Goru`Kar Mesa and find a stone at /loc 50, 2020, 9. Click it to receive an "Anachronistic Kromtus Runestone"._
You examine the runestone that sits precariously balanced on the stalagmite in the center of the cave. It has faded with age but you make out depictions of trees, blades and shields along with some text written in a language you can't quite make out. You pick up the runestone in hopes that it is in fact one of the Kromtus Relics.
Recover a Kromtus Shield 0/1 (The Steppes)
_Head to the Steppes and find a shield at /loc 2580, -3, 160. Click it to receive a "Battered Kromtus Shield"._
A battered shield sits discarded in the corner. Upon closer inspection you make out several inscriptions that have been marred by the wear of heavy battle, 'For one who will march while cowards hide and fall where heroes lie.' You hoist the large shield onto your back, assured that this is one of the ancient Kromtus relics.
Obtain a Kromtus Helm 0/1 (Icefall Glacier)
_Head to Icefall Glacier and find a helmet at /loc 2310, -130, 705 (on top of the eastern totem). Click it to receive a "Worn Kromtus Helm"._
A giant's helmet sits half buried in snow at the top of this monument. Its age is hardly apparent upon inspection due to the magnificence of its construction. The metal, though heavily dented, shines pristinely. You brace yourself against the stone monument below and pull with all of your might and are able to dislodge the ancient piece of armor from the snow.
Borrow a Tome of the Tribes 0/1 (Valdeholm)
_Head to Valdeholm and find some books at /loc 3880, -985, 475. Click them to receive "Hoary Tome of the Tribes". This item is on top of one of the bookshelves. You might want to use levitate + a mount of some sort to get close enough to it._
You look up at the stack of tomes that rests atop the bookshelf, lodged between two volumes on royal lineage is a tome entitled, 'On the Tribes That Marched'. You pull the book down and look over the first few pages. You soon realize that the short tome gives a detailed account of every giant clan in Rallos' army at the time of his invasion on the Rathe. This will clearly be of interest to Historian Fjilnauk.
Exhume a Kromtus Longsword 0/1 (Frostcrypt, Throne of the Shade King)
_Head to Frostcrypt and find a sword at /loc 1470, -365, -129. Click on it to receive "Ancient Kromtus Longsword". As with the Valdeholm update, you'll probably need a levitate effect or Rocketeer Boots to reach it. It's a giant sword on the statue's immediate east side._
To the side of the frozen statue of the Warlord sits a sword somehow kept in pristine condition. Shining runes are cut into the blade just above the hilt, their pattern matches other Kromtus relics that you have come across. You look around the room to ensure you are not being watched then carefully take the blade down from its resting place.

---

Bring the Kromtus Runestone to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Bring the Kromtus Shield to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Bring the Kromtus Helm to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Bring the Tome of the Tribes to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Bring the Kromtus Longsword to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Historian Fjilnauk looks upon the artifacts you have presented him in disbelief. He speaks with renewed excitement, 'Simply incredible! To finally know our tribe once tread upon so much of the world. These instruments of war speak to a time when we were strong, capable of winning wars and toppling nations. This tome speaks of a proud lineage of generals, elders whose lives ended on the field of battle rather than withering away in solitude.' The giant pauses then looks to the west, 'Something stirs near the village square. You should investigate, small one.'
An elder giant spirit has taken form in the center of town. Step closer to learn of its purpose.

---

Listen to the ancient Kromtus spirit 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ An old Kromtus spirit has taken form in the center of town. Stand in its presence and listen to what it has to say. Should the spirit be preoccupied by another or you fail to heed its words, return to Historian Fjilnauk and speak to him to have the spirit make a new appearance.
_Upon turning in all five items, "The Spirit of Hjirvlauk" a short distance southwest of the Historian. Stand near it while it tells a story, and be prepared for mob aggro. Soon, 1x "a restless shade" spawns and attack (max hit ~17,000; immune to mez; but is rootable/snareable; five-minute timer to kill). Soon after its death, another 2x "a restless shade" spawn and attack (same abilities; 8-minute timer to kill)._
_If you fail the encounter or otherwise leave it idle after having spawned it, you can hail the Historian to respawn it. The same applies if you're having to wait for another player to do it._
_Failure to kill the ambush mobs within their timers will cause the encounter to reset._
You need to be closer to the ancient giant's spirit to hear it.
The giant looks down at you, its ethereal face contorted in an expression of remorse. Without moving its mouth its voice consumes your thoughts, but he speaks in words you do not understand.
In your mind you see the Kromtus runestone, it is affixed to a battered suit of armor that rests upon the chest of a mighty warrior. The sky behind the warrior is blood red. The shouting of battle begins to overtake your ears right before the vision is lost.
The giant spirit's voice continues speaking to you and suddenly images of the now battered Kromtus shield coalesce into something lucid. The shield appears to you, unmarred with battle scars. It sits with several identical replicas in an armory. Your vision turns from the armory to several orcs that stand nearby. You quickly shake your head and regain sight of the land you stand in.
The spirit begins to move its hands in an elaborate gesture before calming its motions and drawing its fingers over the top of its head. You think back to the snow covered helmet you unearthed in Icefall Glacier atop the Valdeholm post.
Suddenly a second spirit takes form. This one does not appear to be as friendly!
a restless shade says 'Die coward!'
a restless shade has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a restless shade says 'Soon...'
That giant's attention returns to you, its gestures now smooth. Its foreign words carry a friendlier tone, it seems to be thankful. It continues to speak at length but little makes sense. Of all its words the only syllables that stick with you are what sounds like a name, 'Hjirvlauk?. You repeat the name, 'Hjirvlauk' and the shade nods slowly.
The spirit holds out its tightly clenched right fist. It places its left fist over it and moves its left arm up slowly, as if expressing the shape of a blade. He swings his right hand to the side and points the imaginary blade toward the sky. His disembodied voice turns to a shout then to heartfelt laughter and finally a sigh.
The spirit's gestures are suddenly interrupted by two voices, shouting in unison in common tongue!
a restless shade shouts, 'Hjirvlauk!'
a restless shade says 'Our blood is on your hands!'
a restless shade has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a restless shade says 'Death is no escape.'
a restless shade has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a restless shade says 'Narikor will soon claim you!'
The spirit of Hjirvlauk takes a final look around the village of giants that appear oblivious to his presence then returns his attention to you. A sense of mutual respect resonates between the two of you. He holds out his hand and points toward the Kromtus historian that remains enraptured by Hjirvlauk's earthly artifacts. The spirit nods, its disembodied voice now simply a whisper in your mind as it says its last word and the first word you are able to make sense of, 'Peace'.

---

Speak to Historian Fjilnauk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Historian Fjilnauk to tell him what you have learned from the shade of Hjirvlauk.
You say, 'Hail, Historian Fjilnauk'
Historian Fjilnauk listens intently as you recount the elaborate pageantry of Hjirvlauk's spirit and the harrowing battle of the enraged undead. The historian remains stoic as his expression changes from interest to wonder as if a great revelation has just dawned upon him. He speaks, turning his attention to the nearest elder then back to you, 'Everything you have told me suggests that we did in fact march proudly in the Warlord's army and now suffer for it. Yet we possess the capacity to recover while others do not. Yes, our elders forbade written language and violence for this very reason, so that we could escape the cycle of war. Small one, I have no treasures great enough to express the depths of my gratitude for what you have shown me today. Please, accept this humble offering and know that I am eternally grateful for your help.'
It seems the elders' refusal to pass on the tribe's history to successive generations may have been an intentional one. Historian Fjilnauk will have to reflect upon whether or not he truly wishes to preserve the tribe's past.
_Rewards:_
Faction with The Kromtus (+68)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Furcula --OR-- 100 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Furcula [item=120461]

Sweep up some of the molerat mess 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Objects called "molerat mess" are found in Kromtus houses. Right-click the broom to update the task._
You feel a bit nauseated as you use your broom to sweep the foul mess into your bucket.
You sweep up the mess into the bucket.
As you sweep the last of the molerat mess into your bucket you slam the broom against the floor and it shatters into shards of wood. Someone else can be bothered to clean 'that' mess up later...

Dump the contents of the bucket in the Kromtus fishing cove 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)

_Go underwater in the Kromtus Fishing Cove (east of the giant village) and right-click the bucket._
You empty the contents of the bucket into the Kromtus fishing cove and are immediately overcome with the desire to swim back to shore to minimize the amount of time you spend wading around in rat filth...

Speak with Scout Krenk 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
You say, 'Hail, Scout Krenk'
Scout Krenk claps his hands, 'Wonderful, small one! You have very clearly earned your pay today. Just look at the sweat of your brow and the wafting odor of your clothes.' He gingerly divvies out a few coins into your hands. 'You made sure to dump the rancid mess far away from the village right? The elders would have my head if they found any more mess making its way back into the village!' You nod, saying nothing, and decide to be on your way.
Your previous sympathy for the scout's situation now weighs against the petty sum he has awarded you with...

a tyrannosaurus hatchling has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a tyrannosaurus hatchling's corpse shakes the forest with the cries of its death!

_Kill as many ankylosauruses as it takes to get an "Ankylosaurus Hide Spike"._
an ankylosaurus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
an ankylosaurus's corpse screeches in agony before falling silent.

Wing, the chief armory officer of the Bloodfeather tribe, is also tasked with ensuring that the village is kept well stocked in not only basic rations but also delicacies. He has no problem ensuring a steady supply of bare necessities, but keeping a stock of the finer foods has proven difficult, so he has enlisted your help in obtaining these rarities.

_This solo task is the first in a series of three and begins with Talon ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with Bloodfeather Aviaks
_Say "willing" to request the task._
_Without minimum faction, you see:_
Talon says 'There is much for you to do to help my people before I can trust you. Commander Wing or the child Etsyl may have work for you, or you could clear away some of the dinosaur threat that roams the central jungle.'
_With apprehensive+ faction, you see:_
Talon says 'So this is the \_\_\_\_\_ my village has been speaking of. Welcome to you, \_\_\_\_\_. I am Talon, leader of the Bloodfeathers. I am sure you have many questions but I must first interject with a request. You have garnered the reputation of being a formidable warrior and so I must ask if you are [willing] to help us with a growing threat.'
You say, 'I am willing'
Talon says 'I knew your reputation was not an exaggeration. Help us stem the threat of the corrupted guardians that dwell to the south and I shall be happy to answer any of your questions.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Ebonwing Threat'.
Talon, the Bloodfeather tribe's leader, is concerned with the corrupted creatures that tend to the Ebonwing nest just south of the village. These tenders and guardians are subservient to the Ebonwing Matriarch, which the Bloodfeather tribe lives in fear of, but they have proven dangerous as they have a tendency to attack or even kill any egret or scout that gets too close to the nest.
Talon would like you to kill a few of the guardians and tenders near the nest. He fears that having his own warriors carry out this task would endanger the lives of the entire tribe should the Ebonwing Matriarch take the action as a sign of aggression toward her chicks.

---

!

---

Kill several Ebonwing tenders and guardians 0/4 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill four ebonwing guardians and/or tenders (the nest is due south of the Aviak village)._

---

Speak with Talon 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
You say, 'Hail, Talon'
Talon listens intently as you recount the details of your excursion, 'Good, good. This is a fine first step but I fear there is more to do. The situation is delicate in that we must not incur the wrath of the grand Ebonwing. Upsetting her would prove more deadly than a war with the lumbering beasts to the west. I have a plan but I will need your help in the future. Return to me when you believe you are ready.'
Talon is thankful for your efforts but believes there is more to be done.
_Rewards:_
Faction with Bloodfeather Aviaks (+40)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Lumbar Vertebrae --OR-- 50 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Lumbar Vertebrae [item=119765]

_This solo task is the second in a series of three and begins with Talon ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with Bloodfeather Aviaks
\- Completion of "The Ebonwing Threat"
_Say "help" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Talon'
Talon says '\_\_\_\_\_, just the \_\_\_\_\_ I wanted to see. As you no doubt surmised, the guardians and tenders that inhabit the Ebonwing nest are merely servants to the Ebonwing Matriarch. Although we have not seen sign of the Matriarch in years we have lived in fear of her presence ever since we arrived on the island. Given malevolent nature of these tenders I fear the longer they remain in service to the Matriarch the more likely it is that she will be turned against us. So I must once again ask for your [help].'
You say, 'What help do you need?'
Talon begins detailing his plan to turn the Matriarch against the malevolent tenders.
You have been assigned the task 'Softening the Shells'.
Talon, the Bloodfeather tribe's leader, believes that a bit of subterfuge is the best plan to deal with the Ebonwing tenders. He suspects that if the Ebonwing eggs end up damaged with no obvious connection to the Bloodfeathers that the Ebonwing Matriarch will suspect the tenders to be at fault.
Extract a bit of venom from a glistenwing butterfly. Extract the blood of a vicious parasite. Harvest the stinger from one of the colossal soulsippers that roam the jungle.

Poison the eggs in the Ebonwing nest 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Do your best to sneak into the Ebonwing nest and spread the poison before you are overrun by irate birds.
_Head into the Ebonwing nest at about /loc 900, -1600, 334 (due south of the aviak village), and right-click the toxin. Note that all of the vulture-looking Ebonwings see invisibility. There are three ravens inside the nest, all of which are a PH for the named raven Hemocoraxius, so use caution here._
You spread a bit of the toxic substance over each of the eggs in the hive and watch as small cracks form in the dark shells.

Talon listens intently as you recount the details of your assignment. He nods then takes a moment to consider the situation. Seconds pass before he speaks, 'We will have to see how this plays out. When you return to me I will either have good news or a final assignment for you. Thank you for all the help you have provided, \_\_\_\_\_.'
Talon must now assess the situation. Return to him when you are ready to see how his plan has played out.

_This solo task is the third in a series of three and begins with Talon ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Minimum apprehensive faction with Bloodfeather Aviaks
\- Completion of "The Ebonwing Threat"
\- Completion of "Softening the Shells"
_Say "plan" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Talon'
Talon says '\_\_\_\_\_, grave news. During the night one of our scouts reported spotting the shadow of a creature larger than any dinosaur that roams the land. The Matriarch may have returned home. We must make haste with the final steps of our plan. It is no longer safe for you to approach the Ebonwing nest without a disguise. The tenders clearly understand that you have been working with us, but I have a [plan]...'
You say, 'What plan?'
Talon begins whispering the details of a convoluted plan to capture one of the Ebonwing chicks for study.
You have been assigned the task 'In Shadow of Moonlight'.
Talon, the Bloodfeather tribe leader, fears his plans to implicate the corrupted guardians for the loss of several Ebonwing eggs has failed. He is now convinced that the only way he will be able to ensure the safety of his tribe is to abduct and study one of the Ebonwing chicks.
Talon fears that the corrupted guardians will now recognize you if you try and infiltrate the nest again so this plan begins with the construction of a disguise.
He wants you to recover the skin of a Thaell Ew scout but does not want you to enact violence upon a member of the Thaell Ew tribe; such an action may spark a war he is not willing to fight. Instead, he suspects that the remains of a Thaell Ew could be found in the belly of a triceratops.

---

P 1648.8253, -935.6730, 340.6729, 0, 0, 0, 1, chick
!

---

Recover a Lizardman Skin from a triceratops 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill any triceratops to receive "Flattened Lizardman Skin"._
a triceratops hatchling has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a triceratops hatchling's corpse's lifeless mass shakes the ground as it falls.
You make a quick incision in the lumbering beast's side and begin rummaging around its colossal stomach for lizardman remains. It takes you mere seconds to pull out 'most of' a Thaell Ew scout's flayed husk...

---

Deliver the Lizardman Skin to Talon 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Upon turn-in, you receive a "Crude Lizardman Disguise" and "Concentrated Avian Sedative"._
Talon cringes as you present the flayed lizardman skin to him. He holds it at arm's length and sighs, 'Couldn't have found one that wasn't  still dripping?' He sets it on the floor and begins cleaning the innards away before revealing a shoddy dagger affixed to a ball of twine which he then uses to stitch up the torn sections of skin. Several minutes pass before he presents you with the dried lizardman skin disguise. 'It's not all that  pretty, but it'll be enough to trick those stupid birds. Wait until nightfall then make your way to their nest again. Oh, and you'll need this.'

---

Wait until nightfall to don the disguise 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Talon's disguise crafting skill has proven, less than optimal, you should wait until nightfall to don the disguise and make your way into the Ebonwing nest.
_Night time is between 7:00 P.M. and 4:00 A.M. game time (/time). Wait until then, and then right-click the disguise._
You place the lizardman skin over your body. Despite Talon's earlier efforts to clean the hide you feel a little uncomfortable by how wet the inside of the disguise feels against your skin.

---

Make your way to the Ebonwing nest and capture a chick 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Sneak into the Ebonwing nest under the aid of darkness and weaken an Ebonwing chick enough to sedate it with the concoction Talon has provided you. If you somehow lose your disguise Talon may be able to provide you with another.
_You can find 1x "an Ebonwing chick" and 3x "a bloodtear Ebonwing" in and around the nest of the Ebonwings (found up the pathway due south of the Aviak village). DPS one of these mobs to 40% health, and then click the Sedative. This results in a "Sedated Ebonwing Chick". (Note: These mobs have no leash range, so you can pull them to any distance.)_
You splash a bit of the avian sedative in the Ebonwing chick's face. It flaps its wings violently then flutters lazily to the ground. You scoop the tired creature up into your arms.

---

Return the Sedated Ebonwing Chick to Talon 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
Talon carefully accepts the sleeping bird and looks it over. He hands it to one of his close advisors and turns to you, 'Once again your help has proven more than invaluable, \_\_\_\_\_. Although your monetary reward pales in comparison to your efforts, I offer you the humble gratitude of my entire tribe. The chick you have recovered will allow us to better understand the Grand Matriarch and perhaps someday overcome the shadow of her capricious tyranny. Should we ever need your aid again we will call upon you as a hero to our tribe.'
Talon remains indebted to you for the help in carrying out his sometimes convoluted plans. You are left wondering if the colossal Ebonwing he speaks of will not think the Thaell Ew guilty of stealing one of her chicks...
_Rewards:_
Faction with Bloodfeather Aviaks (+42)
--AND--
Tiny Parrot's Left Carpometacarpus --OR-- 100 platinum
_This task is repeatable with a 6-hour lockout timer from the time of request._
- Tiny Parrot's Left Carpometacarpus [item=120421]

_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Note: Silwat Viltor only speaks Elder Dragon (90+ skill level required in order to converse with him). Without speaking Elder Dragon, you see:_
Silwat Viltor appears to have no idea what you are saying.
_At 90+ Elder Dragon (trainable at guildmasters, if you're needing it), he'll speak with you._
_Say "what" to request the task._
Silwat Viltor looks at you through ancient glassy eyes. You suddenly take note of the wyvern's skin which shares the texture of aged stone, cracked and brittle. He looms his head closer to yours before speaking, his voice sounds strained as if each word he utters is a struggle, '\_\_\_\_\_, long I have yearned to hear my language echoed back to me. These, children, some call them lesser. They cannot share the gift of conversation but they cry out, anguished, terrified.' He breathes heavily, 'You, \_\_\_\_\_! You must do [what] I cannot, for their sake.'
You say, 'What is that?'
Silwat Viltor turns his gaze to the drogmors across the river, 'They suffer, their aggression is the symptom of a greater disease. You must go from here, gather these things and return. Together', he pauses, taking another deep breath, 'together we will help them.'
You have been assigned the task 'Aiding the Ailing'.
Silwat Viltor, a surprisingly ancient wyvern, needs you to collect a few ingredients to concoct an antidote to treat the ailing drogmors.
The docile tortoises that roam the Kromtus fishing cove exhibit a long natural life and even temperament. Although the death of one creature to save another makes for an elaborate moral quandary, Silwat's concerns are with the drogmors. Extract a bit of tortoise blood.
The ferocious swordfish that swim in the deep waters of the Kromtus fishing cove exhibit a liveliness that Silwat hopes to utilize in curing the drogmors. Extract a bit of impaler blood.
The final component Silwat requires for his elixir is a few strands of Riverweed. This plant can be found at the bottom of the ponds formed by rivers around the island. Submerge yourself in the ponds on the interior of the island and forage for some Riverweed.

---

!

---

Extract the blood of a freshly slain tortoise 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill "tortoise" mobs until you receive an "Unusual Tortoise Blood"._
Extract blood from freshly slain impalers 0/2 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Kill "an impaler" mobs until you have 2x "Discolored Swordfish Blood"._
Forage some riverweed from the nearby waters 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_"Riverweed" supposedly can be foraged in the deeper waters of the river in the central of the zone, but it can also be foraged on land._
_Known locations are: +448 +1689, make sure you are submerged in the water._
_The vicinity of Lyirae Oakwynd's roaming area has been known to work as well._
_You don't have to forage it yourself, a forager friend can give it to you and it will update this step._

---

Deliver the components to Silwat 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Hand in all four items at once to Silwat Viltor to receive a "Simple Antibody Concoction"._
Silwat Viltor gazes upon your reflection in the water, 'Your hasty return reinforces my confidence in your resolve, perhaps I was too quick to judge you, \_\_\_\_\_. Set the ingredients down here in the water.'
You set the ingredients down in the water at the old wyvern's feet. He stares intently at them and the water begins to stir. A flash of light emanating from Silwat's feet blinds you for a moment. As your vision returns a small vial bobs gently where the loose ingredients once stirred. You grab the vial, knowing what you must do next.

---

Sedate and inoculate a drogmor 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Given the erratic behavior the drogmors have been presenting lately it is not likely that you will be able to deliver the antidote to it without first weakening it. Do your best to avoid killing the creature as you weaken it then apply a bit of the antibody around its head, hopefully that will be enough to calm the creature back to its natural docility.
_DPS any drogmor down to 40% health, and right-click the concoction to update._
_Emote If Too Soon:_ The drogmor trashes violently. You'll need to weaken it a bit more before giving it the antibody.
_Emote Update:_ You splash the antibody over the drogmor's face. It snaps angrily at you at first but after mere moments it grows increasingly amiable. It emits a low peaceful roar in your direction before turning and running away into the jungle.

---

Return to Silwat Viltor 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
_Task Window Says:_ Let Silwat Viltor know you were successful in curing at least one of the ailing drogmors.
_Make sure your language is set to Elder Dragon. or try hail him in common, NOT elder dragon._
You say, 'Hail, Silwat Viltor'
The skin around the ancient wyvern's mouth grinds like fractured stone as he beams with the beginnings of a smile. He slowly turns his gaze toward the remaining drogmors, pleased to know that their suffering will eventually be mitigated.
Silwat Viltor is pleased with your assistance and will likely have more work for you when you are ready.
_Rewards:_
Skeletal Parrot's Synsacrum --OR-- 50 platinum
_Task repeatability isn't known: You may only be able to repeat by redoing the series after completion, but this isn't known for sure._
- Skeletal Parrot's Synsacrum [item=120446]

---

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is the third in a series of three and begins with a wyvern named Silwat Viltor ( **click for map image**) on Thuliasaur Island._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Completed "Aiding the Ailing"
\- Completed "Our Simple Purpose"
_Note: Silwat Viltor only speaks Elder Dragon (90+ skill level required in order to converse with him)._
_Say "find" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Silwat Viltor'
Silwat Viltor says, in Elder Dragon, 'The elders refer to your kind as mortals, for the extent of your lifetime is a blink of their eye. To live as long as I have, a lesser, is no small feat, \_\_\_\_\_. I watched those who departed our home with me wither away here, yet I remain.' The ancient wyvern pauses, breathes slowly and continues, 'It is time my task ended. You must [find] the one who sent us and tell them we are done.'
You say, 'I'll find the one...'
Silwat Viltor says, in Elder Dragon, 'Return to the first scar and seek the lineage of my order.'
You have been assigned the task 'To Whom It May Concern'.
Silwat Viltor arrival upon the island dates back centuries. He has outlived all that have since set out to make contact with him. He is well aware that he would not survive the return home but hopes that you can speak with those who sent him here on his behalf.
Silwat traces his lineage to the land where the Crystalline Dragon herself first scarred the planet. Travel to the Western Wastes to where the wyverns now dwell.

---

!
!

---

Seek out Silwat's liaison in Western Wastes 0/1 (Western Wastes)
_Head to the Western Wastes and to the wyvern caves in the northeastern part of the zone (entrance is in the vicinity of /loc 2550, -3100). Go into the northwestern-most room of these caves._

---

Inspect the suspicious remains 0/1 (Western Wastes)
_Task Window Says:_ As you move deeper into the caves you suspect the ones who sent Silwat upon his expedition may no longer be of mortal form. The wyvern remains that rest in the corner of this room catch your eye, perhaps you should inspect them more closely.
_In the corner of this area, you'll find "suspicious remains". /inspect this to spawn "Tyglaria Co`Votaliar" (non-aggro)._

---

Speak to the spectre of Silwat's liaison 0/1 (Western Wastes)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with the spectre of Silwat's liaison to learn what has happened since his departure.
_Note: Like Silway, Tyglaria Co`Votaliar only speaks Elder Dragon (90+ skill level required in order to converse with her)._
You say, 'Hail, Tyglaria Co`Votaliar'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar the wyvern appears nearly as ancient Silwat, though obviously not as long lived. It rears its head back in confusion, 'I have slumbered too long. How did you come to be here, \_\_\_\_\_?'
(Perhaps mentioning that [Silwat sent you] will make the creature more amiable to conversation.)
You say, 'Silwat sent me'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar's voice echoes through the otherwise empty room, 'Viltor still draws breath? It has been centuries since Viltor and I last spoke. His name is lost memory for the Claws, though perhaps not the [traitors]. What concern is this to me though, \_\_\_\_\_?'
You say, 'Traitors?'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar snarls, his ghostly voice now tinged with rage, 'Those who fractured our empire and whose upstart hubris will be the death of us all. The Dars, those fools. The [Ring] as they call themselves.'
You say, 'What Ring?'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar seems to be growing increasingly angry, 'That lesser elder who dispatched Silwat upon his task. His name is lost to me. I simply know that upon our division he cowered in the shadow of Jaled in the presence of Kildrukaun. He escaped with his life, not all of us were so [lucky]...'
You say, 'Lucky?'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar says, in Elder Dragon, 'I tire of you, \_\_\_\_\_, but for Viltor's sake heed with these words. You will find your elder where the bones of our long dead rest under the skies of fire.'
Tyglaria Co`Votaliar fades into the darkness.

---

Seek out Efilrak of the Ring in Skyfire Mountains 0/1 (Skyfire Mountains)
_Task Window Says:_ It seems that Silwat was dispatched to the island of ancient dinosaurs long before the division between the Claws of Veeshan and the Ring of Scale. Tyglaria's death during the split has and should remain a secret, lest the two sides find a reason to reignite their feud. Tyglaria's only insight to the situation is the whereabouts of the elder dragon responsible for Silwat's task, "Where the bones of the long dead rest under the skies of fire."
_Head to the Skyfire Mountains, to the area where wizards port into the zone (around /loc -3000, 1000, -100: many dragon bones mark the area). "Efilrak of the Ring" spawns nearby (this mob is aggro, but NOT auto-aggro and spawns far enough away that it won't immediately aggro)._
The bones of the ancient dead begin to stir!
Efilrak of the Ring's roar shakes the valley around you! You get the impression the dragon's spirit is not in a communicative mood.

---

Subdue the enraged ghost of Efilrak 0/1 (Skyfire Mountains)
_Task Window Says:_ The bones of Silwat's commander now stir, his spirit now twisted, enraged and intent on your destruction!
_DPS Efilrak of the Ring to 15% health to subdue it. This is an average difficulty fight for Thuliasaur-level mobs. It can be pulled away from its spawn point, but will warp back when it goes non-aggro:_
Efilrak of the Ring roars, 'ENOUGH! Speak your purpose, mortal!'

---

Speak with the ghost of Efilrak 0/1 (Skyfire Mountains)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak to the now subdued Efilrak regarding Silwat's final orders.
You say, 'Hail, Efilrak of the Ring'
Efilrak of the Ring's voice is consumed by hatred from his humiliation, 'Speak your purpose, \_\_\_\_\_!'
(You think it best to quickly mention [Silwat's mission] before the spirit stops humoring you.)
You say, 'What is Silwat's mission?'
Efilrak of the Ring's voice sheds its former rage, 'Viltor draws breath!?' The ancient dragon begins to laugh maniacally, 'A shame I did not [deal] with him as I did Co`Votaliar. One of only two regrets...'
You say, 'What deal did you have?'
Efilrak of the Ring's skull swings down to meet you eye to eye, 'All lesser beasts of our lineage should be expunged! Viltor's errand and breath should have ended centuries ago with his and their extinction. I was a fool to think that watching them might reveal some greater purpose! If only those damned usurpers had the foresight to heed me in [life].'
You say, 'What life?'
Efilrak of the Ring raises his skull high into the air, 'The Dars are weak, too scared to do what must be done. They found it easier to silence me than to put down their own pets...'
You suspect Efilrak will have nothing more of interest to reveal.

---

Return to Silwat Viltor 0/1 (Thuliasaur Island)
You say, 'Hail, Silwat Viltor'
Silwat Viltor remains motionless as you recount Efilrak's words. As you conclude your story he takes a heavy breath, years of contemplation now weighing upon him. Tense moments of silence pass as the aged wyvern stares blankly into the distance. His glassy eyes close as his raspy voice breaks the silence, 'Until my last breath I shall serve as guardian to my kin.'
Silwat Viltor opens his eyes and offers you his heartfelt gratitude. He remains undeterred in his mission to watch over the lesser dragonkin of the island.
_Rewards:_
Skeletal Parrot's Innominate Bones --OR-- 50 platinum
_Task repeatability isn't known: You may only be able to repeat by redoing the series after completion, but this isn't known for sure._
- Skeletal Parrot's Innominate Bones [item=120448]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task begins with Captain Mewes ( **click for map image**), located on the deck of the boat docked in the Combine Dredge._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "disrupt" to request the task._
_Note: A November 2014 patch included in its notes:_
\- The Pirate and the Pea - Clarified a few points and fixed a few text errors. Captain Mewes now asks to have the bucket returned to him.
**(Details needed about these changes for the writeup below.)**
You say, 'Hail, Captain Mewes'
Captain Mewes says 'Shhhhhhh. Don't let [them] see you talking to me.'
You say, 'Who is 'them'?'
Captain Mewes says 'Those horrible Doomscale. They do such dreadful things to me, but I can [explain] later. I have some [tasks] for you if you want to help me stick a [wrench] in their work, but you can't let them know I sent you. If you are in a hurry and don't want to hear me prattle on, I need you to [disrupt] their rest, [slow] their mining, and [hamper] their research.'
You say, 'No, explain now...'
Captain Mewes looks around to be sure the two of you aren't being observed. `Yes. Lanys, that... that...' Captain Mewes mumbles something so no-one can hear. 'Lanys and her Doomscale cronies captured my ship and killed all my guards and much of my crew. Then, once we got here, she made me do [horrible] things!
You say, 'Horrible things?'
Captain Mewes says 'I'm just the captain of a merchant ship. Sure, I like to dress like a pirate, but I'm not! And she... She [makes] me torture cultists who don't give the proper prayer and obedience. She even makes me execute her prisoners!'
You say, 'She makes you?'
Captain Mewes says 'Okay, okay, I'm not brave. I'm a coward. But if I don't do what she asks me to, she will to do it to me, instead. I just want to go home!'
You say, 'I'll disrupt their rest...'
Captain Mewes says 'Thank you. I have written an explanation in your journal, but if you like I can give you more [details].'
You have been assigned the task 'The Pirate and the Pea'.
Captain Mewes is not happy about his treatment. He wants you to complete tasks to disrupt the Doomscale and Combine efforts. For this task he wants you to make their rest time uncomfortable.
You say, 'About those details...'
Captain Mewes says 'You will be putting [pebbles] in their [beds] and [salt water] in their [ale barrels]. I also want you to travel to their [housing platform] and kill some of the [guards] along the way.'
You say, 'Where do I find the pebbles?'
Captain Mewes says 'You can gather the pebbles down below in the water of this cove. Check under where my ship is trapped.'
You say, 'Where are their beds?'
Captain Mewes says 'In the [housing platform] you'll find multiple beds. Approach them and place your pebbles. Sleeping people may not notice you when you place the pebbles, but I wouldn't count on it. I would count on anyone who is awake in the room being aggressive.'
You say, 'What housing platform?'
Captain Mewes says 'The housing platform is the eastmost platform. Take the northerly path between the two exits from the dock, and continue until you're under the platform. The ramp into it is on the east side of the central pillar.'
You say, 'Where are their ale barrels?'
Captain Mewes says 'The ale barrels can be found on both floors of the [housing platform]. The salt will make it so bad they may even act like a landlubber and spew on the deck.'
You say, 'You want salt water in them?'
Captain Mewes says 'Yes. Use the [bucket] when you're in the water of this cove, and gather some water.'
You say, 'What bucket?'
Captain Mewes says 'Heh. You already have one. If you lost it in your bags or bank, maybe you need to be better organized.'
You say, 'What about the guards?'
Captain Mewes says 'There are Doomscale and Combine guards all over the island, though you are more likely to find the former. If you come across any Combine guards, killing them will be just as good. The Combine name their guards praetors.'

---

!

---

=== **Step 1** ===
Collect 10 sea pebbles 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_These are found as ground spawns ("Sea Pebbles") at the edges of the cove where the boat is trapped._

---

=== **Step 2** ===
Fill the bucket with water 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Use the "Saltwater Collection Bucket" you received to gather "Collected Saltwater" (you'll be underwater for these updates)._

---

=== **Step 3** ===
Make your way to the housing platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Explore update at /loc 1035, -885, 40._

---

=== **Step 4** ===
Kill 15 guards 0/15 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any 15 "guard" or "praetor" mobs._

---

=== **Step 5** ===
Put 2 pebbles under beds 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Here, you'll have to get close to beds with mobs on them. Stand very close to the bed, and right-click the pebbles twice on each bed, for a total of five beds. Sleeping mobs have a low aggro range, but you'll still want to be careful. Note: The right-click breaks invisibility effects._

---

=== **Step 6** ===
Pour salt water into ale kegs 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_These look exactly like kegs (they are NOT the standard barrels in the area). There are three of them in the southwest room of the first floor, and another three in the southwest room of the second floor. Note: Some mobs here can be Shroud of Stealthed AND see invisibility._

---

=== **Step 7** ===
Kill 15 guards 0/15 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill 15 more mobs with "guard" or "praetor" in their names._
Or
Deliver 1 saltwater collection bucket to Captain Mewes 0/1 (Combine Dredge)

---

=== **Step 8** ===
Report Success to Captain Mewes 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
Or
Speak with Captain Mewes about your sabotage 0/1 (Combine Dredge)

---

_Rewards:_
Experience
225 platinum

---

_This solo task begins with Captain Mewes ( **click for map image**), located on the deck of the boat docked in the Combine Dredge._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "slow" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Captain Mewes'
Captain Mewes says 'Shhhhhhh. Don't let [them] see you talking to me.'
You say, 'Who is 'them'?'
Captain Mewes says 'Those horrible Doomscale. They do such dreadful things to me, but I can [explain] later. I have some [tasks] for you if you want to help me stick a [wrench] in their work, but you can't let them know I sent you. If you are in a hurry and don't want to hear me prattle on, I need you to [disrupt] their rest, [slow] their mining, and [hamper] their research.'
You say, 'A wrench?'
Captain Mewes says 'I have some subtle sabotage [tasks] for you. ...Okay, they're not that subtle at all. I just hope she won't make me do those [horrible] things if her attention is drawn elsewhere.'
You say, 'What tasks?'
Captain Mewes says 'I would like to disrupt their [rest], slow their [mining], and hamper their [research]. Will you help me?'
You say, 'What about the mining?'
Captain Mewes says 'The Combine used this area as a mining platform ages ago. They recently started using it again shortly before Lanys and her toadies came and forcefully took it over. It was [awful]. While Lanys allows them most of the product of the mining, she takes some and has them working on some [other] task. But enough prattling on. I need you to [slow] their mining with some judicious sabotage.'
You say, 'What was awful?'
Captain Mewes says 'The Combine attacked everyone on the boat, but they were not prepared for Lanys and the Doomscale. It was a slaughter. Ten members of the Combine were slain for every Doomscale that fell. And it was a sight to behold when Lanys defeated half of the Combine single-handedly.'
You say, 'I'll slow them down...'
Captain Mewes says 'Thank you. I have written what you need to do in your journal, but if you like I can tell you more about the [sabotage].'
You have been assigned the task 'Undermining the Mining'.
Captain Mewes is not happy about his treatment. He wants you to complete tasks to disrupt the Doomscale and Combine efforts. For this task he wants you to hamper their ability to process ore.
You say, 'Tell me about the sabotage'
Captain Mewes says 'Go to the [rock quarry] and destroy some of the [elementari] and [operari] there. Afterwards, move on to the [ore processing platform] and make your way inside. At the top, you will find some of the operari processing ore. Destroy some of them, and damage a few of the ore processing kilns by putting a [heat resistant] explosive from the bundle inside. If you lose the [bundle], come back to me and tell me. I will give you another. Please don't waste them as I have a difficult time getting them. When you are done, kill a few [Doomscale] on your way back to me.'
You say, 'Where is the rock quarry?'
Captain Mewes says 'The rock quarry is to the northeast. Take the north path between the two exits from the dock. Pass under the housing platform and take the northeastern path past it. Stay to the left, and when you reach a dead end, activate the geomantic elevator to raise some of the seabed to cross over to the quarry.'
You say, 'What are the elementari?'
Captain Mewes says 'The elementari are three-legged abominations of rock, created by the geomancers. You'll find most of them at the quarry.'
You say, 'What are the operari?'
Captain Mewes says 'The operari are golem like creatures created by the Combine as laborers. They do not create them here, but replacements are shipped in from time to time.'
You say, 'Where is the ore processing platform?'
Captain Mewes says 'The ore processing platform is west of the quarry. Take the west path out of the quarry and it will lead you right to it.'
You say, 'What is this heat resistant thing?'
Captain Mewes says 'The explosive is in a heat-resistant wrapper. Note that I said heat-resistant, not heatproof. After about five seconds the heat-resistant wrapper will burn away in the kiln, and the contents will explode. Though this explosion is small, you should step away from the kiln to avoid being injured. Also, the operari are very resilient and will not be damaged by this explosion, though you're likely to have already fought them in order to gain access to the kiln.'
You say, 'What bundle are you talking about?'
Captain Mewes says 'Look. I appreciate your help, but you already have a bundle of explosives. I'm not responsible if you left it in a bank, somewhere else, or you misplaced it in your copious bags. I can't afford to give you another if you already have one.'
You say, 'What about the Doomscale?'
Captain Mewes says 'I don't care which Doomscale you kill, as long as you make Lanys' life difficult by doing so.'

---

!

---

Make your way to the rock quarry 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update at about /loc 1540, -1150, -30 (the quarry is a large area in the northeastern part of the zone)._
Kill elementari and operari 0/6 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill six mobs with "elementari" or "operari" in their names._

---

Make your way to the ore processing platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update at about /loc 1380, -470, 40 (this is the northernmost of the spiral towers)._
Kill operari 0/2 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill two mobs with "operari" in their names._
Sabotage the ore processing kilns 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Use the "Fire Resistant Explosive Bundle" (received at the beginning of the task) near kilns (kilns are located in each room of the top floor of this tower). This causes an explosion, so move away from it after you set it._
Kill 20 Doomscales 0/20 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any 20 "Doomscale" mobs._

---

Report success to Captain Mewes 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Rewards:_
Experience
150 platinum

---

_This solo task begins with Captain Mewes ( **click for map image**), located on the deck of the boat docked in the Combine Dredge._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "hamper" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, Captain Mewes'
Captain Mewes says 'Shhhhhhh. Don't let [them] see you talking to me.'
You say, 'Who is 'them'?'
Captain Mewes says 'Those horrible Doomscale. They do such dreadful things to me, but I can [explain] later. I have some [tasks] for you if you want to help me stick a [wrench] in their work, but you can't let them know I sent you. If you are in a hurry and don't want to hear me prattle on, I need you to [disrupt] their rest, [slow] their mining, and [hamper] their research.'
You say, 'I'll hamper their research...'
Captain Mewes says 'Yes. I don't know what they are doing, but they are such terrible people that we need to do what we can to hinder their work. I have written the initial plans in your journal. We will see if we need to do more once you bring me the research. Do you need more [information]?'
You have been assigned the task 'Research Wrecks'.
Captain Mewes is not happy about his treatment. He wants you to complete tasks to disrupt the Doomscale and Combine efforts. For this task he wants you to hamper their research progress.
You say, 'Give me more information, now'
Captain Mewes says 'So you'll travel to the [main platform]. Once inside, collect research [papers], set fire to the [bookshelves] with the [magical torch], and kill Combine [casters].'
You say, 'Where is the main platform?'
Captain Mewes says 'Go west off the docks. The platform is right there.'
You say, 'What papers?'
Captain Mewes says 'They should be easy to find. They are constantly working on them.'
You say, 'What bookshelves?'
Captain Mewes says 'Yeah. You know. Bookshelves. Shelves... that have books on them. Sorry. That was uncalled for. Just... my fear sometimes brings out my snarky side. Only destroy shelves on the bottom floor.'
You say, 'What agical torch?'
Captain Mewes says 'Just like all of the supplies, I do not have an unlimited amount. But if you need more, just ask me.'
You say, 'What casters?'
Captain Mewes says 'Their names are highly variable, but they should be in blue robes.'

---

!

---

Enter the main platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update around 680, -665, 40 (this is the southernmost spiral tower, due north of the dock)._
Collect research papers 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_"Geomantic Research Notes" are ground spawns on tables in the area. There are many of them, appearing as small scrolls on tables._
Set fire to bookshelves on the bottom floor 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Get close to bookshelves and right-click the "Magical Torch". Repeat for five total._
Kill any Combine casters that you find 0/4 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any four mobs with "indagator" or "indagatrix" in their name._
Deliver research papers to Captain Mewes 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
Give Captain Mewes a moment to skim the papers 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Stand next to him until this updates._

---

Make your way to the far east mining machine 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update around 1280, -3010, -30 (this is the far eastern tip of the island)._
Set a bomb at the base of the platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Get near a geomantic device and use the "Self Hiding Bomb" (received from Captain Mewes)._

---

Make your way to the far north mining machine 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update around 2400, -1490, -30 (the far northeastern tip of the island)._
Set a bomb at the base of the platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Same as above._

---

Make your way to the far west mining machine 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Location update around 2250, 1740, -30 (the far northwestern tip of the island)._
Set a bomb at the base of the platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Same as above._

---

Kill Doomscale and Combine 0/25 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any 25 mobs with "Doomscale", "praetor", "perfidus", "vicarum", "indagator", or "indagatrix" in their names._

---

Return to Captain Mewes with news of your success 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Rewards:_
Experience
187 platinum, 5 gold

_This is a kill task that begins with a scurvy mariner ( **click for map image**) on the deck of the ship docked in the Combine Dredge._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "magic" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, a scurvy mariner'
a scurvy mariner glances over with a languid grimace, 'Arr... The worst part 'o this, island's covered in crabs. One took a bit out me leg 'n it still won't heal! Help a poor, stranded soul and put an end to their evil crab [magic].'
You say, 'Magic?'
a scurvy mariner waves his hand dismissively, 'Dead men tell no tales.' He grabs his leg with both hands and looks you in the eye, 'No crabs, no magic. It's that simple.'
You have been assigned the task 'Scurvy Vengeance'.
a scurvy mariner thinks that exterminating the crabs will help heal his leg.

---

Kill 15 crabs 0/15 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill 15 "crab" mobs (far western part of the zone). Task completes upon the last kill._
_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

**About Heroic Adventures:**
\- They use the task system (steps are shown in task window, but steps are only revealed as you get to them).
\- Scale in difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels (based on your group's highest level player - as low as 85).
\- Tuned for 2-3 players and their mercenaries.
\- Nameds can spawn as you progress through the task (you usually have to complete a few steps before this can happen).
\- Collection items can drop from mobs in the instances, and ground spawns appear midway through tasks.
\- Expected time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- There is some variations/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
**About Our Documentation of Heroic Adventures**
\- We only display task steps we know about.
\-\-\- If you encounter a step that isn't documented here, please post the details (or email us) and **be sure to indicate which step number it was** so that we can display it in its proper place.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with Aqusos Ulto ( **click for map image**) in Combine Dredge (underwater off the western coast)._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- None
_Say "task" to request the mission and "ready" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Aqusos Ulto'
Aqusos Ulto says 'Air breather, I'll get right to it and not waste your time. I have a [task] for you. If you're as slow as most air breathers, then you'll simply accept my task and move on. You'll ask for more [information], though, if you have even a glimmer of intelligence.'
You say, 'What information?'
Aqusos Ulto says 'So that thin stuff you breathe does allow your brain to grow, at least somewhat. The regrua aren't native to Norrath. They are special servants of the gods of water and its avatar Coirnav. I have been watching the fighting, and a new one comes from the water every time one falls in combat. They will eventually overwhelm the Doomscale, though they are also very determined and resourceful themselves. I have a suspicion, but I would like you to confirm it. Explore the waters, probably not too far from here, and see if you can find the [origin].'
You say, 'What is their origin?'
Aqusos Ulto says 'As I said, I have a suspicion. But I need confirmation and for that I need your unbiased observation. If I provide any more information, it may influence the outcome. While I did say that you were intelligent to ask me for information, in this case I cannot comply. However, I suspect that I'll have more to say after you investigate. We can speak more then.'
You say, 'What task?'
Aqusos Ulto says 'Go and find the origin of the regrua in this area. When you're [ready] I'll send you to sleep until night falls so you're properly rested.'
You have been assigned the task 'Kedge Counterblow'.
Aqusos Ulto is concerned with the activity around the Combine Dredge. It sends you to confirm a suspicion it has.
You say, 'Ready!'
Aqusos Ulto says 'Sleep. When you awaken you'll be ready for investigation.'

---

!

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"High Guard Vnayyanye" has a chance to spawn after the third step is completed. She has three possible spawn points, all on the small hill adjacent to the docks from Brother Island (there are three tents here). Her PHs are "a restive guard" in this area._
_Known loot from this named:_
Level 105
Dread Vnayyanye's Censor
Vnayyanye's Transfixion
Level 100
???
Level 95
???

---

Search for the origin of the regrua 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Task Window Says:_ Search for the origin of the attacking regrua. The origin is not likely very far from the giant squid, so it would be wise to restrict your search to the west side of the zone.
_This is a location update around 1000, 2200, -65._
Defend yourself from the elementals 0/5 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill five elementals (here, you'll get one spawn every 60 seconds). These mobs are mezzable._
Return to Aqusos Ulto and speak of the elementals 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
**At this point in the task, collectibles spawn and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**

---

Venture to the main platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_Location update around 700, -200, 50. See-invis mobs are about, so be careful._
Kill 10 Doomscale 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any 10 "Doomscale" mobs._
Set a water portal spell on the top floor and run 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_When planting "bombs", get away from them quickly (or evacuate), or you'll be killed in the blast._

---

Venture to the ore processing platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_Location update around 1400, -450, 40. No see invis mobs are reported in this area._
Kill 10 Doomscale 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill 10 more "Doomscale" mobs._
Set a water portal spell on the top floor and run 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Again, move away from where you set it._

---

Venture to the quarry 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_Location update around 1600, -1100, -30. There are many see-invis mobs in this area, so take care with travel._
Kill 10 beings at the mine 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_Anything counts for your 10 in the quarry area, except the doomscales. So clear the area around the platform when doing your 10._
Place the water portal spell underneath the mining device 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_As with previous "place" steps, once you place the spell, get away from it as it generates a damage aura that does around 10,000 dmg per second._

---

Venture to the housing platform 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_Location update around 1100, -800, 40. Be watchful of the wrathforged mobs here as they can see invis (also a larger than normal aggro range)._
Kill 10 Doomscale of any type or Combine guards 0/10 (Combine Dredge)
_Kill any 10 "Doomscale" or "praetor" mobs._
Set a water portal spell on the top floor and run 0/1 (Combine Dredge)
_Move away once set!_

---

Return to Aqusos Ulto and report your success 0/1 (Combine Dredge)

---

**TASK REWARDS**
\- 387 "Pieces of Eight" (group currency)
- Dread Vnayyanye's Censor [item=122125]
- Piece of Eight [item=120483]
- Vnayyanye's Transfixion [item=121493]

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is part of a series and begins with a noctis viratum ( **click for map image**) at the zone-in of Arx Mentis._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
_Say "citadel" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum makes a beckoning motion, quietly, urging you to come closer without attracting any attention. His speaks in a hurried whisper, 'Don't let the praetor see you! They've been attacking foreigners on sight. The citizens are a bit more lenient, at least for now. If you intend to get anywhere here, you're going to need to access the [citadel].'
You say, 'The citadel?'
a noctis viratum continues his hurried whisper, 'That pyramid across the way is the junction between the city and the citadel. Every movement within the citadel is tracked, so I suggest you keep your guard up if you manage to survive the trip up.' The shadowy figure begins to detail the logistics of his plan...
You have been assigned the task 'Key to the Citadel'.
The shady figure hiding in the streets of the city is skeptical of your intentions but seems willing to work with you. Your first order of business will be attuning yourself to the Citadel portal.

---

!

---

'Obtain' a key from a praetor 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ 'Obtain' an attuned portal key from one of the praetor. It is unlikely they will part with their key willingly...
_Kill any NPC with "praetor" in its name to receive a "Praetor's Attuned Portal Key" on your cursor. Note that one of these mobs in the zone-in area is a named PH, so watch what you pull!_

---

Make your way to the Penutarx Junction 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ The citadel is divided into four accessible floors. When entering the citadel from the city you will be on the Protarx. Below the Protarx is the Teritarx that is off limits to any lower-ranking praetor. Going up the citadel sequentially from the Protarx are the Anetarx and Penutarx. Travel to the east end of the Penutarx to find the power junction that feeds energy to the inaccessible Apitarx.
_This updates around /loc -35, -140, 175._

---

Kill 3 Penutarx Junction workers 0/3 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Eliminate some of the workers in the power junction.
_Kill any three mobs in this room (any mobs count: humanoids and otherwise)._

---

Grab the Key Attuner from the Arx Junction 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_"Portal Key Attuner" is a ground spawn at /loc 20, -190, 176 (This is a steaming clockwork-looking object sitting on the northeastern table in this room)._

---

Give the Attuned Portal Key to the noctis viratum 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
Give the Key Attuner to the noctis viratum 0/1 (Arx Mentis)

---

Speak with the noctis viratum 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with the shady figure to have him re-attune the portal key to your body.
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum fiddles with the key attuning device for a moment before pointing it directly at you. You feel your chest heat up as the device emits a brief beam of blue light. The figure nods, 'You've done well. Perhaps you are ready for more.'
You are now attuned to the city's portal to the citadel. Though it seems your work with the shady figure has only just begun...
_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum
_You can now use the portal on the pyramid to enter the floating tower without taking damage._
_Despite getting a lockout timer, this task is not repeatable._
