**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

_This solo task is part of a series and begins with a noctis viratum ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Completed "Key to the Citadel"
_Say "taken" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum says 'So you're ready for more, \_\_\_\_\_? Good, good... I suppose your eagerness in assaulting the citadel deserves some candor. In addition to being a 'shady figure' I was once a praetor serving as hand of the citadel to enact his highness's will. I proved to be rather incapable of this job as it required a number of, let's say, inhumane qualities. I escaped the ranks when it became necessary to send a number of us down to the dredge, but now I fear for a number of my companions that have been [taken] from the city to the citadel in the interim.'
You say, 'Taken?'
a noctis viratum says 'Taken is a more accurate word for what the citizens call 'ascending.' The populous thinks that being chosen to serve in the citadel is a grand honor, but I fear more sinister measures are taking place...'
You have been assigned the task 'What Lies Above'.
The shady figure is more amenable now that you have proved your willingness to work with him. He has revealed himself to be an escaped citadel praetor by the name of Tsalis Ferin and would like you to make contact with two citizens who have recently 'ascended' but have not been heard from for some time.

---

!

---

Locate Serphus Arlis 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Serphus Arlis was a philosopher before he was chosen to join the ranks of the praetor guard. Tsalis found it surprising that Serphus was chosen given his propensity to muse on the ethics of militarization. Tsalis believes that Serphus does not share his fellow citizens' enthusiasm for ascension and may have more to reveal about the strange occurrences happening within the Arx.
_Serphus Arlis is found roaming the port-in area of the Arx (roams through the port-in room from hall to hall)._
Speak with Serphus 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Serphus appears to be patrolling the Protarx portal. Let him know that Tsalis sent you.
You say, 'Hail, Serphus Arlis'
Serphus Arlis stands upright and projects his voice past you, 'H-Halt intruder!' He quickly lowers his voice to a whisper, 'Just go, I don't want any trouble.' His expression changes from fearful to anxious as you explain that Tsalis sent you. He nods, his voice still a whisper, 'The last I saw of Crasis was on the Penutarx. Go now. We can't be seen conversing. Though I may have work for you later if you are truly a friend of Tsalis.'

---

Locate Crasis Whesar 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Crasis Whesar was a prominent artist in the city before his ascension to the citadel. His ascension is particularly surprising to Tsalis given the primary subject of his work was of the citadel's weaponry. Tsalis is unsure where Crasis may be located.
_Crasis Whesar is found in the Penutarx Reanimation Ward at /loc 60, -5, 175 (on a table in the northwestern room of the top floor)._
As you survey the ward, you take notice of the body lying on the table. You believe you have found Crasis...

---

Kill anything that gets in your way 0/5 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Although Tsalis encourages you to utilize stealth when traveling through the citadel, it may be necessary to dispatch anything that takes notice of you as you explore the halls.
_Any five mobs on any floor of the Arx should update this step._

---

Speak with Tsalis 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_If you've been paying attention, you know this is "a noctis viratum"._
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum listens intently as you recount the details of your encounters. 'Crasis will be missed. He was a brave man amidst many cowards who will not speak out. I am glad to hear Serphus is well, he will prove valuable in weakening the praetor's hold on the populous. When you are ready, there are two more people I would like you to make contact with.'
Tsalis is certain that Serphus will prove to be a useful ally in uncovering and unraveling the praetor's plot. It seems there are at least two more people that Tsalis would like you to track down in the citadel whenever you are ready.
_Rewards:_
Experience
75 platinum
_Despite getting a lockout timer, this task is not repeatable._

---

_This solo task is part of a series and begins with a noctis viratum ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 95
\- Completed "Key to the Citadel"
\- Completed "What Lies Above"
_Say "friends" to request the task._
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum says 'It's strange, \_\_\_\_\_. It wasn't many years ago that the citadel, the city, the praetor and the populous seemed to summate a veritable utopia. The subtle iron-fisted rule, the abductions, and the tedious lives of the citizens are such fresh distractions that I am not sure our city will make it out of this unscathed. There are two more citizens I would like you to make contact with, a husband and wife who were good [friends] of mine before I 'ascended.''
You say, 'You? Friends?'
a noctis viratum says 'Given what we know so far, I fear the worst for my old friends as they were both unabashedly brazen in their critique of the citadel's recent ambitions.'
You have been assigned the task 'Delving Deeper'.
Tsalis is convinced that the dramatic increase in 'ascensions' is a cover for the praetor to silence dissenting citizens without the populous becoming suspicious. There are two more people he'd like you to make contact with to confirm or dismiss his theory.

---

!

---

Locate Oreda Safli 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Oreda Safli and her husband Rakus were sculptors before their ascension. Tsalis does not recall anything particularly objectionable about their work that would lead the praetor to want to silence them. Make contact with the couple and see if they report anything suspicious occurring.
_Oreda Safli is located at -60, 145, 88. This is on the Antearx floor (second floor). She's on the lower of the two very narrow halls in the western part of the Anterax._

---

Speak with Oreda 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Speak with Oreda to find Rakus' whereabouts.
You say, 'Hail, Oreda Safli'
Oreda Safli ignores you and remains transfixed on the scene playing out in front of her. You mention that Tsalis sent you and you are looking for her husband. She responds curtly without turning her attention away from the window, 'Try the Teritarx.'

---

Locate Rakus Safli 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Search the Teriarx for any sign of Rakus.
_Rakus Safli - or what's left of him - is found at /loc 50, 40, -67 (the basement area)._

---

Recover evidence of Rakus' demise 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Recover anything you can from Rakus' remains to bring to Oreda.
_"Mangled Wedding Band" is a ground spawn... inside... Rakus' gooey remains._

---

Return Rakus' ring to Oreda 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Deliver Rakus' ring to Oreda to inform her of her husband's fate.
Oreda Safli holds the ring up to her face. Her sullen expression remains unchanged. She places the mangled ring on her left hand to create an awkward symmetry with her right then lets out a brief sigh before speaking, 'Be off, but return later...'

---

Kill anything that gets in your way 0/10 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Tsalis is growing less concerned with your stealth given your display of power with your previous excursions. Eliminate anyone or anything that gets in your way within the citadel.
_Kill any 10 mobs in the citadel._

---

Speak with Tsalis 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Tsalis to confirm his suspicions.
_Hail "a noctis viratum" to complete._
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum sighs contemplatively as you explain your encounters. 'This is deeply troubling. To think that Rakus is gone and Oreda is numb to her husband's slaughter? The Arx's grip around our necks grows tighter. I may not have much more time here.' He pauses, 'If you wish to continue to weaken the praetor's hold then you should return to Serphus and Oreda. There is little more I have to offer you but gratitude.'
Tsalis is thankful for your help in making contact with the ascended citizens. He believes the best course of action is to leave any remaining work in your hands with the help of your new contacts.
_Rewards:_
75 platinum
Experience
_Despite getting a lockout timer, this task is not repeatable._

**The Darkened Sea Info & Guides: Overview \| Progression & Task List \| Raiding \| Visible Armor**

---

**About Heroic Adventures:**
\- They use the task system (steps are shown in task window, but steps are only revealed as you get to them).
\- Scale in difficulty, loot quality, and mob levels (based on your group's highest level player - as low as 85).
\- Tuned for 2-3 players and their mercenaries.
\- Nameds can spawn as you progress through the task (you usually have to complete a few steps before this can happen).
\- Collection items can drop from mobs in the instances, and ground spawns appear midway through tasks.
\- Expected time of completion will vary by your group's makeup (anywhere from 30-90+ minutes).
\- There is some variations/randomization in the task steps you'll be assigned as you progress through the task.
**About Our Documentation of Heroic Adventures**
\- We only display task steps we know about.
\-\-\- If you encounter a step that isn't documented here, please post the details (or email us) and **be sure to indicate which step number it was** so that we can display it in its proper place.

---

_This is a heroic adventure (1-6 players) that begins with a noctis viratum ( **click for map image**) in Arx Mentis._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Minimum Level 85
\- Completed "Key to the Citadel"
\- Completed "What Lies Above"
\- Completed "Delving Deeper"

---

**TASK SUMMARY**
\- Say "stop" to request the task and "ready" to zone in.
**Step #1:**
Eradicate a few ambulatory carcasses 0/5
\-\-\- Kill five of these mobs.
Break their virga vitalas 0/4
\-\-\- Kill four of these mobs.
Tear up their libre vitalas 0/4
\-\-\- Kill update (4 mobs).
**Step #2:**
Silence the citadel's arcanists 0/20
\-\-\- Kill update (20 mobs).
Silence the citadel's priests 0/15
\-\-\- Kill update (15 mobs).
**Step #3:**
Disrupt the citadel's experiments on the scavenger magicae 0/10
\-\-\- Kill update (10 mobs)
Beat some sense into the violent citizens 0/7
\-\-\- Kill update (seven mobs)
Subdue a few praetors 0/7
\-\-\- Kill update (seven mobs)
**\- At this point in the task, collectibles spawn in the zone (ground spawns) and a named (or its PH) has a chance to spawn.**
**Step #4:**
Speak to the noctis viratum 0/1
\-\-\- Hail update.

---

!

---

**NAMED MOBS**
_"Praetor Loricas the Hollow" (or its PH, "a fading steel worker") spawns in the Anetarx Armory at around /loc 190, -40, 83. Spawn rate for the named is about 30%._
_Known loot from this named:_
Level 105
Praetor's Claws
Ring of Hollows
Level 100
???
Level 95
???

---

**TASK IN DETAIL**
========================================================================
TRIGGERING THE TASK
========================================================================
You say, 'Hail, a noctis viratum'
a noctis viratum says 'It's best that I return to the shadows. It is up to you, \_\_\_\_\_, to [stop] what the citadel has set in motion.'
You say, 'I'll stop it'
a noctis viratum says 'Very well, \_\_\_\_\_. Give the word when you're [ready] to begin.'
You have been assigned the task 'Shake the Citadel'.
Tsalis Ferin feels that it is in his best interest to lay low, but that does not stop him from offering you a chance to strike the citadel on his behalf.
========================================================================
STEP #1
========================================================================
Eradicate a few ambulatory carcasses 0/5 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have five updates._
=====================
Break their virga vitalas 0/4 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ ?
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have four updates._
=====================
Tear up their libre vitalas 0/4 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ The citadel continues to test their arcane powers of animation on stranger and stranger objects. They have most recently given life to the pages of heavy tomes. Hinder their research by eliminating these living books. You'll likely find the libre vitalas in the Antearx Extraction Lab as well as the Antearx Armory, Penuarx Stasis Lab, or the Protarx Relic Keep.
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have 22 updates._
========================================================================
STEP #2
========================================================================
Silence the citadel's arcanists 0/20 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ While traversing the halls of the citadel, do away with any indagators or indagatrixes you come across.
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have 20 updates._
=====================
Silence the citadel's priests 0/15 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ While traversing the halls of the citadel do away with any vicarums you come across.
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have 15 updates._
You can find the priests in the library. Kill any of the A vicarum vitai.
========================================================================
STEP #3
========================================================================
Disrupt the citadel's experiments on the scavenger magicae 0/10 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ You should be able to deal a critical blow to the citadel's research efforts by exterminating the scavenger magicaes that are held in the Antearx Magicae Sanctuary.
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have 10 updates._
=====================
Beat some sense into the violent citizens 0/7 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ The normally docile citizens that inhabit the town are becoming increasingly violent toward outsiders. Though it may seem counter-intuitive, beating some sense into the most aggressive citizens should send a message to the remaining population that confronting adventurers is not in their best interests.
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have seven updates. These are the "an artist", "an ethicist", "an author", "a chef" types down by the zone-in area._
=====================
Subdue a few praetors 0/7 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says: The normally docile citizens that inhabit the town are becoming increasingly violent towards outsiders._
_Though it may seem counter-intuitive, beating some sense into the most aggressive should send a message to remaining_
_population that confronting adventurers is not in their best interests._
_If You Get This Step: Mob kills until you have seven updates._
========================================================================
STEP #4
========================================================================
Speak to the noctis viratum 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Task Window Says:_ Return to Tsalis Ferin and inform him of your success.
_If You Get This Step: Hail update for task completion._

---

**TASK REWARDS**
\- 191 "Pieces of Eight"
- Piece of Eight [item=120483]
- Praetor's Claws [item=120521]
- Ring of Hollows [item=122127]

---

_This group mission was introduced on December 17, 2014. It begins with Firiona Vie ( **click for map image**) in Katta Castrum: Deluge. She's found in the southwestern part of the temple's main floor._
_Pre-Requisites for Requesting:_
\- Completion of "A Rising Storm" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Gathering Clouds" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "Clearer Skies" (Tempest Temple)
\- Completion of "The Edge of Balance" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "The Abscent Scholar" (Katta Castrum: Deluge)
\- Completion of "Just Recompense" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "The Cover of Night" (Brother Island)
\- Completion of "Loyal is the Heart" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Leave No Brother Behind" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "Two Sides of the Shield" (Caverns of Endless Song)
\- Completion of "The Sacred Profaned" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Crying for the Dead" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "Burden of the Past" (Degmar, the Lost Castle)
\- Completion of "An Endless Cycle" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "To Remake Eden" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "Fit for the New World" (Thuliasaur Island)
\- Completion of "A Fickle Wind Blows" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "What Hate Begets" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Deep Lies the Snow" (Combine Dredge)
\- Completion of "Free Us From Ourselves" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of "A Beginning From an End" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of "All Rivers Lead to the Sea" (Arx Mentis)
\- Completion of "The Cost of Balance" (Arx Mentis)
_Say "confront Calix" to request the task and "free" to zone in._
You say, 'Hail, Firiona Vie'
Firiona Vie looks up as you approach, taken out of deep thought. '\_\_\_\_\_, I still do not know where Al`Kabor is. Before the [beam] struck the dome, he had been reading the [books] in the city and wandering the streets, visiting portals. Since our journey began, he has seemed [troubled], and his agitation grew when we descended into the sea. I'm sure he was primarily concerned with the potential magical power of the leaders of this place when we eventually must [face them] if your [group] is ready.'
You say, 'My group is ready'
Firiona Vie says 'There is much left undone as we follow Lanys' path from the ship to the Citadel. It is finally time: We must [confront Calix] Quirinus and recover Tsaph. It seems unlikely that he would have harmed their [leader], Tsaph Katta. His people, calling themselves Indagatoris Opertum, worship him. We must be prepared to face not only great power but great faith.'
You say, 'What about their leader?'
Firiona Vie says 'I would never tell anyone whom or how to worship, but from what I understand of history, Tsaph Katta never really led the Combine. His efforts to bring his people together, while noble, fell apart the moment he was gone. I have no doubt about his skill as a leader and inspiration, but he has yet to be truly tested. I can only hope he meets up to expectations, but after building up his legend for so long, I don't see how they can be satisfied with the real man.'
You say, 'We will confront Calix...'
Firiona Vie says 'Wonderful! We appreciate your assistance, and we can never repay you. We will never forget the risks you take on our behalf. Some of my companions have gone ahead. We shall meet them when you are [free].'
You have been assigned the task 'Confronting Calix'.
Firiona and her allies need your assistance in confronting the leader of Arx Mentis to rescue Tsaph Katta.

---

Defeat Calix and rescue Tsaph Katta 0/1 (Arx Mentis)
_Speak with Calix Quirinus to begin the event. First, you'll have Principal Indagator Gordianus to deal with (has a few AEs and spawns damage auras that you'll need to avoid._
_Once Principal Indagator Gordianus is dead, you'll have Calix Quirinus go aggro (spawns adds). Soon after, Lanys T`Vyl goes aggro as well, and you'll have both of them to contend with. Each has their own AEs and unique abilities._
_Kill Calix Quirinus to complete the event, and stand under Ognit Eznertob to avoid Una Lucem's AE at the end._

---

**Event Summary**
Phase One
\- Speak with Calix to trigger the event.
\- Here, you'll have to contend with Principal Indagator Gordianus.
\-\-\- Lanys T`Vyl and Calix remain out of the fight, for now.
\- Principal Indagator Gordianus:
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Stormpulse" every 35 seconds (PBAE 20' range 43,000 dmg + double attack inhibition + fizzle rate increase).
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Energy Wave" every 25 seconds (rear 40' range AE for 42,500 dmg + knockback).
\-\-\- Spawns storm auras every 60 seconds that pulse "Lesser Manastorm" every 3 seconds (40,000 dmg + 2,500 mana drain).
\-\-\-\-\-\- His damage auras sit idle for a period of time before eventually exploding. As the fight goes on, more auras appear at a time.
\- This phase ends when Principal Indagator Gordianus dies.
Phase Two
\- When Principal Indagator Gordianus dies, Calix Quirinus and 2x "a Doomscale destroyer" go live.
\-\-\- The friendly NPCs will kill the two adds.
\-\-\- Calix Quirinus takes control of Galeth's arc worker suit and sends it to attack the other friendly NPCs, at which time Lanys T`Vyl goes live.
\- You now have Calix Quirinus and Lanys T`Vyl to contend with.
\- Lanys T`Vyl:
\-\-\- Her health can't drop below Calix's health (will auto-raise +1%).
\-\-\- The more health she loses, the weaker she becomes.
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Surge of Pain" (500' range PBAE 10-tick levitation suppression + 1,000 mana drain + 37,500 dmg).
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Repulsion" at same time as "Surge of Pain" (100' range PBAE knockback + one-slow dispell).
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Mana Conversion" buff on herself (causes spells to heal her for 50,000hp).
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Lifescourge" (emote-based frontal beam spell; reduces max hp by 20% for 60 seconds; has 25 corruption counters).
\-\-\- Swaps between tank targets every 75-100 seconds (switches from top hated to second hated).
\-\-\- Alternates between being vulnerable to melee (title "Exhausted" appears under her name) and vulnerable to spells (title "Rested" appears under her name) - she will emote about this as well.
\-\-\- Spawns undead adds four at a time (adds have very low hitpoints, but hit for a max ~10,000).
\-\-\- Weakening as she loses health:
\-\-\-\-\-\- When below 60% health, adds stop spawning.
\-\-\-\-\-\- When below 40% health, stops casting "Lesser Lifescourage" and doing tank swaps.
\- Calix Quirinus:
\-\-\- The more health he loses, the stronger he becomes.
\-\-\- Casts "Lesser Energy Conversion", 150' range PBAE, every 45 seconds which does range-based damage.
\-\-\-\-\-\- Does 22,500 dmg at close range to Calix, up to 90,000 dmg at further range away from Calix.
\-\-\- Once below 50%, gains two spells (emotes are seen by inflicted targets):
\-\-\-\-\-\- "Lesser Energy Charge" (every 20 seconds on four targets: 50% spell interrupt; DOT for 30,000/tick (60 seconds); cured by moving 120' range in any direction).
\-\-\-\-\-\- "Lesser Energy Barrier" (same time as Energy Charge; four targets; inflicted player should not move more than 25' range with this debuff on, or it drops. After 10 seconds, if this buff isn't still on the player, they are hit with "Lesser Energy Discharge" for 62,500 dmg.)
\-\-\- Once below 30%, gains another attack:
\-\-\-\-\-\- "Lesser Energy Flow" (alternates with "Lesser Energy Conversion" casts; range-based damage the opposite of "Energy Conversion" - the closer you are, the more damage you take).
\-\-\- Lanys T`Vyl must be 10% health or lower before you can kill Calix.
\- To complete the event:
\-\-\- Calix Quirinus remains locked at 2% health until Lanys T`Vyl is at or under 10%. At this point, Calix can be killed. This completes the mission.
\- One last problem:
\-\-\- Una Quirinus, having remained out of the fight all this time, does an AE for 200,000 dmg after the completion. Get into Ognit Eznertob's aura to protect yourself from this.

---

**Event Trigger**
You say, 'Hail, Calix Quirinus'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'So, you dare to show yourself here, in my sanctum. I am shocked by your stupidity. You assault my people, nearly kill my [daughter], and now you come here. Your [desire for death] must be overwhelming. You were correct, however, [Lanys]. They are persistent and dangerous. But their reign of terror ends now.'
You say, 'Your daughter?'
Calix Quirinus says 'Una is the perfect child, first of her kind. She is the future of Norrath. The perfect being, blended from all the greatest parts of all the races of Norrath. Myself and others that are less perfect will give way to them, and those that they create will replace them in time. We grow much stronger with each generation. You, unfortunately, are a remnant of the past, no longer wanted, no longer needed.'
You say, 'Lanys?'
Calix Quirinus says 'She has her uses, of course. She has helped us anticipate your actions, though her direct contributions could be stronger. She is quite mad, clearly, but insanity can be a useful tool for those that know how to channel it.'
You say, 'You are the one with a desire for death'
Calix Quirinus says 'Fine, Gordianus, deal with them, I must see to my daughter.'
Firiona Vie shouts 'Even though so much blood has already been spilled, it is clear that you will not allow this to end before even more is shed.'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'Really? What about my daughter's blood? She is precious and unique. She is the future of Norrath, and you would destroy her.'
Una Quirinus shouts 'I can still fight them, father! Don't give up on me yet.'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'You can barely stand, child. Just wait here while Gordianus deals with these intruders. Save your strength. Once they are dead I will use the chamber to heal your wounds.'
Firiona Vie shouts 'I'm sorry, but I can't allow that. Tsaph needs that chamber, and I have no plans to allow the Lifeguide to be used to forward your insane plans. You should never have allied yourself with Lanys. She can't be trusted. Death and pain are the only things she brings to your alliance. Death, torture, pain... All the things she brought to Dagda she will bring to you.'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'Hah! It's no surprise that you blame me. That's all you've ever done. But it wasn't me that hid behind her friends. It wasn't my fault that I had to go through that pathetic barbarian to get to you. If you hadn't brought her here to protect you, she wouldn't be dead now.'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'Vengeance? Pain? Perfect. This is what you deserve for what you have done to my people, to my daughter.'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'Good. You get your revenge. I will get what I came for!'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'No! You must not disturb Tsaph Katta! He will die if you open that chamber or take the healing gem. Put off your petty desires for a little longer! Stop her, Gordianus!'
Principal Indagator Gordianus shouts 'I can't harm her and you know it. You should never have given her that seal. My magic won't touch her.'
Principal Indagator Gordianus shouts 'Now the storm begins. I am Gordianus, first Principal of Arx Mentis. They call me Magestorm. I shall now teach you why.'
Al`Kabor says 'My companions, I'm afraid I can't protect you all from these storms, merely four of us.'

---

**Principal Indagator Gordianus**
_First, you have Principal Indagator Gordianus and his auras to deal with. You'll see this emote as he spawns one:_
Principal Indagator Gordianus shouts 'Do you feel it? The storm is coming!'
_They move around the event area. Stay out of them to avoid their damage auras._
_You only have a very short time after Principal Indagator Gordianus's death before Calix Quirinus goes live (summons from 100% health)._
Principal Indagator Gordianus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Firiona Vie shouts 'Lanys, stop! Opening that chamber might kill Tsaph!'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'What makes you think I care what happens to this relic, Tsaph? Doomscale, keep her from interfering.'

---

**Calix Quirinus & Lanys T`Vyl**
_Lanys T`Vyl stays out of the fight until Calix Quirinus is around 85-80% health:_
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'Get out of my way, you old fossil. Finally! Now I have the whole Lifeguide! That just leaves finishing you fools off and I can get on about my business.'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'You will not leave with your prize, Lanys. You cannot breach the Citadel. We can discuss your plight once you have aided me in destroying the intruders that you brought to our home.'
Lanys T`Vyl shouts 'You are demented, Calix. But I will not die here. My magic links us. I cannot die while you still live.'
Calix Quirinus shouts 'You, Galeth! Even if you inhabit one of our creations, you can never truly control it. But I CAN!' Galeth stops moving for a moment, and then moves toward his companions.'
_Lanys T`Vyl also summons from 100% health. Now, you have both of them to contend with. Keep in mind that Lanys T`Vyl's health cannot drop below Calix Quirinus's health, and that the lower in health Lanys goes, the weaker she becomes while the lower in health Calix goes, the stronger he becomes._
_Lanys has an emote-based directional "Lifescourge" debuff (reduces maximum hitpoints of anyone in the path of the beam spell); occasionally memblurs herself (save for one random target on her hatelist). Throughout the encounter, she alternates between being vulnerable to melee attacks and vulernable to spell attacks (you'll see these two emotes indicating which one she's vulnerable to at the time)._
_Emotes you'll see here:_
**Lanys is growing tired. As her ability to block your attacks lessens, she quickly casts a spell to protect herself from magic.**
\-\-\- vulnerable to melee; immune to spells
**Lanys regains her endurance and begins to block nearly every physical blow, just as her magical defenses drop.**
\-\-\- vulnerable to spells; immune to melee
**Lanys T`Vyl shifts her attention to someone else that has been irritating her and forgets the transgressions of everyone else!**
\-\-\- this is her memblur and target shift
**Lanys gains strength. She seems linked in some way to Calix.**
\-\-\- seen if you try to damage her lower than Calix's current health
**Lanys T`Vyl says 'Heads up, \_\_\_\_\_!'**
\-\-\- seen 10 seconds in advance of directional "Lesser Lifescourge" on target
**A power begins surging through you. It weakens as you move.**
\-\-\- "Lesser Energy Charge" lands on you; move 120' to remove it
**A power surrounds and protects you, but it is very fragile.**
\-\-\- "Lesser Energy Barrier" lands on you; do not move 25' while it's on you
**The barrier around you prevents a deadly energy discharge from striking you.**
\-\-\- you avoided one of Calix's positional AEs towards the end of the fight
**Lanys grows weak. As she does, so does Calix. You believe he can be harmed now.**
\-\-\- Lanys is under 10%; you can now kill Calix to finish the event
_NOTE: Lanys T`Vyl continues attacking until Una Quirinus does her AE. (The event is won at this point, so dying after Calix's death has no actual consequence other than the inconvenience of having to run back to the task.)_

---

**The Finish**
Calix Quirinus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Galeth Veredeth looks confused. 'Did I... Did I just attack you? I'm so sorry, Ognit. I hope I did no permanent harm to the Erudite. That Calix fellow took control of my body. Perhaps this form was a poor choice.'
Firiona was very grateful for your help. Tspah Katta shook your hand and was very pleased as well.
Ognit Eznertob shouts 'Quickly! Gather!'
_Get to Ognit Eznertob's location._
Una Quirinus shouts 'I die, but I take our foes with us!'
Una Quirinus begins to cast a spell.
Your immunity buff protected you from the spell Retribution of the Indagatoris Opertum!
Firiona Vie says 'Well, here we are again. You have brought war to this place, murdered and tortured my friends, and corrupted all of those you call Doomscale. You have come to represent all that is terrible in this world. And now you have finally failed.'
Lanys T`Vyl whispers, clearly pained by the simple act of speaking, 'I have always been stronger than you. Your allies and that... monstrous child were, once again, the only reason you survived. Now is your chance to prove your strength. Prove you are worthy of all the worship these people give you. End me. Finish what you started. Take my life. End your struggles. Protect your friends.'
Galeth Veredeth says 'You can't do that, Firiona. Don't follow her into that darkness.'
Firiona Vie says 'She is correct. She has destroyed too many lives. It will be a service to the world, to the Mother of All, to everyone, if she is dead. It doesn't matter what happens to me. I am willing to sacrifice anything for those that I care about.' Firiona raises her blade over Lanys.'
Firiona Vie says 'But you are right, Lanys. I am not as strong as you. I can't just murder a helpless foe, not even you. And you are correct, I couldn't defeat you without my friends. I am weak. It is my understanding of that fact, and my ability to trust in those friends, that has stopped you. I can live happily in that weakness.' Firiona reaches out and takes the Lifeguide from Lanys' burned and broken fingers.'
Tsaph Katta says 'Thank you, stranger, for your help. I can never repay you. I only hope that I can be worthy of your sacrifices.'
Al`Kabor groans, rubbing the back of his head. 'What hit me?'
Ognit Eznertob says 'Well, we managed it. I can't seem to be happy about it, though.'
Galeth Veredeth sighs. 'At least it's over. It may be years before our hearts recover. I still can't believe Dagda is dead. I'm not sure that healing Tsaph Katta was worth the price. I suppose it can't be, at least for us, her companions.'
Firiona Vie says 'Such a terrible loss. Such a hollow victory. It seems to be time to [leave].'
